    pub fn new(tenant_id: &str, password: &str) -> Result<Self, CassandraTenantError> {
        if tenant_id.is_empty()
            || tenant_id.len() > 32
            || !tenant_id
                .bytes()
                .all(|b| b.is_ascii_lowercase() || b.is_ascii_digit() || b == b'_')
        {
            return Err(CassandraTenantError::InvalidTenantId);
        }
        if password.len() < 24 || !password.bytes().all(|b| (33..=126).contains(&b)) {
            return Err(CassandraTenantError::WeakPassword);
        }
        let id = tenant_id.to_ascii_lowercase();
        Ok(Self {
            tenant_id: id.clone(),
            keyspace: format!("proxima_{id}"),
            role: format!("proxima_{id}_app"),
            password: password.to_owned(),
        })
    }
    pub fn tenant_id(&self) -> &str {
        &self.tenant_id
    }
    pub fn keyspace(&self) -> &str {
        &self.keyspace
    }
    pub fn role(&self) -> &str {
        &self.role
    }
    /// Generates a dedicated keyspace, login role, and data-only grants.
    pub fn provisioning_cql(
        &self,
        datacenter: &str,
        replication_factor: u8,
    ) -> Result<Vec<String>, CassandraTenantError> {
        if datacenter.is_empty()
            || datacenter.len() > 48
            || !datacenter
                .bytes()
                .all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'_')
        {
            return Err(CassandraTenantError::InvalidDatacenter);
        }
        if !(1..=5).contains(&replication_factor) {
            return Err(CassandraTenantError::InvalidReplicationFactor);
        }
        let password = self.password.replace('\'', "''");
        Ok(vec![
            format!("CREATE KEYSPACE IF NOT EXISTS {} WITH replication = {{'class': 'NetworkTopologyStrategy', '{}': {}}} AND durable_writes = true", self.keyspace, datacenter, replication_factor),
            format!("CREATE ROLE IF NOT EXISTS {} WITH PASSWORD = '{}' AND LOGIN = true", self.role, password),
            format!("GRANT SELECT ON KEYSPACE {} TO {}", self.keyspace, self.role),
            format!("GRANT MODIFY ON KEYSPACE {} TO {}", self.keyspace, self.role),
        ])
    }
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn provisions_separate_keyspace_and_restricted_role() {
        let t = CassandraTenant::new("customer_01", "a-long-random-cassandra-secret").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(t.keyspace(), "proxima_customer_01");
        assert_eq!(t.role(), "proxima_customer_01_app");
        let cql = t.provisioning_cql("datacenter1", 3).unwrap().join("\n");
        assert!(cql.contains("NetworkTopologyStrategy"));
        assert!(cql.contains("GRANT SELECT ON KEYSPACE proxima_customer_01"));
        assert!(cql.contains("GRANT MODIFY ON KEYSPACE proxima_customer_01"));
        assert!(!cql.contains("AUTHORIZE"));
        assert!(!cql.contains("GRANT ALL"));
    }
    #[test]
    fn escapes_password_and_rejects_identifier_injection() {
        let t = CassandraTenant::new("tenant_a", "a-long-random-password-with-'quote").unwrap();
        assert!(t.provisioning_cql("dc1", 1).unwrap()[1].contains("''"));
        assert!(matches!(
            CassandraTenant::new("a; DROP KEYSPACE x", "a-long-random-cassandra-secret"),
            Err(CassandraTenantError::InvalidTenantId)
        ));
        assert!(matches!(
            CassandraTenant::new("Customer_01", "a-long-random-cassandra-secret"),
            Err(CassandraTenantError::InvalidTenantId)
        ));
    }
    #[test]
    fn rejects_weak_secrets_and_invalid_topology() {
        assert!(matches!(
            CassandraTenant::new("tenant", "short"),
            Err(CassandraTenantError::WeakPassword)
        ));
        let t = CassandraTenant::new("tenant", "a-long-random-cassandra-secret").unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(
            t.provisioning_cql("dc1", 0),
            Err(CassandraTenantError::InvalidReplicationFactor)
        ));
        assert!(matches!(
            t.provisioning_cql("dc-1;DROP", 1),
            Err(CassandraTenantError::InvalidDatacenter)
        ));
    }
}

========== crates/proxima-engine/src/elasticsearch_tenant.rs ==========
//! Elasticsearch native-role policy helpers. These helpers do not connect to
//! clusters, provision credentials, or implement an Elasticsearch/OpenSearch proxy.
use serde_json::{json, Value};

pub fn valid_tenant_id(value: &str) -> bool {
    !value.is_empty()
        && value.len() <= 48
        && value
            .bytes()
            .all(|b| b.is_ascii_lowercase() || b.is_ascii_digit() || b"_-".contains(&b))
}

pub fn tenant_index_pattern(tenant_id: &str) -> Option<String> {
    valid_tenant_id(tenant_id).then(|| format!("tenant-{tenant_id}-*"))
}

/// Build a least-privilege Elasticsearch role descriptor. The caller must
/// provision it through a trusted admin channel and test effective privileges.
pub fn elasticsearch_role_descriptor(tenant_id: &str) -> Option<Value> {
    let pattern = tenant_index_pattern(tenant_id)?;
    Some(json!({
        "indices": [{
            "names": [pattern],
            "privileges": ["read", "write"],
            "query": {"term": {"tenant_id": tenant_id}},
            "field_security": {"grant": ["tenant_id", "id", "created_at", "updated_at", "data"]}
        }],
        "cluster": [],
        "applications": [],
        "run_as": [],
        "metadata": {"managed_by": "proxima-engine", "tenant_id": tenant_id},
        "transient_metadata": {"enabled": true}
    }))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use serde_json::json;

    #[test]
    fn tenant_namespace_is_exact_and_bounded() {
        assert_eq!(
            tenant_index_pattern("tenant_42").as_deref(),
            Some("tenant-tenant_42-*")
        );
        assert!(tenant_index_pattern("Tenant_42").is_none());
        assert!(tenant_index_pattern("tenant/42").is_none());
        assert!(tenant_index_pattern("").is_none());
        assert!(tenant_index_pattern(&"a".repeat(49)).is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn role_descriptor_scopes_index_and_document_access() {
        let role = elasticsearch_role_descriptor("acme_01").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(role["indices"][0]["names"][0], "tenant-acme_01-*");
        assert_eq!(role["indices"][0]["query"]["term"]["tenant_id"], "acme_01");
        assert_eq!(role["indices"][0]["privileges"], json!(["read", "write"]));
        assert_eq!(role["cluster"], json!([]));
        assert!(elasticsearch_role_descriptor("Acme").is_none());
    }
}

========== crates/proxima-engine/src/opensearch_tenant.rs ==========
//! OpenSearch Security-plugin role descriptor helpers.
//!
//! This module builds an OpenSearch-specific role payload. It is deliberately
//! separate from Elasticsearch's native role API; it does not provision roles,
//! create API keys, or connect to a cluster.
use serde_json::{json, Value};

pub fn valid_tenant_id(value: &str) -> bool {
    !value.is_empty()
        && value.len() <= 48
        && value
            .bytes()
            .all(|b| b.is_ascii_lowercase() || b.is_ascii_digit() || b"_-".contains(&b))
}

pub fn tenant_index_pattern(tenant_id: &str) -> Option<String> {
    valid_tenant_id(tenant_id).then(|| format!("tenant-{tenant_id}-*"))
}

/// Build a Security-plugin role body with an exact tenant index pattern,
/// explicit data-plane actions, DLS and FLS. The authenticated caller must
/// still bind the resulting role to a tenant-specific backend role or API key.
pub fn opensearch_role_descriptor(tenant_id: &str) -> Option<Value> {
    let pattern = tenant_index_pattern(tenant_id)?;
    let dls = json!({"term":{"tenant_id":tenant_id}}).to_string();
    Some(json!({
        "cluster_permissions": [],
        "index_permissions": [{
            "index_patterns": [pattern],
            "allowed_actions": [
                "indices:data/read/search",
                "indices:data/read/get",
                "indices:data/write/index",
                "indices:data/write/update",
                "indices:data/write/delete"
            ],
            "dls": dls,
            "fls": ["tenant_id", "id", "created_at", "updated_at", "data"]
        }],
        "tenant_permissions": [],
        "description": format!("Proxima tenant-scoped role for {tenant_id}")
    }))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn role_is_scoped_and_has_no_cluster_permissions() {
        let role = opensearch_role_descriptor("org_01").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(role["cluster_permissions"], json!([]));
        assert_eq!(role["tenant_permissions"], json!([]));
        assert_eq!(
            role["index_permissions"][0]["index_patterns"][0],
            "tenant-org_01-*"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            role["index_permissions"][0]["fls"],
            json!(["tenant_id", "id", "created_at", "updated_at", "data"])
        );
        let dls: Value =
            serde_json::from_str(role["index_permissions"][0]["dls"].as_str().unwrap()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(dls["term"]["tenant_id"], "org_01");
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_ambiguous_or_injected_tenant_ids() {
        for value in ["", "Org_01", "../org", "org/a", "org a", &"x".repeat(49)] {
            assert!(opensearch_role_descriptor(value).is_none());
        }
    }
}

========== crates/proxima-engine/src/mongodb_gateway.rs ==========
use crate::mongodb_policy::{MongoSessionRegistry, MongoTenantPolicy};
use mongodb::bson::{self, Bson, Document};
use rustls::pki_types::{CertificateDer, PrivateKeyDer, ServerName};
use rustls::{ClientConfig, RootCertStore, ServerConfig};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::fs::File;
use std::io::{self, BufReader};
use std::path::Path;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;
use tokio::io::{AsyncRead, AsyncReadExt, AsyncWrite, AsyncWriteExt};
use tokio::net::{TcpListener, TcpStream};
use tokio::sync::Semaphore;
use tokio_rustls::{TlsAcceptor, TlsConnector};
use tracing::{info, warn};

const OP_MSG: i32 = 2013;
const MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES: usize = 48 * 1024 * 1024;
const MAX_SEQUENCE_DOCUMENTS: usize = 100_000;
const SCRAM_SHA_256: &str = "SCRAM-SHA-256";

trait MongoIo: AsyncRead + AsyncWrite + Unpin + Send {}
impl<T> MongoIo for T where T: AsyncRead + AsyncWrite + Unpin + Send {}
type BoxedMongoIo = Box<dyn MongoIo>;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
struct MongoTenantIdentity {
    username: String,
    tenant_id: String,
    database: String,
    auth_source: String,
}

pub struct MongoIdentityRegistry {
    users: HashMap<String, MongoTenantIdentity>,
}

impl MongoIdentityRegistry {
    pub fn load(path: impl AsRef<Path>) -> io::Result<Self> {
        let bytes = std::fs::read(path)?;
        let value: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|error| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                format!("invalid MongoDB identity registry JSON: {error}"),
            )
        })?;
        let records = value
            .get("users")
            .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_array)
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                    "MongoDB identity registry must contain a users array",
                )
            })?;
        if records.is_empty() {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                "MongoDB identity registry cannot be empty",
            ));
        }

        let mut users = HashMap::new();
        let mut tenant_databases: HashMap<String, String> = HashMap::new();
        for (index, record) in records.iter().enumerate() {
            let field = |name: &str| -> io::Result<String> {
                record
                    .get(name)
                    .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)
                    .map(str::trim)
                    .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
                    .map(str::to_owned)
                    .ok_or_else(|| {
                        io::Error::new(
                            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                            format!("users[{index}].{name} is required"),
                        )
                    })
            };
            let username = field("username")?;
            let tenant_id = field("tenant_id")?;
            let database = field("database")?;
            let auth_source = record
                .get("auth_source")
                .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)
                .unwrap_or("admin")
                .trim()
                .to_owned();
            if username.len() > 128 || !username.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_graphic()) {
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                    format!("users[{index}].username is invalid"),
                ));
            }
            if auth_source != "admin" {
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                    "the initial MongoDB gateway supports admin as the SCRAM authentication source",
                ));
            }
            MongoTenantPolicy::new(tenant_id.clone(), database.clone()).map_err(|error| {
                io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                    format!("users[{index}] has an invalid tenant binding: {error}"),
                )
            })?;
            if let Some(existing) = tenant_databases.get(&tenant_id) {
                if existing != &database {
                    return Err(io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                        format!("tenant {tenant_id} maps to more than one database"),
                    ));
                }
            } else if tenant_databases
                .values()
                .any(|existing| existing == &database)
            {
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                    format!("database {database} is assigned to multiple tenants"),
                ));
            } else {
                tenant_databases.insert(tenant_id.clone(), database.clone());
            }
            let identity = MongoTenantIdentity {
                username: username.clone(),
                tenant_id,
                database,
                auth_source,
            };
            if users.insert(username.clone(), identity).is_some() {
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                    format!("duplicate MongoDB gateway username: {username}"),
                ));
            }
        }
        Ok(Self { users })
    }

    fn get(&self, username: &str) -> Option<MongoTenantIdentity> {
        self.users.get(username).cloned()
    }

    pub fn principal_count(&self) -> usize {
        self.users.len()
    }
}

struct MongoGatewayConfig {
    listen_addr: String,
    upstream_addr: String,
    upstream_tls: bool,
    upstream_tls_server_name: Option<String>,
    upstream_tls_ca_file: Option<String>,
    max_connections: usize,
}

impl MongoGatewayConfig {
    fn from_env() -> io::Result<Self> {
        let listen_addr = env_required_or("PROXIMA_MONGODB_LISTEN_ADDR", "127.0.0.1:27018")?;
        let upstream_addr = env_required("PROXIMA_MONGODB_UPSTREAM_ADDR")?;
        let upstream_tls = env_bool("PROXIMA_MONGODB_UPSTREAM_TLS", true)?;
        let upstream_tls_server_name = env_optional("PROXIMA_MONGODB_UPSTREAM_TLS_SERVER_NAME");
        let upstream_tls_ca_file = env_optional("PROXIMA_MONGODB_UPSTREAM_TLS_CA_FILE");
        if upstream_tls && (upstream_tls_server_name.is_none() || upstream_tls_ca_file.is_none()) {
            return Err(io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, "MongoDB upstream TLS requires PROXIMA_MONGODB_UPSTREAM_TLS_SERVER_NAME and PROXIMA_MONGODB_UPSTREAM_TLS_CA_FILE"));
        }
        let max_connections = env_optional("PROXIMA_MONGODB_MAX_CONNECTIONS")
            .unwrap_or_else(|| "256".to_owned())
            .parse::<usize>()
            .map_err(|error| {
                io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                    format!("invalid PROXIMA_MONGODB_MAX_CONNECTIONS: {error}"),
                )
            })?;
        if max_connections == 0 {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                "PROXIMA_MONGODB_MAX_CONNECTIONS must be greater than zero",
            ));
        }
        Ok(Self {
            listen_addr,
            upstream_addr,
            upstream_tls,
            upstream_tls_server_name,
            upstream_tls_ca_file,
            max_connections,
        })
    }
}

pub async fn run_mongodb_gateway() -> io::Result<()> {
    let _ = rustls::crypto::ring::default_provider().install_default();
    let config = MongoGatewayConfig::from_env()?;
    let registry_path = env_required("PROXIMA_MONGODB_IDENTITY_REGISTRY")?;
    let cert_path = env_required("PROXIMA_MONGODB_TLS_CERT_FILE")?;
    let key_path = env_required("PROXIMA_MONGODB_TLS_KEY_FILE")?;
    let registry = Arc::new(MongoIdentityRegistry::load(registry_path)?);
    let acceptor = build_server_tls(&cert_path, &key_path)?;
    let connector = if config.upstream_tls {
        Some(build_client_tls(
            config.upstream_tls_ca_file.as_deref().unwrap(),
        )?)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let listener = TcpListener::bind(&config.listen_addr).await?;
    let permits = Arc::new(Semaphore::new(config.max_connections));
    let sessions = Arc::new(MongoSessionRegistry::default());

    info!(listen = %config.listen_addr, upstream = %config.upstream_addr,
        principals = registry.principal_count(), max_connections = config.max_connections,
        upstream_tls = config.upstream_tls, "Proxima MongoDB wire gateway listening");

    loop {
        let (socket, peer) = listener.accept().await?;
        let permit = match permits.clone().try_acquire_owned() {
            Ok(permit) => permit,
            Err(_) => {
                warn!(%peer, "MongoDB gateway connection limit reached");
                drop(socket);
                continue;
            }
        };
        let acceptor = acceptor.clone();
        let connector = connector.clone();
        let upstream = config.upstream_addr.clone();
        let server_name = config.upstream_tls_server_name.clone();
        let registry = registry.clone();
        let sessions = sessions.clone();
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            let _permit = permit;
            if let Err(error) = handle_connection(
                socket,
                acceptor,
                connector,
                &upstream,
                server_name.as_deref(),
                registry,
                sessions,
            )
            .await
            {
                warn!(%peer, %error, "MongoDB gateway session closed");
            }
        });
    }
}

async fn handle_connection(
    socket: TcpStream,
    acceptor: TlsAcceptor,
    connector: Option<TlsConnector>,
    upstream_addr: &str,
    upstream_server_name: Option<&str>,
    registry: Arc<MongoIdentityRegistry>,
    sessions: Arc<MongoSessionRegistry>,
) -> io::Result<()> {
    let mut client: BoxedMongoIo = Box::new(
        tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(10), acceptor.accept(socket))
            .await
            .map_err(|_| {
                io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::TimedOut,
                    "MongoDB client TLS handshake timed out",
                )
            })?
            .map_err(|error| {
                io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                    format!("MongoDB client TLS handshake failed: {error}"),
                )
            })?,
    );

    let upstream_socket =
        tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(10), TcpStream::connect(upstream_addr))
            .await
            .map_err(|_| {
                io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::TimedOut,
                    "MongoDB upstream connection timed out",
                )
            })??;
    let mut upstream: BoxedMongoIo = if let Some(connector) = connector {
        let server_name = ServerName::try_from(upstream_server_name.unwrap_or_default().to_owned())
            .map_err(|_| {
                io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                    "invalid MongoDB upstream TLS server name",
                )
            })?;
        Box::new(
            tokio::time::timeout(
                Duration::from_secs(10),
                connector.connect(server_name, upstream_socket),
            )
            .await
            .map_err(|_| {
                io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::TimedOut,
                    "MongoDB upstream TLS handshake timed out",
                )
            })?
            .map_err(|error| {
                io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                    format!("MongoDB upstream TLS handshake failed: {error}"),
                )
            })?,
        )
    } else {
        Box::new(upstream_socket)
    };

    let mut identity: Option<MongoTenantIdentity> = None;
    let mut authenticated = false;

    loop {
        let frame = read_frame(&mut client).await?;
        let mut message = match decode_op_msg(&frame) {
            Ok(message) => message,
            Err(error) => {
                send_command_error(&mut client, request_id(&frame), "admin", &error.to_string())
                    .await?;
                return Err(error);
            }
        };
        let command_name = message.body.keys().next().cloned().unwrap_or_default();

        match command_name.as_str() {
            "hello" | "isMaster" => {
                // Disable wire compression and speculative SCRAM so every later
                // command remains inspectable and authentication follows one path.
                message.body.remove("compression");
                message.body.remove("speculativeAuthenticate");
                write_frame(
                    &mut upstream,
                    &encode_op_msg(message.request_id, 0, &message.body)?,
                )
                .await?;
            }
            "saslStart" => {
                if authenticated || identity.is_some() {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        "admin",
                        "authentication restart is not permitted",
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                }
                let mechanism = message.body.get_str("mechanism").unwrap_or_default();
                if mechanism != SCRAM_SHA_256 {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        "admin",
                        "only SCRAM-SHA-256 authentication is supported",
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                }
                let username = match scram_username(&message.body) {
                    Ok(username) => username,
                    Err(error) => {
                        send_command_error(
                            &mut client,
                            message.request_id,
                            "admin",
                            &error.to_string(),
                        )
                        .await?;
                        continue;
                    }
                };
                let Some(principal) = registry.get(&username) else {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        "admin",
                        "unknown MongoDB gateway principal",
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                };
                if message.body.get_str("$db").unwrap_or_default() != principal.auth_source {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        "admin",
                        "SCRAM authentication source does not match the principal registry",
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                }
                identity = Some(principal);
                write_frame(&mut upstream, &frame).await?;
            }
            "saslContinue" => {
                if identity.is_none() || authenticated {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        "admin",
                        "unexpected SCRAM continuation",
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                }
                write_frame(&mut upstream, &frame).await?;
            }
            "ping" => {
                if !authenticated || message.body.get_str("$db").unwrap_or_default() != "admin" {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        "admin",
                        "ping requires an authenticated principal and the admin database",
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                }
                write_frame(&mut upstream, &frame).await?;
            }
            "endSessions" => {
                let Some(principal) = identity.as_ref() else {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        "admin",
                        "authentication is required",
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                };
                if !authenticated || message.body.get_str("$db").unwrap_or_default() != "admin" {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        "admin",
                        "endSessions requires an authenticated principal on the admin database",
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                }
                let end_sessions = match message.body.get_array("endSessions") {
                    Ok(values) if values.len() <= 10_000 => values,
                    _ => {
                        send_command_error(
                            &mut client,
                            message.request_id,
                            "admin",
                            "endSessions must contain at most 10000 logical session identifiers",
                        )
                        .await?;
                        continue;
                    }
                };
                let mut invalid_session = None;
                for value in end_sessions {
                    let Some(lsid) = value.as_document() else {
                        invalid_session = Some(
                            "endSessions contains a non-document session identifier".to_owned(),
                        );
                        break;
                    };
                    if let Err(error) = sessions.release_if_owned(&principal.tenant_id, lsid) {
                        invalid_session = Some(error.to_string());
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if let Some(error) = invalid_session {
                    send_command_error(&mut client, message.request_id, "admin", &error).await?;
                    continue;
                }
                write_frame(&mut upstream, &frame).await?;
            }
            _ => {
                let Some(principal) = identity.as_ref() else {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        "admin",
                        "authentication is required before data commands",
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                };
                if !authenticated {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        "admin",
                        "SCRAM authentication has not completed",
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                }
                let mut policy_command = message.body.clone();
                if let Err(error) = merge_sequences(&mut policy_command, &message.sequences) {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        &principal.database,
                        &error.to_string(),
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                }
                if matches!(policy_command.get_bool("startTransaction"), Ok(true))
                    || matches!(policy_command.get_bool("autocommit"), Ok(false))
                {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        &principal.database,
                        "MongoDB transactions are not supported by this gateway profile",
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                }
                if let Ok(lsid) = policy_command.get_document("lsid") {
                    if let Err(error) = sessions.bind(&principal.tenant_id, lsid) {
                        send_command_error(
                            &mut client,
                            message.request_id,
                            &principal.database,
                            &error.to_string(),
                        )
                        .await?;
                        continue;
                    }
                    if let Err(error) = sessions.authorize(&principal.tenant_id, lsid) {
                        send_command_error(
                            &mut client,
                            message.request_id,
                            &principal.database,
                            &error.to_string(),
                        )
                        .await?;
                        continue;
                    }
                }
                let policy = MongoTenantPolicy::new(&principal.tenant_id, &principal.database)
                    .map_err(|error| {
                        io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidData, error.to_string())
                    })?;
                if let Err(error) = policy.authorize(&policy_command) {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        &principal.database,
                        &error.to_string(),
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                }
                write_frame(&mut upstream, &frame).await?;
            }
        }

        let response = read_frame(&mut upstream).await?;
        if response.len() < 16 {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                "truncated MongoDB upstream response",
            ));
        }
        let response_to = i32::from_le_bytes(response[8..12].try_into().unwrap());
        if response_to != message.request_id {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                "MongoDB upstream response correlation mismatch",
            ));
        }

        if matches!(command_name.as_str(), "saslStart" | "saslContinue") {
            if let Ok(reply) = decode_op_msg(&response) {
                let ok = match reply.body.get("ok") {
                    Some(Bson::Double(value)) => *value == 1.0,
                    Some(Bson::Int32(value)) => *value == 1,
                    Some(Bson::Int64(value)) => *value == 1,
                    _ => false,
                };
                if ok && matches!(reply.body.get_bool("done"), Ok(true)) {
                    authenticated = true;
                }
            }
        }

        let mut client_response = response;
        if matches!(command_name.as_str(), "hello" | "isMaster") {
            let mut reply = decode_op_msg(&client_response)?;
            let writable_primary = matches!(reply.body.get_bool("isWritablePrimary"), Ok(true))
                || matches!(reply.body.get_bool("ismaster"), Ok(true));
            if !writable_primary {
                send_command_error(
                    &mut client,
                    message.request_id,
                    "admin",
                    "configured MongoDB upstream is not a writable primary or standalone node",
                )
                .await?;
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                    "MongoDB upstream is not writable",
                ));
            }
            if matches!(reply.body.get_str("msg"), Ok("isdbgrid")) {
                send_command_error(
                    &mut client,
                    message.request_id,
                    "admin",
                    "sharded MongoDB upstreams are not supported by this gateway profile",
                )
                .await?;
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::Unsupported,
                    "sharded MongoDB upstream is not supported",
                ));
            }
            for field in [
                "compression",
                "speculativeAuthenticate",
                "hosts",
                "passives",
                "arbiters",
                "primary",
                "setName",
                "setVersion",
                "electionId",
                "topologyVersion",
                "me",
                "msg",
                "serviceId",
                "secondary",
                "arbiterOnly",
                "hidden",
                "passive",
            ] {
                reply.body.remove(field);
            }
            client_response = encode_op_msg(reply.request_id, reply.response_to, &reply.body)?;
        }
        write_frame(&mut client, &client_response).await?;
    }
}

struct MongoMessage {
    request_id: i32,
    response_to: i32,
    body: Document,
    sequences: Vec<(String, Vec<Document>)>,
}

fn request_id(frame: &[u8]) -> i32 {
    if frame.len() >= 8 {
        i32::from_le_bytes(frame[4..8].try_into().unwrap())
    } else {
        0
    }
}

async fn read_frame(stream: &mut BoxedMongoIo) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut header = [0u8; 4];
    stream.read_exact(&mut header).await?;
    let length = i32::from_le_bytes(header) as usize;
    if !(16..=MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES).contains(&length) {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            format!("invalid MongoDB wire message length: {length}"),
        ));
    }
    let mut frame = vec![0u8; length];
    frame[..4].copy_from_slice(&header);
    stream.read_exact(&mut frame[4..]).await?;
    Ok(frame)
}

fn decode_op_msg(frame: &[u8]) -> io::Result<MongoMessage> {
    if frame.len() < 21 {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "truncated MongoDB OP_MSG",
        ));
    }
    let length = i32::from_le_bytes(frame[..4].try_into().unwrap()) as usize;
    if length != frame.len() || length > MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "MongoDB OP_MSG length mismatch",
        ));
    }
    let request_id = i32::from_le_bytes(frame[4..8].try_into().unwrap());
    let response_to = i32::from_le_bytes(frame[8..12].try_into().unwrap());
    let opcode = i32::from_le_bytes(frame[12..16].try_into().unwrap());
    if opcode != OP_MSG {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::Unsupported,
            format!("MongoDB opcode {opcode} is not supported; only OP_MSG is accepted"),
        ));
    }
    let flags = i32::from_le_bytes(frame[16..20].try_into().unwrap());
    if flags != 0 {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::Unsupported,
            "MongoDB OP_MSG checksums are not supported",
        ));
    }

    let mut offset = 20usize;
    let mut body = None;
    let mut sequences = Vec::new();
    while offset < frame.len() {
        let kind = frame[offset];
        offset += 1;
        match kind {
            0 => {
                let (document, consumed) = read_bson_document(frame, offset, frame.len())?;
                if body.replace(document).is_some() {
                    return Err(io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                        "MongoDB OP_MSG contains multiple body sections",
                    ));
                }
                offset = consumed;
            }
            1 => {
                let section_start = offset;
                if offset + 4 > frame.len() {
                    return Err(io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                        "truncated MongoDB document sequence",
                    ));
                }
                let section_size =
                    i32::from_le_bytes(frame[offset..offset + 4].try_into().unwrap());
                if section_size < 5 {
                    return Err(io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                        "invalid MongoDB document sequence size",
                    ));
                }
                let section_end = section_start
                    .checked_add(section_size as usize)
                    .filter(|end| *end <= frame.len())
                    .ok_or_else(|| {
                        io::Error::new(
                            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                            "MongoDB document sequence exceeds frame",
                        )
                    })?;
                offset += 4;
                let nul = frame[offset..section_end]
                    .iter()
                    .position(|byte| *byte == 0)
                    .ok_or_else(|| {
                        io::Error::new(
                            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                            "MongoDB document sequence identifier is unterminated",
                        )
                    })?;
                let identifier = std::str::from_utf8(&frame[offset..offset + nul])
                    .map_err(|_| {
                        io::Error::new(
                            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                            "MongoDB document sequence identifier is not UTF-8",
                        )
                    })?
                    .to_owned();
                offset += nul + 1;
                let mut documents = Vec::new();
                while offset < section_end {
                    if documents.len() >= MAX_SEQUENCE_DOCUMENTS {
                        return Err(io::Error::new(
                            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                            "MongoDB document sequence exceeds document limit",
                        ));
                    }
                    let (document, consumed) = read_bson_document(frame, offset, section_end)?;
                    documents.push(document);
                    offset = consumed;
                }
                if offset != section_end {
                    return Err(io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                        "MongoDB document sequence boundary mismatch",
                    ));
                }
                sequences.push((identifier, documents));
            }
            other => {
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                    format!("unknown MongoDB OP_MSG section kind: {other}"),
                ))
            }
        }
    }
    let body = body.ok_or_else(|| {
        io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "MongoDB OP_MSG is missing its body section",
        )
    })?;
    Ok(MongoMessage {
        request_id,
        response_to,
        body,
        sequences,
    })
}

fn read_bson_document(frame: &[u8], offset: usize, limit: usize) -> io::Result<(Document, usize)> {
    if offset + 4 > limit {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "truncated BSON document length",
        ));
    }
    let length = i32::from_le_bytes(frame[offset..offset + 4].try_into().unwrap()) as usize;
    if length < 5 || offset.checked_add(length).is_none_or(|end| end > limit) {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "invalid BSON document length",
        ));
    }
    let end = offset + length;
    let document = bson::from_slice::<Document>(&frame[offset..end]).map_err(|error| {
        io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            format!("invalid MongoDB BSON document: {error}"),
        )
    })?;
    Ok((document, end))
}

fn merge_sequences(
    command: &mut Document,
    sequences: &[(String, Vec<Document>)],
) -> io::Result<()> {
    for (identifier, documents) in sequences {
        if !matches!(identifier.as_str(), "documents" | "updates" | "deletes") {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                format!("MongoDB document sequence {identifier} is not admitted"),
            ));
        }
        if command.contains_key(identifier) {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                format!("MongoDB command contains duplicate {identifier} payload"),
            ));
        }
        command.insert(
            identifier,
            Bson::Array(documents.iter().cloned().map(Bson::Document).collect()),
        );
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn encode_op_msg(request_id: i32, response_to: i32, body: &Document) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let bson = bson::to_vec(body).map_err(|error| {
        io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            format!("could not encode MongoDB BSON: {error}"),
        )
    })?;
    let length = 16usize
        .checked_add(4)
        .and_then(|value| value.checked_add(1))
        .and_then(|value| value.checked_add(bson.len()))
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                "MongoDB response length overflow",
            )
        })?;
    if length > MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
            "MongoDB response exceeds maximum wire message size",
        ));
    }
    let mut frame = Vec::with_capacity(length);
    frame.extend_from_slice(&(length as i32).to_le_bytes());
    frame.extend_from_slice(&request_id.to_le_bytes());
    frame.extend_from_slice(&response_to.to_le_bytes());
    frame.extend_from_slice(&OP_MSG.to_le_bytes());
    frame.extend_from_slice(&0i32.to_le_bytes());
    frame.push(0);
    frame.extend_from_slice(&bson);
    Ok(frame)
}

async fn send_command_error(
    stream: &mut BoxedMongoIo,
    request_id: i32,
    database: &str,
    message: &str,
) -> io::Result<()> {
    let mut response = Document::new();
    response.insert("ok", 0.0);
    response.insert("errmsg", message);
    response.insert("code", 13);
    response.insert("codeName", "Unauthorized");
    response.insert("$db", database);
    let frame = encode_op_msg(request_id.wrapping_add(1), request_id, &response)?;
    write_frame(stream, &frame).await
}

async fn write_frame(stream: &mut BoxedMongoIo, frame: &[u8]) -> io::Result<()> {
    stream.write_all(frame).await?;
    stream.flush().await
}

fn scram_username(command: &Document) -> io::Result<String> {
    let payload = match command.get("payload") {
        Some(Bson::Binary(binary)) => &binary.bytes,
        _ => {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                "SCRAM saslStart payload must be BSON binary",
            ))
        }
    };
    let payload = std::str::from_utf8(payload).map_err(|_| {
        io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "SCRAM client-first message is not UTF-8",
        )
    })?;
    let attributes = payload.strip_prefix("n,,").ok_or_else(|| {
        io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
            "SCRAM channel binding is not supported by this gateway",
        )
    })?;
    let encoded = attributes
        .split(',')
        .find_map(|attribute| attribute.strip_prefix("n="))
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                "SCRAM client-first message has no username",
            )
        })?;
    let username = encoded.replace("=2C", ",").replace("=3D", "=");
    if username.is_empty() || username.len() > 128 {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "SCRAM username is invalid",
        ));
    }
    Ok(username)
}

fn build_server_tls(cert_path: &str, key_path: &str) -> io::Result<TlsAcceptor> {
    let certs = load_certs(cert_path)?;
    let key = load_private_key(key_path)?;
    let config = ServerConfig::builder()
        .with_no_client_auth()
        .with_single_cert(certs, key)
        .map_err(|error| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                format!("invalid MongoDB gateway TLS certificate/key: {error}"),
            )
        })?;
    Ok(TlsAcceptor::from(Arc::new(config)))
}

fn build_client_tls(ca_path: &str) -> io::Result<TlsConnector> {
    let mut roots = RootCertStore::empty();
    for certificate in load_certs(ca_path)? {
        roots.add(certificate).map_err(|error| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                format!("invalid MongoDB upstream CA: {error}"),
            )
        })?;
    }
    let config = ClientConfig::builder()
        .with_root_certificates(roots)
        .with_no_client_auth();
    Ok(TlsConnector::from(Arc::new(config)))
}

fn load_certs(path: &str) -> io::Result<Vec<CertificateDer<'static>>> {
    let file = File::open(path)?;
    rustls_pemfile::certs(&mut BufReader::new(file))
        .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
        .map_err(|error| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                format!("invalid certificate PEM: {error}"),
            )
        })
}

fn load_private_key(path: &str) -> io::Result<PrivateKeyDer<'static>> {
    rustls_pemfile::private_key(&mut BufReader::new(File::open(path)?))
        .map_err(|error| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                format!("invalid private key PEM: {error}"),
            )
        })?
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                "MongoDB gateway private key is missing",
            )
        })
}

fn env_required(name: &str) -> io::Result<String> {
    std::env::var(name)
        .map_err(|_| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, format!("{name} is required")))
        .and_then(|value| {
            if value.trim().is_empty() {
                Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                    format!("{name} cannot be empty"),
                ))
            } else {
                Ok(value)
            }
        })
}

fn env_required_or(name: &str, default: &str) -> io::Result<String> {
    Ok(std::env::var(name).unwrap_or_else(|_| default.to_owned()))
}

fn env_optional(name: &str) -> Option<String> {
    std::env::var(name)
        .ok()
        .filter(|value| !value.trim().is_empty())
}

fn env_bool(name: &str, default: bool) -> io::Result<bool> {
    match std::env::var(name) {
        Ok(value) => match value.trim().to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
            "true" | "1" | "yes" | "on" => Ok(true),
            "false" | "0" | "no" | "off" => Ok(false),
            other => Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                format!("invalid {name}: {other}"),
            )),
        },
        Err(_) => Ok(default),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use mongodb::bson::doc;

    #[test]
    fn op_msg_round_trip_preserves_command_document() {
        let command =
            doc! { "find": "records", "filter": { "active": true }, "$db": "tenant_a_db" };
        let frame = encode_op_msg(7, 0, &command).unwrap();
        let parsed = decode_op_msg(&frame).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(parsed.request_id, 7);
        assert_eq!(parsed.body, command);
    }

    #[test]
    fn hello_reply_removes_upstream_topology_addresses() {
        let mut reply = doc! {
            "ok": 1.0,
            "isWritablePrimary": true,
            "setName": "rs0",
            "hosts": ["mongo-a.internal:27017", "mongo-b.internal:27017"],
            "primary": "mongo-a.internal:27017",
            "topologyVersion": { "counter": 1_i64 }
        };
        for field in [
            "hosts",
            "passives",
            "arbiters",
            "primary",
            "setName",
            "setVersion",
            "electionId",
            "topologyVersion",
            "me",
            "msg",
            "serviceId",
        ] {
            reply.remove(field);
        }
        assert!(reply.get("hosts").is_none());
        assert!(reply.get("setName").is_none());
        assert_eq!(reply.get_bool("isWritablePrimary").unwrap(), true);
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_non_op_msg_and_checksum_flags() {
        let mut frame = encode_op_msg(7, 0, &Document::new()).unwrap();
        frame[12..16].copy_from_slice(&2004i32.to_le_bytes());
        assert!(decode_op_msg(&frame).is_err());

        let mut frame = encode_op_msg(7, 0, &Document::new()).unwrap();
        frame[16..20].copy_from_slice(&1i32.to_le_bytes());
        assert!(decode_op_msg(&frame).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn merges_insert_document_sequences_for_policy_validation() {
        let mut command = doc! { "insert": "records", "$db": "tenant_a_db" };
        merge_sequences(
            &mut command,
            &[("documents".into(), vec![doc! { "secret": "a" }])],
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(command.get_array("documents").unwrap().len(), 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn scram_username_is_decoded_from_client_first_message() {
        let command = doc! {
            "saslStart": 1,
            "mechanism": SCRAM_SHA_256,
            "payload": bson::Binary { subtype: bson::spec::BinarySubtype::Generic, bytes: b"n,,n=tenant=2Ca,r=nonce".to_vec() }
        };
        assert_eq!(scram_username(&command).unwrap(), "tenant,a");
    }
}

========== crates/proxima-engine/src/redis_gateway.rs ==========
use crate::redis_protocol::{encode_command, parse_command, rewrite_keys, MAX_FRAME_BYTES};
use rustls::pki_types::{CertificateDer, PrivateKeyDer, ServerName};
use rustls::{ClientConfig, RootCertStore, ServerConfig};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use std::{
    collections::HashMap,
    fs::File,
    future::Future,
    io::{self, BufReader},
    net::SocketAddr,
    path::Path,
    pin::Pin,
    sync::Arc,
    time::Duration,
};
use tokio::{
    io::{AsyncRead, AsyncReadExt, AsyncWrite, AsyncWriteExt},
    net::{TcpListener, TcpStream},
    sync::Semaphore,
    time::timeout,
};
use tokio_rustls::{TlsAcceptor, TlsConnector};
use tracing::{info, warn};

trait RedisIo: AsyncRead + AsyncWrite + Unpin + Send {}
impl<T: AsyncRead + AsyncWrite + Unpin + Send> RedisIo for T {}
type Io = Box<dyn RedisIo>;

#[derive(Clone)]
struct Principal {
    tenant_id: String,
    client_hash: [u8; 32],
    upstream_user: String,
    upstream_password: String,
}
pub struct RedisIdentityRegistry {
    users: HashMap<String, Principal>,
}
impl RedisIdentityRegistry {
    pub fn load(path: impl AsRef<Path>) -> io::Result<Self> {
        let raw = std::fs::read(path)?;
        let root: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&raw).map_err(invalid)?;
        let records = root
            .get("users")
            .and_then(|v| v.as_array())
            .ok_or_else(|| invalid("users array required"))?;
        if records.is_empty() {
            return Err(invalid("at least one Redis principal is required"));
        }
        let mut users = HashMap::new();
        let mut tenant_users = HashMap::new();
        let mut upstream_tenants = HashMap::new();
        for (i, r) in records.iter().enumerate() {
            let field = |k: &str| -> io::Result<String> {
                r.get(k)
                    .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                    .map(str::trim)
                    .filter(|v| !v.is_empty())
                    .map(str::to_owned)
                    .ok_or_else(|| invalid(format!("users[{i}].{k} is required")))
            };
            let username = field("username")?;
            let tenant_id = field("tenant_id")?;
            let hash = hex::decode(field("client_password_sha256")?).map_err(invalid)?;
            let client_hash: [u8; 32] = hash.try_into().map_err(|_| {
                invalid(format!(
                    "users[{i}].client_password_sha256 must be 32 bytes"
                ))
            })?;
            let upstream_user = field("upstream_username")?;
            let upstream_password = field("upstream_password")?;
            if username.len() > 128
                || tenant_id.len() > 256
                || upstream_user.len() > 128
                || username.bytes().any(|b| !b.is_ascii_graphic())
                || upstream_user.bytes().any(|b| !b.is_ascii_graphic())
            {
                return Err(invalid(format!(
                    "users[{i}] contains an invalid identifier"
                )));
            }
            if users.contains_key(&username) {
                return Err(invalid("duplicate Redis gateway username"));
            }
            if let Some(old) = tenant_users.insert(tenant_id.clone(), upstream_user.clone()) {
                if old != upstream_user {
                    return Err(invalid(
                        "one tenant cannot map to multiple upstream Redis ACL users",
                    ));
                }
            }
            if let Some(old_tenant) =
                upstream_tenants.insert(upstream_user.clone(), tenant_id.clone())
            {
                if old_tenant != tenant_id {
                    return Err(invalid(
                        "upstream Redis ACL users must be unique per tenant",
                    ));
                }
            }
            users.insert(
                username,
                Principal {
                    tenant_id,
                    client_hash,
                    upstream_user,
                    upstream_password,
                },
            );
        }
        Ok(Self { users })
    }
    fn authenticate(&self, user: &str, password: &[u8]) -> Option<Principal> {
        let p = self.users.get(user)?;
        let got = Sha256::digest(password);
        let mut diff = 0u8;
        for (a, b) in got.iter().zip(p.client_hash.iter()) {
            diff |= a ^ b;
        }
        if diff == 0 {
            Some(p.clone())
        } else {
            None
        }
    }
    pub fn principal_count(&self) -> usize {
        self.users.len()
    }
}
fn invalid(e: impl std::fmt::Display) -> io::Error {
    io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidData, e.to_string())
}

struct Config {
    listen: SocketAddr,
    upstream: String,
    upstream_name: String,
    ca: String,
    cert: String,
    key: String,
    max: usize,
}
impl Config {
    fn load() -> io::Result<Self> {
        let get = |k: &str| {
            std::env::var(k).map_err(|_| {
                io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, format!("{k} is required"))
            })
        };
        let listen = std::env::var("PROXIMA_REDIS_LISTEN_ADDR")
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| "127.0.0.1:6380".into())
            .parse()
            .map_err(invalid)?;
        let max = std::env::var("PROXIMA_REDIS_MAX_CONNECTIONS")
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| "128".into())
            .parse::<usize>()
            .map_err(invalid)?;
        if max == 0 {
            return Err(invalid("PROXIMA_REDIS_MAX_CONNECTIONS must be positive"));
        }
        Ok(Self {
            listen,
            upstream: get("PROXIMA_REDIS_UPSTREAM_ADDR")?,
            upstream_name: get("PROXIMA_REDIS_UPSTREAM_TLS_SERVER_NAME")?,
            ca: get("PROXIMA_REDIS_UPSTREAM_TLS_CA_FILE")?,
            cert: get("PROXIMA_REDIS_TLS_CERT_FILE")?,
            key: get("PROXIMA_REDIS_TLS_KEY_FILE")?,
            max,
        })
    }
}
pub async fn run_redis_gateway() -> io::Result<()> {
    let _ = rustls::crypto::ring::default_provider().install_default();
    let cfg = Config::load()?;
    let path = std::env::var("PROXIMA_REDIS_IDENTITY_REGISTRY").map_err(invalid)?;
    let registry = Arc::new(RedisIdentityRegistry::load(path)?);
    let acceptor = TlsAcceptor::from(Arc::new(server_tls(&cfg.cert, &cfg.key)?));
    let connector = TlsConnector::from(Arc::new(client_tls(&cfg.ca)?));
    let listener = TcpListener::bind(cfg.listen).await?;
    let permits = Arc::new(Semaphore::new(cfg.max));
    info!(listen=%cfg.listen,upstream=%cfg.upstream,principals=registry.principal_count(),max_connections=cfg.max,"Proxima Redis RESP2 gateway listening");
    loop {
        let (socket, peer) = listener.accept().await?;
        let permit = match permits.clone().try_acquire_owned() {
            Ok(p) => p,
            Err(_) => {
                warn!(%peer,"Redis gateway connection limit reached");
                drop(socket);
                continue;
            }
        };
        let (a, c, r, u, n) = (
            acceptor.clone(),
            connector.clone(),
            registry.clone(),
            cfg.upstream.clone(),
            cfg.upstream_name.clone(),
        );
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            let _permit = permit;
            if let Err(e) = session(socket, a, c, r, &u, &n).await {
                warn!(%peer,%e,"Redis gateway session closed");
            }
        });
    }
}
async fn session(
    socket: TcpStream,
    acceptor: TlsAcceptor,
    connector: TlsConnector,
    registry: Arc<RedisIdentityRegistry>,
    upstream_addr: &str,
    server_name: &str,
) -> io::Result<()> {
    let mut client: Io = Box::new(
        timeout(Duration::from_secs(10), acceptor.accept(socket))
            .await
            .map_err(|_| io::ErrorKind::TimedOut)??,
    );
    let mut input = Vec::new();
    let mut auth_attempts = 0usize;
    let principal = loop {
        auth_attempts += 1;
        if auth_attempts > 3 {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                "Redis authentication attempt limit reached",
            ));
        }
        let args = timeout(
            Duration::from_secs(10),
            read_command(&mut *client, &mut input),
        )
        .await
        .map_err(|_| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::TimedOut, "Redis authentication timed out"))??;
        let cmd = std::str::from_utf8(args.first().ok_or_else(|| invalid("empty command"))?)
            .map_err(invalid)?
            .to_ascii_uppercase();
        if cmd != "AUTH" {
            write_error(&mut *client, "authentication required").await?;
            continue;
        }
        let (user, password) = match args.as_slice() {
            [_, p] => ("default", p.as_slice()),
            [_, u, p] => (std::str::from_utf8(u).map_err(invalid)?, p.as_slice()),
            _ => {
                write_error(&mut *client, "invalid AUTH arguments").await?;
                continue;
            }
        };
        if let Some(p) = registry.authenticate(user, password) {
            break p;
        }
        write_error(&mut *client, "invalid credentials").await?;
    };
    let tcp = timeout(Duration::from_secs(10), TcpStream::connect(upstream_addr))
        .await
        .map_err(|_| io::ErrorKind::TimedOut)??;
    let name = ServerName::try_from(server_name.to_owned()).map_err(invalid)?;
    let mut upstream: Io = Box::new(
        timeout(Duration::from_secs(10), connector.connect(name, tcp))
            .await
            .map_err(|_| io::ErrorKind::TimedOut)??,
    );
    let auth = vec![
        b"AUTH".to_vec(),
        principal.upstream_user.as_bytes().to_vec(),
        principal.upstream_password.as_bytes().to_vec(),
    ];
    upstream.write_all(&encode_command(&auth)).await?;
    upstream.flush().await?;
    let reply = timeout(Duration::from_secs(30), read_resp_frame(&mut *upstream, 0))
        .await
        .map_err(|_| {
            io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::TimedOut, "Redis upstream response timed out")
        })??;
    if !reply.starts_with(b"+OK\r\n") {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
            "upstream Redis ACL authentication failed",
        ));
    }
    client.write_all(b"+OK\r\n").await?;
    client.flush().await?;
    loop {
        let mut args = match timeout(
            Duration::from_secs(60),
            read_command(&mut *client, &mut input),
        )
        .await
        {
            Ok(Ok(v)) => v,
            Ok(Err(e)) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::UnexpectedEof => return Ok(()),
            Ok(Err(e)) => return Err(e),
            Err(_) => {
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::TimedOut,
                    "Redis client idle timeout",
                ))
            }
        };
        let cmd = std::str::from_utf8(&args[0])
            .map_err(invalid)?
            .to_ascii_uppercase();
        match cmd.as_str() {
            "PING" => {
                if args.len() > 2 {
                    write_error(&mut *client, "wrong number of arguments").await?;
                } else if args.len() == 2 {
                    let mut out = b"$".to_vec();
                    out.extend_from_slice(args[1].len().to_string().as_bytes());
                    out.extend_from_slice(b"\r\n");
                    out.extend_from_slice(&args[1]);
                    out.extend_from_slice(b"\r\n");
                    client.write_all(&out).await?;
                } else {
                    client.write_all(b"+PONG\r\n").await?;
                }
                client.flush().await?;
                continue;
            }
            "QUIT" => {
                client.write_all(b"+OK\r\n").await?;
                client.flush().await?;
                return Ok(());
            }
            "CLIENT"
                if args.len() == 4
                    && args[1].eq_ignore_ascii_case(b"SETINFO")
                    && (args[2].eq_ignore_ascii_case(b"LIB-NAME")
                        || args[2].eq_ignore_ascii_case(b"LIB-VER")) =>
            {
                client.write_all(b"+OK\r\n").await?;
                client.flush().await?;
                continue;
            }
            _ => {}
        }
        if rewrite_keys(&mut args, &principal.tenant_id).is_err() {
            write_error(&mut *client, "command is not in the tenant-safe allowlist").await?;
            continue;
        }
        let frame = encode_command(&args);
        if frame.len() > MAX_FRAME_BYTES {
            return Err(invalid("rewritten Redis frame exceeds limit"));
        }
        timeout(Duration::from_secs(10), upstream.write_all(&frame))
            .await
            .map_err(|_| {
                io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::TimedOut, "Redis upstream write timed out")
            })??;
        upstream.flush().await?;
        let reply = timeout(Duration::from_secs(30), read_resp_frame(&mut *upstream, 0))
            .await
            .map_err(|_| {
                io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::TimedOut, "Redis upstream response timed out")
            })??;
        client.write_all(&reply).await?;
        client.flush().await?;
    }
}
async fn read_command<R: AsyncRead + Unpin + ?Sized>(
    r: &mut R,
    buf: &mut Vec<u8>,
) -> io::Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>> {
    loop {
        match parse_command(buf) {
            Ok(Some((args, n))) => {
                buf.drain(..n);
                return Ok(args);
            }
            Ok(None) => {}
            Err(e) => return Err(invalid(format!("invalid RESP command: {e:?}"))),
        }
        if buf.len() >= MAX_FRAME_BYTES {
            return Err(invalid("RESP request exceeds frame limit"));
        }
        let mut chunk = [0u8; 8192];
        let n = r.read(&mut chunk).await?;
        if n == 0 {
            return Err(io::ErrorKind::UnexpectedEof.into());
        }
        buf.extend_from_slice(&chunk[..n]);
    }
}
async fn line<R: AsyncRead + Unpin + ?Sized>(r: &mut R) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut v = Vec::new();
    loop {
        let b = r.read_u8().await?;
        v.push(b);
        if v.len() > 128 {
            return Err(invalid("RESP line too long"));
        }
        if v.ends_with(b"\r\n") {
            v.truncate(v.len() - 2);
            return Ok(v);
        }
    }
}
fn read_resp_frame<'a, R: AsyncRead + Unpin + Send + ?Sized>(
    r: &'a mut R,
    depth: usize,
) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = io::Result<Vec<u8>>> + Send + 'a>> {
    Box::pin(async move {
        if depth > 32 {
            return Err(invalid("RESP nesting limit exceeded"));
        }
        let ty = r.read_u8().await?;
        let mut out = vec![ty];
        match ty {
            b'+' | b'-' | b':' => {
                let l = line(r).await?;
                out.extend_from_slice(&l);
                out.extend_from_slice(b"\r\n");
            }
            b'$' => {
                let l = line(r).await?;
                let n = std::str::from_utf8(&l)
                    .map_err(invalid)?
                    .parse::<i64>()
                    .map_err(invalid)?;
                out.extend_from_slice(&l);
                out.extend_from_slice(b"\r\n");
                if n >= 0 {
                    if n as usize > MAX_FRAME_BYTES {
                        return Err(invalid("RESP bulk reply too large"));
                    }
                    let mut b = vec![0; (n as usize) + 2];
                    r.read_exact(&mut b).await?;
                    if !b.ends_with(b"\r\n") {
                        return Err(invalid("invalid RESP bulk terminator"));
                    }
                    out.extend_from_slice(&b);
                }
            }
            b'*' => {
                let l = line(r).await?;
                let n = std::str::from_utf8(&l)
                    .map_err(invalid)?
                    .parse::<i64>()
                    .map_err(invalid)?;
                out.extend_from_slice(&l);
                out.extend_from_slice(b"\r\n");
                if n >= 0 {
                    if n > 1024 {
                        return Err(invalid("RESP array too large"));
                    }
                    for _ in 0..n {
                        let child = read_resp_frame(r, depth + 1).await?;
                        out.extend_from_slice(&child);
                        if out.len() > MAX_FRAME_BYTES {
                            return Err(invalid("RESP reply too large"));
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            _ => {
                return Err(invalid(
                    "upstream returned unsupported RESP3 or invalid frame",
                ))
            }
        }
        if out.len() > MAX_FRAME_BYTES {
            return Err(invalid("RESP reply too large"));
        }
        Ok(out)
    })
}
async fn write_error<W: AsyncWrite + Unpin + ?Sized>(w: &mut W, msg: &str) -> io::Result<()> {
    let safe = msg.replace('\r', " ").replace('\n', " ");
    w.write_all(format!("-ERR {safe}\r\n").as_bytes()).await?;
    w.flush().await
}
fn server_tls(cert: &str, key: &str) -> io::Result<ServerConfig> {
    let certs = rustls_pemfile::certs(&mut BufReader::new(File::open(cert)?))
        .collect::<Result<Vec<CertificateDer<'static>>, _>>()
        .map_err(invalid)?;
    let key = rustls_pemfile::private_key(&mut BufReader::new(File::open(key)?))
        .map_err(invalid)?
        .ok_or_else(|| invalid("TLS private key missing"))?;
    ServerConfig::builder()
        .with_no_client_auth()
        .with_single_cert(certs, key)
        .map_err(invalid)
}
fn client_tls(ca: &str) -> io::Result<ClientConfig> {
    let mut roots = RootCertStore::empty();
    let certs = rustls_pemfile::certs(&mut BufReader::new(File::open(ca)?))
        .collect::<Result<Vec<CertificateDer<'static>>, _>>()
        .map_err(invalid)?;
    for cert in certs {
        roots.add(cert).map_err(invalid)?;
    }
    Ok(ClientConfig::builder()
        .with_root_certificates(roots)
        .with_no_client_auth())
}

========== crates/proxima-engine/src/redis_protocol.rs ==========
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
pub const MAX_FRAME_BYTES: usize = 1_048_576;
pub const MAX_ARGS: usize = 1024;
pub const MAX_BULK_BYTES: usize = 524_288;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum RespError {
    Malformed,
    LimitExceeded,
    UnsupportedCommand,
    InvalidTenant,
}

pub fn parse_command(input: &[u8]) -> Result<Option<(Vec<Vec<u8>>, usize)>, RespError> {
    if input.len() > MAX_FRAME_BYTES {
        return Err(RespError::LimitExceeded);
    }
    if input.is_empty() {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    if input[0] != b'*' {
        return Err(RespError::Malformed);
    }
    let (count, mut pos) = match decimal(input, 1)? {
        Some(v) => v,
        None => return Ok(None),
    };
    if count == 0 || count > MAX_ARGS {
        return Err(RespError::LimitExceeded);
    }
    let mut args = Vec::with_capacity(count);
    for _ in 0..count {
        if pos >= input.len() {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        if input[pos] != b'$' {
            return Err(RespError::Malformed);
        }
        let (len, start) = match decimal(input, pos + 1)? {
            Some(v) => v,
            None => return Ok(None),
        };
        if len > MAX_BULK_BYTES {
            return Err(RespError::LimitExceeded);
        }
        let end = start.checked_add(len).ok_or(RespError::LimitExceeded)?;
        let next = end.checked_add(2).ok_or(RespError::LimitExceeded)?;
        if next > input.len() {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        if &input[end..next] != b"\r\n" {
            return Err(RespError::Malformed);
        }
        args.push(input[start..end].to_vec());
        pos = next;
    }
    Ok(Some((args, pos)))
}
fn decimal(input: &[u8], start: usize) -> Result<Option<(usize, usize)>, RespError> {
    let Some(n) = input
        .get(start..)
        .and_then(|s| s.windows(2).position(|w| w == b"\r\n"))
    else {
        return Ok(None);
    };
    let end = start + n;
    let digits = &input[start..end];
    if digits.is_empty() || !digits.iter().all(u8::is_ascii_digit) {
        return Err(RespError::Malformed);
    }
    let value = std::str::from_utf8(digits)
        .map_err(|_| RespError::Malformed)?
        .parse::<usize>()
        .map_err(|_| RespError::LimitExceeded)?;
    Ok(Some((value, end + 2)))
}
pub fn tenant_prefix(tenant_id: &str) -> Result<Vec<u8>, RespError> {
    if tenant_id.trim().is_empty() || tenant_id.len() > 256 {
        return Err(RespError::InvalidTenant);
    }
    let digest = Sha256::digest(tenant_id.as_bytes());
    Ok(format!("proxima:{{{}}}:", hex::encode(&digest[..16])).into_bytes())
}
pub fn rewrite_keys(args: &mut [Vec<u8>], tenant_id: &str) -> Result<(), RespError> {
    if args.is_empty() {
        return Err(RespError::Malformed);
    }
    let cmd = std::str::from_utf8(&args[0])
        .map_err(|_| RespError::UnsupportedCommand)?
        .to_ascii_uppercase();
    let keys: Vec<usize> = match cmd.as_str() {
        "GET" | "SET" | "SETNX" | "GETSET" | "GETDEL" | "APPEND" | "STRLEN" | "INCR" | "DECR"
        | "INCRBY" | "DECRBY" | "EXPIRE" | "PEXPIRE" | "TTL" | "PTTL" | "PERSIST" | "TYPE"
        | "HGET" | "HSET" | "HSETNX" | "HMGET" | "HMSET" | "HDEL" | "HEXISTS" | "HLEN"
        | "HSTRLEN" | "HGETALL" | "LPUSH" | "RPUSH" | "LPOP" | "RPOP" | "LLEN" | "LRANGE"
        | "LINDEX" | "LSET" | "LREM" | "LTRIM" | "SADD" | "SREM" | "SISMEMBER" | "SMEMBERS"
        | "SCARD" | "SPOP" | "ZADD" | "ZREM" | "ZSCORE" | "ZRANGE" | "ZCARD" | "ZCOUNT"
        | "ZINCRBY" | "PFADD" | "GEOADD" | "XRANGE" | "XREVRANGE" | "XLEN" | "XADD" | "XDEL"
        | "XTRIM" | "XACK" => vec![1],
        "MGET" | "DEL" | "UNLINK" | "EXISTS" | "TOUCH" => {
            if args.len() < 2 {
                return Err(RespError::Malformed);
            }
            (1..args.len()).collect()
        }
        "MSET" | "MSETNX" => {
            if args.len() < 3 || args.len() % 2 == 0 {
                return Err(RespError::Malformed);
            }
            (1..args.len()).step_by(2).collect()
        }
        "RENAME" | "RENAMENX" | "RPOPLPUSH" | "SMOVE" | "COPY" => {
            if args.len() < 3 {
                return Err(RespError::Malformed);
            }
            vec![1, 2]
        }
        "BITOP" => {
            if args.len() < 4 {
                return Err(RespError::Malformed);
            }
            (2..args.len()).collect()
        }
        _ => return Err(RespError::UnsupportedCommand),
    };
    let prefix = tenant_prefix(tenant_id)?;
    for i in keys {
        let key = args.get_mut(i).ok_or(RespError::Malformed)?;
        let mut v = prefix.clone();
        v.extend_from_slice(key);
        *key = v;
    }
    Ok(())
}
pub fn encode_command(args: &[Vec<u8>]) -> Vec<u8> {
    let mut out = format!("*{}\r\n", args.len()).into_bytes();
    for arg in args {
        out.extend_from_slice(b"$");
        out.extend_from_slice(format!("{}\r\n", arg.len()).as_bytes());
        out.extend_from_slice(arg);
        out.extend_from_slice(b"\r\n");
    }
    out
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn frame(a: &[&[u8]]) -> Vec<u8> {
        encode_command(&a.iter().map(|v| v.to_vec()).collect::<Vec<_>>())
    }
    #[test]
    fn parses_binary_and_pipeline() {
        let one = frame(&[b"SET", b"k", b"a\0b"]);
        let mut data = one.clone();
        data.extend(frame(&[b"GET", b"k"]));
        let (a, n) = parse_command(&data).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(a[2], b"a\0b");
        assert_eq!(n, one.len());
        assert_eq!(parse_command(&data[n..]).unwrap().unwrap().0[0], b"GET");
    }
    #[test]
    fn incomplete_is_not_a_command() {
        assert_eq!(parse_command(b"*2\r\n$3\r\nGET\r\n$4\r\nk"), Ok(None));
    }
    #[test]
    fn tenant_prefix_is_opaque() {
        assert_ne!(tenant_prefix("a").unwrap(), tenant_prefix("b").unwrap());
        assert_eq!(tenant_prefix("a").unwrap(), tenant_prefix("a").unwrap());
    }
    #[test]
    fn mget_and_mset_namespace_keys_only() {
        let mut a = vec![b"MGET".to_vec(), b"a".to_vec(), b"b".to_vec()];
        rewrite_keys(&mut a, "t").unwrap();
        let p = tenant_prefix("t").unwrap();
        assert!(a[1].starts_with(&p) && a[2].starts_with(&p));
        let mut b = vec![b"MSET".to_vec(), b"k".to_vec(), b"v".to_vec()];
        rewrite_keys(&mut b, "t").unwrap();
        assert!(b[1].starts_with(&p));
        assert_eq!(b[2], b"v");
    }
    #[test]
    fn bitop_rewrites_destination_and_sources_but_not_operation() {
        let mut a = vec![
            b"BITOP".to_vec(),
            b"AND".to_vec(),
            b"dest".to_vec(),
            b"left".to_vec(),
            b"right".to_vec(),
        ];
        rewrite_keys(&mut a, "t").unwrap();
        let p = tenant_prefix("t").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(a[1], b"AND");
        assert!(a[2].starts_with(&p) && a[3].starts_with(&p) && a[4].starts_with(&p));
    }
    #[test]
    fn rejects_empty_and_malformed_command_shapes() {
        for mut a in [
            vec![b"GET".to_vec()],
            vec![b"MSET".to_vec(), b"k".to_vec()],
            vec![b"BITOP".to_vec(), b"AND".to_vec(), b"d".to_vec()],
        ] {
            assert_eq!(rewrite_keys(&mut a, "t"), Err(RespError::Malformed));
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn dangerous_commands_are_denied() {
        for c in [
            "FLUSHALL",
            "CONFIG",
            "ACL",
            "EVAL",
            "SUBSCRIBE",
            "PUBLISH",
            "MONITOR",
            "SELECT",
            "MODULE",
        ] {
            let mut a = vec![c.as_bytes().to_vec(), b"x".to_vec()];
            assert_eq!(
                rewrite_keys(&mut a, "t"),
                Err(RespError::UnsupportedCommand)
            );
        }
    }
}

========== crates/proxima-engine/src/mysql_gateway.rs ==========
use crate::mysql_identity::MysqlIdentityRegistry;
use crate::mysql_policy::classify_statement;
use crate::mysql_session::MysqlConnectionIdentity;
use crate::mysql_upstream::MysqlUpstream;

use futures_util::StreamExt;
use mysql_async::prelude::Queryable;
use mysql_async::{Conn, Row, Statement, Value as UpstreamValue};

use opensrv_mysql::{
    AsyncMysqlIntermediary, AsyncMysqlShim, Column, ColumnFlags, ColumnType, ErrorKind, InitWriter,
    IntermediaryOptions, OkResponse, ParamParser, QueryResultWriter, StatementMetaWriter,
    StatusFlags, ToMysqlValue, ValueInner,
};

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::io::{self, Write};
use std::net::SocketAddr;
use std::panic::{catch_unwind, AssertUnwindSafe};
use std::sync::Arc;

use tokio::net::TcpListener;
use tokio::sync::{Mutex, Semaphore};
use tokio::task;
use uuid::Uuid;

const SERVER_VERSION: &str = "8.0.0-agata-proxima";
const UPSTREAM_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_SECS: u64 = 10;
const QUERY_TIMEOUT_SECS: u64 = 30;
const DEFAULT_MAX_CONNECTIONS: usize = 128;

/// One upstream connection and its prepared statements belong to one
/// authenticated client session. The pool is retained for the lifetime
/// of the session; the checked-out connection is never returned to it.
struct MysqlUpstreamSession {
    _pool: MysqlUpstream,
    connection: Conn,
    statements: HashMap<u32, Statement>,
}

impl MysqlUpstreamSession {
    fn new(pool: MysqlUpstream, connection: Conn) -> Self {
        Self {
            _pool: pool,
            connection,
            statements: HashMap::new(),
        }
    }
}

/// One gateway session represents exactly one client connection.
///
/// The authenticated principal is immutable. The upstream connection
/// is established only after successful authentication and remains
/// bound to that principal for the session lifetime.
struct MysqlGatewaySession {
    registry: Arc<MysqlIdentityRegistry>,
    identity: MysqlConnectionIdentity,
    upstream: Mutex<Option<MysqlUpstreamSession>>,
    salt: [u8; 20],
}

impl MysqlGatewaySession {
    fn new(registry: Arc<MysqlIdentityRegistry>) -> Self {
        Self {
            registry,
            identity: MysqlConnectionIdentity::new(),
            upstream: Mutex::new(None),
            salt: {
                // Use raw UUID bytes rather than hexadecimal text so the
                // 20-byte MySQL nonce retains 160 bits of random material.
                let first = Uuid::new_v4();
                let second = Uuid::new_v4();
                let mut salt = [0u8; 20];
