                salt[..16].copy_from_slice(first.as_bytes());
                salt[16..].copy_from_slice(&second.as_bytes()[..4]);
                salt
            },
        }
    }

    fn authenticated(&self) -> bool {
        self.identity.is_authenticated()
    }

    async fn upstream_ready(&self) -> bool {
        self.upstream.lock().await.is_some()
    }
}

/// Convert a protocol parameter into an owned mysql_async value.
///
/// Date/time binary parameter payloads are decoded according to the MySQL
/// binary protocol and rejected when their lengths or component ranges are invalid.
fn decode_date_payload(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<(u16, u8, u8), ()> {
    if bytes.is_empty() {
        return Ok((0, 0, 0));
    }
    if bytes.len() != 4 {
        return Err(());
    }
    let year = u16::from_le_bytes([bytes[0], bytes[1]]);
    let (month, day) = (bytes[2], bytes[3]);
    if month > 12 || day > 31 {
        return Err(());
    }
    Ok((year, month, day))
}

fn decode_datetime_payload(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<(u16, u8, u8, u8, u8, u8, u32), ()> {
    if bytes.is_empty() {
        return Ok((0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0));
    }
    if !matches!(bytes.len(), 4 | 7 | 11) {
        return Err(());
    }
    let (year, month, day) = decode_date_payload(&bytes[..4])?;
    let (hour, minute, second) = if bytes.len() >= 7 {
        (bytes[4], bytes[5], bytes[6])
    } else {
        (0, 0, 0)
    };
    let micros = if bytes.len() == 11 {
        u32::from_le_bytes(bytes[7..11].try_into().map_err(|_| ())?)
    } else {
        0
    };
    if hour > 23 || minute > 59 || second > 59 || micros > 999_999 {
        return Err(());
    }
    Ok((year, month, day, hour, minute, second, micros))
}

fn decode_time_payload(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<(bool, u32, u8, u8, u8, u32), ()> {
    if bytes.is_empty() {
        return Ok((false, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0));
    }
    if !matches!(bytes.len(), 8 | 12) || bytes[0] > 1 {
        return Err(());
    }
    let days = u32::from_le_bytes(bytes[1..5].try_into().map_err(|_| ())?);
    let (hours, minutes, seconds) = (bytes[5], bytes[6], bytes[7]);
    let micros = if bytes.len() == 12 {
        u32::from_le_bytes(bytes[8..12].try_into().map_err(|_| ())?)
    } else {
        0
    };
    if hours > 23 || minutes > 59 || seconds > 59 || micros > 999_999 {
        return Err(());
    }
    Ok((bytes[0] == 1, days, hours, minutes, seconds, micros))
}

fn convert_parameter(value: ValueInner<'_>) -> Result<UpstreamValue, ()> {
    match value {
        ValueInner::NULL => Ok(UpstreamValue::NULL),
        ValueInner::Bytes(bytes) => Ok(UpstreamValue::Bytes(bytes.to_vec())),
        ValueInner::Int(value) => Ok(UpstreamValue::Int(value)),
        ValueInner::UInt(value) => Ok(UpstreamValue::UInt(value)),
        ValueInner::Double(value) => Ok(UpstreamValue::Double(value)),
        ValueInner::Date(bytes) => {
            let (year, month, day) = decode_date_payload(bytes)?;
            Ok(UpstreamValue::Date(year, month, day, 0, 0, 0, 0))
        }
        ValueInner::Datetime(bytes) => {
            let (year, month, day, hour, minute, second, micros) = decode_datetime_payload(bytes)?;
            Ok(UpstreamValue::Date(
                year, month, day, hour, minute, second, micros,
            ))
        }
        ValueInner::Time(bytes) => {
            let (negative, days, hours, minutes, seconds, micros) = decode_time_payload(bytes)?;
            Ok(UpstreamValue::Time(
                negative, days, hours, minutes, seconds, micros,
            ))
        }
    }
}

/// opensrv-mysql 0.7.0's ParamParser contains unchecked parsing paths.
/// Catch parser panics at this protocol boundary so malformed client
/// packets cannot unwind through the gateway session task.
fn decode_parameters(params: ParamParser<'_>) -> Result<Vec<UpstreamValue>, ()> {
    catch_unwind(AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
        params
            .into_iter()
            .map(|parameter| convert_parameter(parameter.value.into_inner()))
            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
    }))
    .unwrap_or(Err(()))
}

/// A typed row value that implements opensrv-mysql's output encoder.
/// This avoids converting BLOBs to UTF-8 or hex text and emits the proper
/// MySQL binary representation for temporal values in prepared results.
#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
enum MysqlOutputValue {
    Bytes(Vec<u8>),
    Int(i64),
    UInt(u64),
    Float(f32),
    Double(f64),
    Date(u16, u8, u8, u8, u8, u8, u32),
    Time(bool, u32, u8, u8, u8, u32),
}

impl ToMysqlValue for MysqlOutputValue {
    fn to_mysql_text<W: Write>(&self, writer: &mut W) -> io::Result<()> {
        match self {
            Self::Bytes(value) => value.to_mysql_text(writer),
            Self::Int(value) => value.to_mysql_text(writer),
            Self::UInt(value) => value.to_mysql_text(writer),
            Self::Float(value) => value.to_mysql_text(writer),
            Self::Double(value) => value.to_mysql_text(writer),
            Self::Date(y, m, d, h, min, sec, micros) => {
                let text = if *micros == 0 {
                    format!("{y:04}-{m:02}-{d:02} {h:02}:{min:02}:{sec:02}")
                } else {
                    format!("{y:04}-{m:02}-{d:02} {h:02}:{min:02}:{sec:02}.{micros:06}")
                };
                writer.write_all(text.as_bytes())
            }
            Self::Time(negative, days, hours, minutes, seconds, micros) => {
                let total_hours = u64::from(*days) * 24 + u64::from(*hours);
                let sign = if *negative { "-" } else { "" };
                let text = if *micros == 0 {
                    format!("{sign}{total_hours:02}:{minutes:02}:{seconds:02}")
                } else {
                    format!("{sign}{total_hours:02}:{minutes:02}:{seconds:02}.{micros:06}")
                };
                writer.write_all(text.as_bytes())
            }
        }
    }

    fn to_mysql_bin<W: Write>(&self, writer: &mut W, column: &Column) -> io::Result<()> {
        match self {
            Self::Bytes(value) => value.to_mysql_bin(writer, column),
            Self::Int(value) => value.to_mysql_bin(writer, column),
            Self::UInt(value) => value.to_mysql_bin(writer, column),
            Self::Float(value) => value.to_mysql_bin(writer, column),
            Self::Double(value) => value.to_mysql_bin(writer, column),
            Self::Date(year, month, day, hour, minute, second, micros) => match column.coltype {
                ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_DATE | ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_NEWDATE => {
                    writer.write_all(&[4])?;
                    writer.write_all(&year.to_le_bytes())?;
                    writer.write_all(&[*month, *day])
                }
                ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_DATETIME
                | ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_TIMESTAMP
                | ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_DATETIME2
                | ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_TIMESTAMP2 => {
                    let len = if *micros != 0 {
                        11
                    } else if *hour != 0 || *minute != 0 || *second != 0 {
                        7
                    } else {
                        4
                    };
                    writer.write_all(&[len])?;
                    writer.write_all(&year.to_le_bytes())?;
                    writer.write_all(&[*month, *day])?;
                    if len >= 7 {
                        writer.write_all(&[*hour, *minute, *second])?;
                    }
                    if len == 11 {
                        writer.write_all(&micros.to_le_bytes())?;
                    }
                    Ok(())
                }
                _ => Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                    "temporal value does not match result column type",
                )),
            },
            Self::Time(negative, days, hours, minutes, seconds, micros) => {
                if !matches!(
                    column.coltype,
                    ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_TIME | ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_TIME2
                ) {
                    return Err(io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                        "time value does not match result column type",
                    ));
                }
                let len = if *days == 0
                    && *hours == 0
                    && *minutes == 0
                    && *seconds == 0
                    && *micros == 0
                {
                    0
                } else if *micros == 0 {
                    8
                } else {
                    12
                };
                writer.write_all(&[len])?;
                if len == 0 {
                    return Ok(());
                }
                writer.write_all(&[u8::from(*negative)])?;
                writer.write_all(&days.to_le_bytes())?;
                writer.write_all(&[*hours, *minutes, *seconds])?;
                if len == 12 {
                    writer.write_all(&micros.to_le_bytes())?;
                }
                Ok(())
            }
        }
    }
}

fn upstream_value_to_output(value: UpstreamValue) -> Option<MysqlOutputValue> {
    Some(match value {
        UpstreamValue::NULL => return None,
        UpstreamValue::Bytes(bytes) => MysqlOutputValue::Bytes(bytes),
        UpstreamValue::Int(value) => MysqlOutputValue::Int(value),
        UpstreamValue::UInt(value) => MysqlOutputValue::UInt(value),
        UpstreamValue::Float(value) => MysqlOutputValue::Float(value),
        UpstreamValue::Double(value) => MysqlOutputValue::Double(value),
        UpstreamValue::Date(year, month, day, hour, minute, second, micros) => {
            MysqlOutputValue::Date(year, month, day, hour, minute, second, micros)
        }
        UpstreamValue::Time(negative, days, hours, minutes, seconds, micros) => {
            MysqlOutputValue::Time(negative, days, hours, minutes, seconds, micros)
        }
    })
}

/// Preserve the upstream MySQL protocol type code. Both driver and server
/// enums follow the MySQL wire-protocol numeric assignments; unknown future
/// types fall back to VAR_STRING rather than emitting an invalid type code.
fn output_column_type(column_type: mysql_async::consts::ColumnType) -> ColumnType {
    ColumnType::try_from(column_type as u8).unwrap_or(ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_VAR_STRING)
}

fn output_column(column: &mysql_async::Column) -> Column {
    Column {
        table: column.table_str().into_owned(),
        column: column.name_str().into_owned(),
        coltype: output_column_type(column.column_type()),
        colflags: ColumnFlags::from_bits_truncate(column.flags().bits()),
    }
}

#[async_trait::async_trait]
impl<W> AsyncMysqlShim<W> for MysqlGatewaySession
where
    W: tokio::io::AsyncWrite + Send + Unpin,
{
    type Error = io::Error;

    fn version(&self) -> String {
        SERVER_VERSION.to_string()
    }

    fn connect_id(&self) -> u32 {
        let uuid = Uuid::new_v4();
        let bytes = uuid.as_bytes();
        u32::from_le_bytes([bytes[0], bytes[1], bytes[2], bytes[3]])
    }

    fn default_auth_plugin(&self) -> &str {
        "caching_sha2_password"
    }

    async fn auth_plugin_for_username(&self, user: &[u8]) -> &str {
        self.registry.auth_plugin_for_username(user)
    }

    fn salt(&self) -> [u8; 20] {
        self.salt
    }

    async fn authenticate(
        &self,
        auth_plugin: &str,
        username: &[u8],
        salt: &[u8],
        auth_data: &[u8],
    ) -> bool {
        if self.authenticated() || self.upstream_ready().await {
            return false;
        }

        let Some(principal) = self
            .registry
            .authenticate(auth_plugin, username, salt, auth_data)
        else {
            return false;
        };

        // The registry entry selects the only upstream identity this
        // client session is permitted to use.
        let pool = match MysqlUpstream::from_url(&principal.upstream_url) {
            Ok(pool) => pool,
            Err(_) => return false,
        };

        // Establish the real upstream connection before accepting
        // authentication. All later work uses this exact connection.
        let mut connection = match tokio::time::timeout(
            std::time::Duration::from_secs(UPSTREAM_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_SECS),
            pool.get_connection(),
        )
        .await
        {
            Ok(Ok(connection)) => connection,
            _ => return false,
        };

        // Check the database selected by the live upstream session as a second
        // guard against a registry/driver configuration mismatch.
        let selected_database: Option<String> = match tokio::time::timeout(
            std::time::Duration::from_secs(UPSTREAM_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_SECS),
            connection.query_first("SELECT DATABASE()"),
        )
        .await
        {
            Ok(Ok(database)) => database,
            _ => return false,
        };
        if selected_database.as_deref() != Some(principal.database.as_str()) {
            tracing::warn!(
                username = %principal.username,
                expected_database = %principal.database,
                selected_database = ?selected_database,
                "MySQL upstream database did not match the tenant registry"
            );
            return false;
        }

        if self.identity.bind_authenticated(principal).is_err() {
            return false;
        }

        let mut guard = self.upstream.lock().await;

        if guard.is_some() {
            return false;
        }

        *guard = Some(MysqlUpstreamSession::new(pool, connection));

        true
    }

    async fn on_prepare<'a>(
        &'a mut self,
        query: &'a str,
        info: StatementMetaWriter<'a, W>,
    ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
        if !self.authenticated() {
            return info
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_ACCESS_DENIED_ERROR,
                    &b"authentication required"[..],
                )
                .await;
        }

        if classify_statement(query).is_err() {
            return info
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_NOT_SUPPORTED_YET,
                    &b"prepared statement rejected by Proxima SQL admission policy"[..],
                )
                .await;
        }

        let mut guard = self.upstream.lock().await;
        let Some(session) = guard.as_mut() else {
            return info
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                    &b"tenant upstream connection unavailable"[..],
                )
                .await;
        };

        let statement = match tokio::time::timeout(
            std::time::Duration::from_secs(QUERY_TIMEOUT_SECS),
            session.connection.prep(query),
        )
        .await
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| Err(mysql_async::DriverError::ConnectionClosed.into()))
        {
            Ok(statement) => statement,
            Err(_) => {
                return info
                    .error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                        &b"upstream statement preparation failed"[..],
                    )
                    .await;
            }
        };

        let client_statement_id = statement.id();

        if session.statements.contains_key(&client_statement_id) {
            let _ = session.connection.close(statement).await;
            return info
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                    &b"prepared statement identifier collision"[..],
                )
                .await;
        }

        let parameter_columns: Vec<Column> = statement.params().iter().map(output_column).collect();

        let result_metadata = statement.columns();

        let result_columns: Vec<Column> = result_metadata.iter().map(output_column).collect();

        session.statements.insert(client_statement_id, statement);

        let reply_result = info
            .reply(
                client_statement_id,
                parameter_columns.iter(),
                result_columns.iter(),
            )
            .await;

        if reply_result.is_err() {
            if let Some(statement) = session.statements.remove(&client_statement_id) {
                let _ = session.connection.close(statement).await;
            }
        }

        reply_result
    }

    async fn on_execute<'a>(
        &'a mut self,
        id: u32,
        params: ParamParser<'a>,
        results: QueryResultWriter<'a, W>,
    ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
        if !self.authenticated() {
            return results
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_ACCESS_DENIED_ERROR,
                    &b"authentication required"[..],
                )
                .await;
        }

        let values = match decode_parameters(params) {
            Ok(values) => values,
            Err(()) => {
                return results
                    .error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_WRONG_ARGUMENTS,
                        &b"invalid or unsupported prepared statement parameters"[..],
                    )
                    .await;
            }
        };

        let mut guard = self.upstream.lock().await;
        let Some(session) = guard.as_mut() else {
            return results
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                    &b"tenant upstream connection unavailable"[..],
                )
                .await;
        };

        let Some(statement) = session.statements.get(&id) else {
            return results
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_STMT_HANDLER,
                    &b"unknown prepared statement for this tenant session"[..],
                )
                .await;
        };

        if values.len() != statement.num_params() as usize {
            return results
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_WRONG_ARGUMENTS,
                    &b"prepared statement parameter count mismatch"[..],
                )
                .await;
        }

        let mut upstream_result = match tokio::time::timeout(
            std::time::Duration::from_secs(QUERY_TIMEOUT_SECS),
            session.connection.exec_iter(statement, values),
        )
        .await
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| Err(mysql_async::DriverError::ConnectionClosed.into()))
        {
            Ok(result) => result,
            Err(error) => {
                tracing::warn!(
                    error = %error,
                    "MySQL upstream prepared statement execution failed"
                );

                return results
                    .error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                        &b"upstream prepared statement execution failed"[..],
                    )
                    .await;
            }
        };

        let upstream_columns = upstream_result.columns_ref();

        if upstream_columns.is_empty() {
            let affected_rows = upstream_result.affected_rows();
            let last_insert_id = upstream_result.last_insert_id().unwrap_or(0);
            let warnings = upstream_result.warnings();

            if upstream_result.drop_result().await.is_err() {
                return results
                    .error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                        &b"upstream result completion failed"[..],
                    )
                    .await;
            }

            return results
                .completed(OkResponse {
                    header: 0x00,
                    affected_rows,
                    last_insert_id,
                    status_flags: StatusFlags::empty(),
                    warnings,
                    info: String::new(),
                    session_state_info: String::new(),
                })
                .await;
        }

        let output_columns: Vec<Column> = upstream_columns.iter().map(output_column).collect();

        let mut output = results.start(&output_columns).await?;

        let mut rows = match upstream_result.stream::<Row>().await {
            Ok(Some(rows)) => rows,
            Ok(None) => return output.finish().await,
            Err(_) => {
                return output
                    .finish_error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                        &b"upstream prepared result stream failed".to_vec(),
                    )
                    .await;
            }
        };

        while let Some(row_result) = match tokio::time::timeout(
            std::time::Duration::from_secs(QUERY_TIMEOUT_SECS),
            rows.next(),
        )
        .await
        {
            Ok(next) => next,
            Err(_) => {
                return output
                    .finish_error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                        &b"upstream row stream timed out".to_vec(),
                    )
                    .await
            }
        } {
            let row = match row_result {
                Ok(row) => row,
                Err(_) => {
                    return output
                        .finish_error(
                            ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                            &b"upstream row retrieval failed".to_vec(),
                        )
                        .await;
                }
            };

            let values: Vec<Option<MysqlOutputValue>> = row
                .unwrap()
                .into_iter()
                .map(upstream_value_to_output)
                .collect();

            output.write_row(values).await?;
        }

        output.finish().await
    }

    async fn on_close<'a>(&'a mut self, stmt: u32)
    where
        W: 'async_trait,
    {
        let mut guard = self.upstream.lock().await;

        if let Some(session) = guard.as_mut() {
            if let Some(statement) = session.statements.remove(&stmt) {
                let _ = session.connection.close(statement).await;
            }
        }
    }

    async fn on_init<'a>(
        &'a mut self,
        database: &'a str,
        writer: InitWriter<'a, W>,
    ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
        match self.identity.authorize_database(database) {
            Ok(()) => writer.ok().await,
            Err(_) => {
                writer
                    .error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_DBACCESS_DENIED_ERROR,
                        &b"database access denied by Proxima tenant policy"[..],
                    )
                    .await
            }
        }
    }

    async fn on_query<'a>(
        &'a mut self,
        query: &'a str,
        results: QueryResultWriter<'a, W>,
    ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
        if !self.authenticated() {
            return results
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_ACCESS_DENIED_ERROR,
                    &b"authentication required"[..],
                )
                .await;
        }

        if classify_statement(query).is_err() {
            return results
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_NOT_SUPPORTED_YET,
                    &b"statement rejected by Proxima SQL admission policy"[..],
                )
                .await;
        }

        let mut guard = self.upstream.lock().await;
        let Some(session) = guard.as_mut() else {
            return results
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                    &b"tenant upstream connection unavailable"[..],
                )
                .await;
        };

        let mut upstream_result = match tokio::time::timeout(
            std::time::Duration::from_secs(QUERY_TIMEOUT_SECS),
            session.connection.query_iter(query),
        )
        .await
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| Err(mysql_async::DriverError::ConnectionClosed.into()))
        {
            Ok(result) => result,
            Err(_) => {
                return results
                    .error(ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR, &b"upstream query failed"[..])
                    .await;
            }
        };

        let upstream_columns = upstream_result.columns_ref();

        if upstream_columns.is_empty() {
            let affected_rows = upstream_result.affected_rows();
            let last_insert_id = upstream_result.last_insert_id().unwrap_or(0);
            let warnings = upstream_result.warnings();

            if upstream_result.drop_result().await.is_err() {
                return results
                    .error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                        &b"upstream result completion failed"[..],
                    )
                    .await;
            }

            return results
                .completed(OkResponse {
                    header: 0x00,
                    affected_rows,
                    last_insert_id,
                    status_flags: StatusFlags::empty(),
                    warnings,
                    info: String::new(),
                    session_state_info: String::new(),
                })
                .await;
        }

        let output_columns: Vec<Column> = upstream_columns.iter().map(output_column).collect();

        let mut output = results.start(&output_columns).await?;

        let mut rows = match upstream_result.stream::<Row>().await {
            Ok(Some(rows)) => rows,
            Ok(None) => return output.finish().await,
            Err(_) => {
                return output
                    .finish_error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                        &b"upstream result stream failed".to_vec(),
                    )
                    .await;
            }
        };

        while let Some(row_result) = match tokio::time::timeout(
            std::time::Duration::from_secs(QUERY_TIMEOUT_SECS),
            rows.next(),
        )
        .await
        {
            Ok(next) => next,
            Err(_) => {
                return output
                    .finish_error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                        &b"upstream row stream timed out".to_vec(),
                    )
                    .await
            }
        } {
            let row = match row_result {
                Ok(row) => row,
                Err(_) => {
                    return output
                        .finish_error(
                            ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                            &b"upstream row retrieval failed".to_vec(),
                        )
                        .await;
                }
            };

            let values: Vec<Option<MysqlOutputValue>> = row
                .unwrap()
                .into_iter()
                .map(upstream_value_to_output)
                .collect();

            output.write_row(values).await?;
        }

        output.finish().await
    }
}

/// Accept only loopback binding in this first gateway release.
/// A remote listener requires client-facing TLS and hardened deployment
/// configuration before it can be enabled.
pub async fn run_mysql_gateway(
    address: SocketAddr,
    registry: Arc<MysqlIdentityRegistry>,
) -> io::Result<()> {
    if !address.ip().is_loopback() {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
            "Proxima MySQL gateway currently requires a loopback bind address",
        ));
    }

    let max_connections = std::env::var("PROXIMA_MYSQL_MAX_CONNECTIONS")
        .ok()
        .map(|value| value.parse::<usize>())
        .transpose()
        .map_err(|error| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, error))?
        .unwrap_or(DEFAULT_MAX_CONNECTIONS);
    if max_connections == 0 || max_connections > 65_536 {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
            "PROXIMA_MYSQL_MAX_CONNECTIONS must be between 1 and 65536",
        ));
    }

    let listener = TcpListener::bind(address).await?;
    let connection_slots = Arc::new(Semaphore::new(max_connections));

    tracing::info!(
        address = %listener.local_addr()?,
        max_connections,
        "Proxima MySQL gateway listening"
    );

    loop {
        let (stream, peer) = listener.accept().await?;
        let permit = match Arc::clone(&connection_slots).try_acquire_owned() {
            Ok(permit) => permit,
            Err(_) => {
                tracing::warn!(
                    peer = %peer,
                    max_connections,
                    "MySQL connection rejected because the gateway connection limit is reached"
                );
                drop(stream);
                continue;
            }
        };
        let registry = Arc::clone(&registry);

        task::spawn(async move {
            let _connection_permit = permit;
            let (reader, writer) = stream.into_split();
            let session = MysqlGatewaySession::new(registry);

            let options = IntermediaryOptions {
                process_use_statement_on_query: false,
                reject_connection_on_dbname_absence: true,
            };

            if let Err(error) =
                AsyncMysqlIntermediary::run_with_options(session, reader, writer, &options).await
            {
                tracing::debug!(
                    peer = %peer,
                    error = %error,
                    "MySQL client session ended"
                );
            }
        });
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn preserves_upstream_column_type_codes() {
        assert_eq!(
            output_column_type(mysql_async::consts::ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_LONG),
            ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_LONG
        );
        assert_eq!(
            output_column_type(mysql_async::consts::ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_NEWDECIMAL),
            ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_NEWDECIMAL
        );
        assert_eq!(
            output_column_type(mysql_async::consts::ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_BLOB),
            ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_BLOB
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn preserves_null_values() {
        assert_eq!(upstream_value_to_output(UpstreamValue::NULL), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn preserves_signed_integer_values() {
        assert!(matches!(
            upstream_value_to_output(UpstreamValue::Int(-42)),
            Some(MysqlOutputValue::Int(-42))
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn preserves_unsigned_integer_values() {
        assert!(matches!(
            upstream_value_to_output(UpstreamValue::UInt(42)),
            Some(MysqlOutputValue::UInt(42))
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn preserves_binary_payloads_byte_for_byte() {
        let bytes = vec![0xff, 0x00, 0x80, 0x41];
        assert!(
            matches!(upstream_value_to_output(UpstreamValue::Bytes(bytes.clone())), Some(MysqlOutputValue::Bytes(value)) if value == bytes)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn preserves_datetime_values_as_typed_values() {
        assert!(matches!(
            upstream_value_to_output(UpstreamValue::Date(2026, 11, 19, 12, 30, 45, 123456)),
            Some(MysqlOutputValue::Date(2026, 11, 19, 12, 30, 45, 123456))
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn converts_signed_prepared_parameter() {
        assert!(matches!(
            convert_parameter(ValueInner::Int(-42)),
            Ok(UpstreamValue::Int(-42))
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn converts_unsigned_prepared_parameter() {
        assert!(matches!(
            convert_parameter(ValueInner::UInt(42)),
            Ok(UpstreamValue::UInt(42))
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn converts_string_prepared_parameter() {
        assert!(matches!(
            convert_parameter(ValueInner::Bytes(b"tenant-a")),
            Ok(UpstreamValue::Bytes(value)) if value == b"tenant-a"
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn converts_null_prepared_parameter() {
        assert!(matches!(
            convert_parameter(ValueInner::NULL),
            Ok(UpstreamValue::NULL)
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn decodes_binary_date_parameter() {
        assert_eq!(
            decode_date_payload(&[0xe8, 0x07, 11, 19]),
            Ok((2024, 11, 19))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn decodes_binary_datetime_with_microseconds() {
        let bytes = [0xe8, 0x07, 11, 19, 12, 30, 45, 0x40, 0x0d, 0x03, 0x00];
        assert_eq!(
            decode_datetime_payload(&bytes),
            Ok((2024, 11, 19, 12, 30, 45, 200000))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn decodes_binary_time_with_days_and_microseconds() {
        let bytes = [1, 2, 0, 0, 0, 3, 4, 5, 0x40, 0x0d, 0x03, 0x00];
        assert_eq!(decode_time_payload(&bytes), Ok((true, 2, 3, 4, 5, 200000)));
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_invalid_binary_temporal_values() {
        assert!(decode_date_payload(&[0xe8, 0x07, 13, 19]).is_err());
        assert!(decode_datetime_payload(&[0; 5]).is_err());
        assert!(decode_time_payload(&[2, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn decodes_binary_date_parameter_into_upstream_value() {
        assert_eq!(
            convert_parameter(ValueInner::Date(&[0xe8, 0x07, 11, 19])),
            Ok(UpstreamValue::Date(2024, 11, 19, 0, 0, 0, 0))
        );
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\attestation.rs ==========
use hmac::{Hmac, Mac};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use std::io;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
use tokio::io::AsyncWriteExt;
use tokio::sync::Mutex;

type HmacSha256 = Hmac<Sha256>;

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct AttestationConfig {
    pub ledger_path: PathBuf,
    pub signing_key: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    pub organization_id: String,
    pub node_id: String,
}

impl AttestationConfig {
    pub fn from_env() -> Self {
        Self {
            ledger_path: std::env::var("PROXIMA_ATTESTATION_LEDGER_PATH")
                .map(PathBuf::from)
                .unwrap_or_else(|_| PathBuf::from("data/proxima-attestations.jsonl")),
            signing_key: std::env::var("PROXIMA_NODE_SIGNING_KEY_HEX")
                .ok()
                .and_then(|value| hex::decode(value.trim()).ok())
                .filter(|value| value.len() == 32)
                .or_else(|| {
                    std::env::var("PROXIMA_ATTESTATION_SIGNING_KEY")
                        .ok()
                        .filter(|value| value.len() >= 32)
                        .map(String::into_bytes)
                }),
            organization_id: std::env::var("PROXIMA_ORGANIZATION_ID")
                .map(|value| safe(&value))
                .unwrap_or_else(|_| "unconfigured".into()),
            node_id: std::env::var("PROXIMA_NODE_ID")
                .map(|value| safe(&value))
                .unwrap_or_else(|_| "local-node".into()),
        }
    }
}

pub struct AttestationLedger {
    config: AttestationConfig,
    lock: Mutex<()>,
}

impl AttestationLedger {
    pub fn new(config: AttestationConfig) -> Self {
        Self {
            config,
            lock: Mutex::new(()),
        }
    }

    pub async fn append_hourly(
        &self,
        telemetry: TelemetrySummary,
        state: SecurityState,
    ) -> io::Result<String> {
        let _guard = self.lock.lock().await;
        let existing_chain = self.verify_chain_unlocked().await?;
        if !existing_chain.valid {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                "existing attestation ledger failed verification; refusing to append",
            ));
        }
        if self.config.signing_key.is_none() {
            return Err(io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied, "a valid node or attestation signing key must be configured before signing attestations"));
        }
        let end = unix_now()?;
        let start = end.saturating_sub(3600);
        let previous_hash = previous_hash(&self.config.ledger_path).await?;
        let canonical = format!(
            "attestation_version=1\norganization_id={}\nnode_id={}\nepoch_timestamp_start={}\nepoch_timestamp_end={}\ntotal_processed_queries={}\nauthenticated_tenant_sessions={}\nblocked_unauthorized_violations={}\np99_latency_overhead_ms={}\nforce_rls_active={}\ntls_termination_enforced={}\ncircuit_breaker_tripped={}\nprevious_block_hash={}\n",
            safe(&self.config.organization_id), safe(&self.config.node_id), start, end,
            telemetry.total_processed_queries, telemetry.authenticated_tenant_sessions,
            telemetry.blocked_unauthorized_violations, telemetry.p99_latency_overhead_ms,
            state.force_rls_active, state.tls_termination_enforced, state.circuit_breaker_tripped, previous_hash
        );
        let digest = hex_sha256(canonical.as_bytes());
        let mut mac =
            HmacSha256::new_from_slice(self.config.signing_key.as_deref().unwrap_or_default())
                .map_err(|_| {
                    io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                        "invalid attestation signing key",
                    )
                })?;
        mac.update(digest.as_bytes());
        let signature = hex_bytes(&mac.finalize().into_bytes());
        let record = format!("{{\"attestation_version\":1,\"organization_id\":\"{}\",\"node_id\":\"{}\",\"epoch_timestamp_start\":{},\"epoch_timestamp_end\":{},\"telemetry_summary\":{{\"total_processed_queries\":{},\"authenticated_tenant_sessions\":{},\"blocked_unauthorized_violations\":{},\"p99_latency_overhead_ms\":{}}},\"security_state\":{{\"force_rls_active\":{},\"tls_termination_enforced\":{},\"circuit_breaker_tripped\":{}}},\"previous_block_hash\":\"{}\",\"canonical_payload\":\"{}\",\"block_hash\":\"{}\",\"block_hash_signature\":\"{}\"}}\n",
            json_escape(&self.config.organization_id), json_escape(&self.config.node_id), start, end,
            telemetry.total_processed_queries, telemetry.authenticated_tenant_sessions,
            telemetry.blocked_unauthorized_violations, telemetry.p99_latency_overhead_ms,
            state.force_rls_active, state.tls_termination_enforced, state.circuit_breaker_tripped,
            previous_hash, json_escape(&canonical), digest, signature
        );
        if let Some(parent) = self.config.ledger_path.parent() {
            tokio::fs::create_dir_all(parent).await?;
        }
        let mut file = tokio::fs::OpenOptions::new()
            .create(true)
            .append(true)
            .open(&self.config.ledger_path)
            .await?;
        file.write_all(record.as_bytes()).await?;
        file.sync_all().await?;
        Ok(digest)
    }

    pub async fn pending_records(&self) -> io::Result<Vec<String>> {
        let _guard = self.lock.lock().await;
        let verification = self.verify_chain_unlocked().await?;
        if !verification.valid {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                verification
                    .error
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| "invalid ledger chain".into()),
            ));
        }

        let cursor = read_delivery_cursor(&self.config.ledger_path).await?;
        let content = match tokio::fs::read_to_string(&self.config.ledger_path).await {
            Ok(content) => content,
            Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound && cursor == "GENESIS" => {
                return Ok(Vec::new());
            }
            Err(error) => return Err(error),
        };

        let records: Vec<String> = content
            .lines()
            .filter(|line| !line.trim().is_empty())
            .map(str::to_owned)
            .collect();
        let start = next_unacknowledged_index(&records, &cursor)?;
        Ok(records.into_iter().skip(start).collect())
    }

    pub async fn acknowledge_record(&self, block_hash: &str) -> io::Result<()> {
        let _guard = self.lock.lock().await;
        let verification = self.verify_chain_unlocked().await?;
        if !verification.valid {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                verification
                    .error
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| "invalid ledger chain".into()),
            ));
        }

        let cursor = read_delivery_cursor(&self.config.ledger_path).await?;
        let content = tokio::fs::read_to_string(&self.config.ledger_path).await?;
        let records: Vec<String> = content
            .lines()
            .filter(|line| !line.trim().is_empty())
            .map(str::to_owned)
            .collect();
        let next = next_unacknowledged_index(&records, &cursor)?;
        let record = records.get(next).ok_or_else(|| {
            io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::NotFound, "no pending record to acknowledge")
        })?;

        if json_field(record, "block_hash").as_deref() != Some(block_hash) {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                "refusing out-of-order delivery acknowledgement",
            ));
        }

        let state_path = self.config.ledger_path.with_extension("delivery.json");
        if let Some(parent) = state_path.parent() {
            tokio::fs::create_dir_all(parent).await?;
        }

        let temp_path = state_path.with_extension("delivery.json.tmp");
        let state = serde_json::json!({"acknowledged_block_hash": block_hash});
        let mut file = tokio::fs::OpenOptions::new()
            .create(true)
            .truncate(true)
            .write(true)
            .open(&temp_path)
            .await?;
        file.write_all(format!("{state}\n").as_bytes()).await?;
        file.sync_all().await?;
        drop(file);

        // Atomic replacement on platforms supporting rename-over-existing.
        // If replacement fails, leave the temp file and report the failure.
        #[cfg(windows)]
        {
            if tokio::fs::metadata(&state_path).await.is_ok() {
                tokio::fs::remove_file(&state_path).await?;
            }
        }
        tokio::fs::rename(&temp_path, &state_path).await?;
        Ok(())
    }
    pub async fn latest_record(&self) -> io::Result<Option<String>> {
        match tokio::fs::read_to_string(&self.config.ledger_path).await {
            Ok(content) => Ok(content.lines().last().map(str::to_owned)),
            Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(None),
            Err(error) => Err(error),
        }
    }

    pub async fn verify_chain(&self) -> io::Result<ChainVerification> {
        let _guard = self.lock.lock().await;
        self.verify_chain_unlocked().await
    }

    async fn verify_chain_unlocked(&self) -> io::Result<ChainVerification> {
        let content = match tokio::fs::read_to_string(&self.config.ledger_path).await {
            Ok(content) => content,
            Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
                return Ok(ChainVerification {
                    valid: true,
                    records: 0,
                    error: None,
                })
            }
            Err(error) => return Err(error),
        };
        let mut previous = "GENESIS".to_string();
        let mut count = 0usize;
        for (index, line) in content.lines().enumerate() {
            let expected_prev = format!("\"previous_block_hash\":\"{}\"", previous);
            if !line.contains(&expected_prev) {
                return Ok(ChainVerification {
                    valid: false,
                    records: count,
                    error: Some(format!("chain link mismatch at line {}", index + 1)),
                });
            }
            let Some(hash) = json_field(line, "block_hash") else {
                return Ok(ChainVerification {
                    valid: false,
                    records: count,
                    error: Some(format!("missing block hash at line {}", index + 1)),
                });
            };
            let Some(payload) = json_field(line, "canonical_payload") else {
                return Ok(ChainVerification {
                    valid: false,
                    records: count,
                    error: Some(format!("missing canonical payload at line {}", index + 1)),
                });
            };
            if hex_sha256(json_unescape(&payload).as_bytes()) != hash {
                return Ok(ChainVerification {
                    valid: false,
                    records: count,
                    error: Some(format!("payload hash mismatch at line {}", index + 1)),
                });
            }
            if !record_matches_payload(line, &json_unescape(&payload))
                || json_field(line, "organization_id").as_deref()
                    != Some(safe(&self.config.organization_id).as_str())
                || json_field(line, "node_id").as_deref()
                    != Some(safe(&self.config.node_id).as_str())
            {
                return Ok(ChainVerification {
                    valid: false,
                    records: count,
                    error: Some(format!("record fields mismatch at line {}", index + 1)),
                });
            }
            let Some(signature) = json_field(line, "block_hash_signature") else {
                return Ok(ChainVerification {
                    valid: false,
                    records: count,
                    error: Some(format!("missing signature at line {}", index + 1)),
                });
            };
            if let Some(key) = self.config.signing_key.as_deref() {
                let mut mac = HmacSha256::new_from_slice(key).map_err(|_| {
                    io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, "invalid signing key")
                })?;
                mac.update(hash.as_bytes());
                if hex_bytes(&mac.finalize().into_bytes()) != signature {
                    return Ok(ChainVerification {
                        valid: false,
                        records: count,
                        error: Some(format!("signature mismatch at line {}", index + 1)),
                    });
                }
            } else {
                return Ok(ChainVerification {
                    valid: false,
                    records: count,
                    error: Some("signing key is not configured".into()),
                });
            }
            previous = hash;
            count += 1;
        }
        Ok(ChainVerification {
            valid: true,
            records: count,
            error: None,
        })
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
pub struct TelemetrySummary {
    pub total_processed_queries: u64,
    pub authenticated_tenant_sessions: u64,
    pub blocked_unauthorized_violations: u64,
    pub p99_latency_overhead_ms: f64,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
pub struct SecurityState {
    pub force_rls_active: bool,
    pub tls_termination_enforced: bool,
    pub circuit_breaker_tripped: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ChainVerification {
    pub valid: bool,
    pub records: usize,
    pub error: Option<String>,
}

fn next_unacknowledged_index(records: &[String], cursor: &str) -> io::Result<usize> {
    if cursor == "GENESIS" {
        return Ok(0);
    }

    records
        .iter()
        .position(|line| json_field(line, "block_hash").as_deref() == Some(cursor))
        .map(|index| index + 1)
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                "delivery cursor does not match the signed ledger",
            )
        })
}

async fn read_delivery_cursor(ledger_path: &Path) -> io::Result<String> {
    let state_path = ledger_path.with_extension("delivery.json");
    let content = match tokio::fs::read_to_string(state_path).await {
        Ok(content) => content,
        Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok("GENESIS".into()),
        Err(error) => return Err(error),
    };

    let value: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&content).map_err(|error| {
        io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            format!("invalid delivery cursor: {error}"),
        )
    })?;

    value
        .get("acknowledged_block_hash")
        .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)
        .map(str::to_owned)
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                "delivery cursor has no acknowledged block hash",
            )
        })
}
async fn previous_hash(path: &Path) -> io::Result<String> {
    match tokio::fs::read_to_string(path).await {
        Ok(content) => match content.lines().last() {
            Some(line) => json_field(line, "block_hash").ok_or_else(|| {
                io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                    "last attestation ledger record has no block hash",
                )
            }),
            None => Ok("GENESIS".into()),
        },
        Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok("GENESIS".into()),
        Err(error) => Err(error),
    }
}

fn record_matches_payload(record: &str, canonical: &str) -> bool {
    for line in canonical.lines() {
        let Some((key, value)) = line.split_once('=') else {
            return false;
        };
        let expected = match key {
            "organization_id" | "node_id" | "previous_block_hash" => {
                format!("\"{key}\":\"{value}\"")
            }
            _ => format!("\"{key}\":{value}"),
        };
        if !record.contains(&expected) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    true
}

fn json_unescape(value: &str) -> String {
    let mut output = String::with_capacity(value.len());
    let mut chars = value.chars();
    while let Some(ch) = chars.next() {
        if ch == '\\' {
            match chars.next() {
                Some('n') => output.push('\n'),
                Some('r') => output.push('\r'),
                Some('\\') => output.push('\\'),
                Some('"') => output.push('"'),
                Some(other) => {
                    output.push('\\');
                    output.push(other);
                }
                None => output.push('\\'),
            }
        } else {
            output.push(ch);
        }
    }
    output
}

fn json_field(line: &str, field: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let marker = format!("\"{field}\":\"");
    let start = line.find(&marker)? + marker.len();
    let end = line[start..].find('"')? + start;
    Some(line[start..end].to_string())
}
fn safe(value: &str) -> String {
    value
        .chars()
        .filter(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || matches!(c, '-' | '_' | '.'))
        .take(128)
        .collect()
}
fn json_escape(value: &str) -> String {
    value
        .replace('\\', "\\\\")
        .replace('"', "\\\"")
        .replace('\n', "\\n")
        .replace('\r', "\\r")
}
fn hex_sha256(input: &[u8]) -> String {
    let digest = Sha256::digest(input);
    hex_bytes(&digest)
}
fn hex_bytes(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    bytes.iter().map(|byte| format!("{byte:02x}")).collect()
}
fn unix_now() -> io::Result<u64> {
    Ok(SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .map_err(|_| io::Error::other("system clock before UNIX epoch"))?
        .as_secs())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[tokio::test]
    async fn signed_records_chain_and_detect_tampering() {
        let path =
            std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("proxima-attest-{}.jsonl", std::process::id()));
        let _ = tokio::fs::remove_file(&path).await;
        let ledger = AttestationLedger::new(AttestationConfig {
            ledger_path: path.clone(),
            signing_key: Some(b"01234567890123456789012345678901".to_vec()),
            organization_id: "org-test".into(),
            node_id: "node-test".into(),
        });
        ledger
            .append_hourly(TelemetrySummary::default(), SecurityState::default())
            .await
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            ledger.verify_chain().await.unwrap(),
            ChainVerification {
                valid: true,
                records: 1,
                error: None
            }
        );
        tokio::fs::write(&path, "{\"previous_block_hash\":\"GENESIS\",\"block_hash\":\"forged\",\"block_hash_signature\":\"bad\"}\n").await.unwrap();
        assert!(!ledger.verify_chain().await.unwrap().valid);
        assert!(ledger
            .append_hourly(TelemetrySummary::default(), SecurityState::default())
            .await
            .is_err());
        let _ = tokio::fs::remove_file(path).await;
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\break_glass.rs ==========
use std::io;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use tokio::io::AsyncWriteExt;
use uuid::Uuid;
pub fn default_outbox_dir() -> PathBuf {
    std::env::var_os("PROXIMA_BREAK_GLASS_OUTBOX_DIR")
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("data/proxima-break-glass-outbox"))
}
pub async fn record_event(dir: &Path, node_id: &str, nonce_hash: &str) -> io::Result<()> {
    if node_id.is_empty()
        || node_id.len() > 128
        || !node_id
            .bytes()
            .all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || matches!(b, b'-' | b'_' | b'.'))
        || nonce_hash.len() != 64
        || !nonce_hash.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_hexdigit())
    {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
            "invalid break-glass event fields",
        ));
    }
    tokio::fs::create_dir_all(dir).await?;
    let event_id = Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
    let observed_at = std::time::SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
        .map_err(|_| io::Error::other("system clock before UNIX epoch"))?
        .as_secs() as i64;
    let event = serde_json::json!({"event_id":event_id,"node_id":node_id,"event_type":"security.break_glass.used","severity":"warning","peer_fingerprint":nonce_hash,"reason":"A short-lived, one-time emergency tenant token was used; tenant isolation remains enforced.","observed_at":observed_at});
    let path = dir.join(format!("event-{event_id}.json"));
    let mut file = tokio::fs::OpenOptions::new()
        .create_new(true)
        .write(true)
        .open(path)
        .await?;
    file.write_all(format!("{event}\n").as_bytes()).await?;
    file.sync_all().await
}
pub async fn pending_events(dir: &Path) -> io::Result<Vec<(PathBuf, serde_json::Value)>> {
    let mut entries = match tokio::fs::read_dir(dir).await {
        Ok(v) => v,
        Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(Vec::new()),
        Err(e) => return Err(e),
    };
    let mut paths = Vec::new();
    while let Some(entry) = entries.next_entry().await? {
        let path = entry.path();
        if path
            .file_name()
            .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
            .is_some_and(|n| n.starts_with("event-") && n.ends_with(".json"))
        {
            paths.push(path);
        }
    }
    paths.sort();
    let mut events = Vec::new();
    for path in paths {
        let raw = tokio::fs::read_to_string(&path).await?;
        let value = serde_json::from_str(&raw)
            .map_err(|e| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidData, e))?;
        events.push((path, value));
    }
    Ok(events)
}
pub async fn acknowledge_event(path: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
    tokio::fs::remove_file(path).await
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[tokio::test]
    async fn event_outbox_persists_until_acknowledged() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        record_event(dir.path(), "node-test", &"a".repeat(64))
            .await
            .unwrap();
        let pending = pending_events(dir.path()).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(pending.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(pending[0].1["event_type"], "security.break_glass.used");
        assert_eq!(pending[0].1["severity"], "warning");
        acknowledge_event(&pending[0].0).await.unwrap();
        assert!(pending_events(dir.path()).await.unwrap().is_empty());
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\canary_probe.rs ==========
use hmac::{Hmac, Mac};
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use std::{
    env,
    net::SocketAddr,
    time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH},
};
use tokio::time::{interval, Duration};
use tokio_postgres::{Config as PgConfig, NoTls};

type HmacSha256 = Hmac<Sha256>;
const SCENARIO_ID: &str = "tenant-isolation-v1";

#[derive(Clone)]
struct CanaryConfig {
    proxy: SocketAddr,
    database: String,
    schema: String,
    table: String,
    tenant_column: String,
    tenants: Vec<String>,
    tenant_signing_key: Vec<u8>,
    node_id: String,
    node_signing_key: Vec<u8>,
    api_token: String,
    register_url: String,
    report_url: String,
    interval_seconds: u64,
}

pub(crate) fn control_plane_url_allowed(url: &str, allow_insecure_http: bool) -> bool {
    url.starts_with("https://")
        || url.starts_with("http://127.0.0.1:")
        || url.starts_with("http://localhost:")
        || (allow_insecure_http && url.starts_with("http://"))
}

impl CanaryConfig {
    fn from_env(listener: SocketAddr) -> Result<Option<Self>, String> {
        if !matches!(
            env::var("PROXIMA_TENANT_CANARY_ENABLED").as_deref(),
            Ok("true" | "1" | "yes")
        ) {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        let proxy = listener;
        if !proxy.ip().is_loopback() || proxy.port() == 0 {
            return Err(
                "PROXIMA_TENANT_CANARY_LOCAL_LISTEN_ADDR must use a fixed loopback address and nonzero port".into(),
            );
        }
        let required = |name: &str| -> Result<String, String> {
            env::var(name)
                .ok()
                .filter(|value| !value.trim().is_empty())
                .ok_or_else(|| format!("missing required environment variable {name}"))
        };
        let node_id = required("PROXIMA_NODE_ID")?;
        let node_id = uuid::Uuid::parse_str(&node_id)
            .map_err(|_| {
                "PROXIMA_NODE_ID must be the enrolled node UUID for tenant canaries".to_string()
            })?
            .to_string();
        let node_signing_key = hex::decode(required("PROXIMA_NODE_SIGNING_KEY_HEX")?.trim())
            .map_err(|_| "PROXIMA_NODE_SIGNING_KEY_HEX must be valid hex")?;
        if node_signing_key.len() != 32 {
            return Err("PROXIMA_NODE_SIGNING_KEY_HEX must decode to 32 bytes".into());
        }
        let tenant_signing_key = required("PROXIMA_TENANT_SIGNING_KEY")?.into_bytes();
        if tenant_signing_key.len() < 32 {
            return Err("PROXIMA_TENANT_SIGNING_KEY must contain at least 32 bytes".into());
        }
        let api_token = env::var("PROXIMA_SECURITY_EVENT_INGEST_TOKEN")
            .or_else(|_| env::var("PROXIMA_ATTESTATION_INGEST_TOKEN"))
            .map_err(|_| {
                "missing PROXIMA_SECURITY_EVENT_INGEST_TOKEN or PROXIMA_ATTESTATION_INGEST_TOKEN"
                    .to_string()
            })?;
        if !api_token.starts_with("aga_") {
            return Err("security event ingest token must be an Agata API key (aga_...)".into());
        }
        let allow_insecure_http = matches!(
            env::var("PROXIMA_TENANT_CANARY_ALLOW_INSECURE_HTTP")
                .unwrap_or_default()
                .trim()
                .to_ascii_lowercase()
                .as_str(),
            "true" | "1" | "yes"
        );
        let event_url = required("PROXIMA_SECURITY_EVENT_INGEST_URL")?;
        if !control_plane_url_allowed(&event_url, allow_insecure_http) {
            return Err("security event ingest URL must use HTTPS; plain HTTP is allowed only for loopback development or with the explicit PROXIMA_TENANT_CANARY_ALLOW_INSECURE_HTTP opt-in".into());
        }
        let base = event_url
            .strip_suffix("/api/v1/security/events")
            .unwrap_or_else(|| event_url.trim_end_matches('/'));
        let register_url = env::var("PROXIMA_SECURITY_PROBE_REGISTER_URL")
            .ok()
            .filter(|value| !value.trim().is_empty())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| format!("{base}/api/v1/security/probes/register"));
        let report_url = env::var("PROXIMA_SECURITY_PROBE_REPORT_URL")
            .ok()
            .filter(|value| !value.trim().is_empty())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| format!("{base}/api/v1/security/probes/report"));
        for url in [&register_url, &report_url] {
            if !control_plane_url_allowed(url, allow_insecure_http) {
                return Err("security probe URLs must use HTTPS; plain HTTP is allowed only for loopback development or with the explicit PROXIMA_TENANT_CANARY_ALLOW_INSECURE_HTTP opt-in".into());
            }
        }
        let tenants = required("PROXIMA_TENANT_CANARY_TENANTS")?
            .split(',')
            .map(str::trim)
            .map(str::to_owned)
            .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if tenants.len() < 2
            || tenants.len() > 10
            || tenants
                .iter()
                .collect::<std::collections::HashSet<_>>()
                .len()
                != tenants.len()
            || tenants
                .iter()
                .map(|tenant| password_env_name(tenant))
                .collect::<std::collections::HashSet<_>>()
                .len()
                != tenants.len()
        {
            return Err("PROXIMA_TENANT_CANARY_TENANTS must contain 2â€“10 unique tenant IDs".into());
        }
        if tenants.iter().any(|tenant| {
            tenant.len() > 128
                || tenant.is_empty()
                || !tenant
                    .bytes()
                    .all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'-' || b == b'_')
        }) {
            return Err(
                "tenant IDs may contain only ASCII letters, digits, hyphens and underscores".into(),
            );
        }
        for tenant in &tenants {
            let key = password_env_name(tenant);
            if env::var(&key)
                .ok()
                .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
                .is_none()
            {
                return Err(format!(
                    "missing password for canary tenant {tenant}: set {key}"
                ));
            }
        }
        let schema =
            env::var("PROXIMA_TENANT_CANARY_SCHEMA").unwrap_or_else(|_| "proxima_security".into());
        let table =
            env::var("PROXIMA_TENANT_CANARY_TABLE").unwrap_or_else(|_| "canary_records".into());
        let tenant_column =
            env::var("PROXIMA_TENANT_CANARY_TENANT_COLUMN").unwrap_or_else(|_| "tenant_id".into());
        for identifier in [&schema, &table, &tenant_column] {
            if !valid_identifier(identifier) {
                return Err(
                    "canary schema, table and column must be simple SQL identifiers".into(),
                );
            }
        }
        let interval_seconds = env::var("PROXIMA_TENANT_CANARY_INTERVAL_SECONDS")
            .ok()
            .and_then(|value| value.parse::<u64>().ok())
            .unwrap_or(300);
        if !(60..=3600).contains(&interval_seconds) {
            return Err(
                "PROXIMA_TENANT_CANARY_INTERVAL_SECONDS must be between 60 and 3600".into(),
            );
        }
        Ok(Some(Self {
            proxy,
            database: env::var("PROXIMA_TENANT_CANARY_DATABASE")
                .unwrap_or_else(|_| "proxima_dev".into()),
            schema,
            table,
            tenant_column,
            tenants,
            tenant_signing_key,
            node_id,
            node_signing_key,
            api_token,
            register_url,
            report_url,
            interval_seconds,
        }))
    }
}

fn valid_identifier(identifier: &str) -> bool {
    !identifier.is_empty()
        && identifier
            .bytes()
            .next()
            .is_some_and(|byte| byte.is_ascii_alphabetic() || byte == b'_')
        && identifier
            .bytes()
            .all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || byte == b'_')
}

fn user_env_name(tenant: &str) -> String {
    format!(
        "PROXIMA_TENANT_CANARY_USER_{}",
        tenant
            .chars()
            .map(|c| if c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() {
                c.to_ascii_uppercase()
            } else {
                '_'
            })
            .collect::<String>()
    )
}

fn default_user_name(tenant: &str) -> String {
    format!(
        "proxima_tenant_{}",
        tenant
            .chars()
            .map(|c| if c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() { c } else { '_' })
            .collect::<String>()
    )
}

fn password_env_name(tenant: &str) -> String {
    format!(
        "PROXIMA_TENANT_CANARY_PASSWORD_{}",
        tenant
            .chars()
            .map(|c| if c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() {
                c.to_ascii_uppercase()
            } else {
                '_'
            })
            .collect::<String>()
    )
}

fn now_unix() -> i64 {
    SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .map(|duration| duration.as_secs() as i64)
        .unwrap_or_default()
}

fn hmac_hex(key: &[u8], message: &str) -> String {
    let mut mac = HmacSha256::new_from_slice(key).expect("HMAC accepts any key size");
    mac.update(message.as_bytes());
    hex::encode(mac.finalize().into_bytes())
}

fn tenant_token(config: &CanaryConfig, tenant: &str) -> String {
    let expires = now_unix() + 300;
    let payload = format!("v1.{tenant}.{expires}");
    format!(
        "{payload}.{}",
        hmac_hex(&config.tenant_signing_key, &payload)
    )
}

async fn register(config: &CanaryConfig, client: &reqwest::Client) -> Result<(), String> {
    let observed_at = now_unix();
    let message = format!(
        "register|{}|{}|{}|{}",
        config.node_id, SCENARIO_ID, config.interval_seconds, observed_at
    );
    let body = json!({
        "node_id": config.node_id, "scenario_id": SCENARIO_ID,
        "interval_seconds": config.interval_seconds, "observed_at": observed_at,
        "signature": hmac_hex(&config.node_signing_key, &message)
    });
    client
        .post(&config.register_url)
        .bearer_auth(&config.api_token)
        .json(&body)
        .send()
        .await
        .map_err(|error| format!("probe registration request failed: {error}"))?
        .error_for_status()
        .map_err(|error| format!("probe registration rejected: {error}"))?;
    Ok(())
}

async fn connect_tenant(
    config: &CanaryConfig,
    tenant: &str,
) -> Result<tokio_postgres::Client, String> {
    let password = env::var(password_env_name(tenant))
        .map_err(|_| format!("missing password for tenant {tenant}"))?;
    let token = tenant_token(config, tenant);
    let user = env::var(user_env_name(tenant)).unwrap_or_else(|_| default_user_name(tenant));
    if !valid_identifier(&user) {
        return Err(format!(
            "invalid database username configured for tenant {tenant}"
        ));
    }
    let options = format!("-c proxima_tenant_token={token}");
    let mut pg = PgConfig::new();
    pg.host(config.proxy.ip().to_string())
        .port(config.proxy.port())
        .user(&user)
        .password(&password)
        .dbname(&config.database)
        .options(&options)
        .connect_timeout(Duration::from_secs(5));
    let (client, connection) = pg
        .connect(NoTls)
        .await
        .map_err(|error| format!("tenant {tenant} could not connect through Proxima: {error}"))?;
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        if let Err(error) = connection.await {
            tracing::debug!(%error, "tenant-canary PostgreSQL connection closed");
        }
    });
    Ok(client)
}

async fn execute_scenario(config: &CanaryConfig) -> (String, Value) {
    let mut checks = Vec::new();
    let mut failed = false;
    let mut review = false;
    let query = format!(
        "SELECT count(*)::bigint FROM {}.{} WHERE {} = $1",
        config.schema, config.table, config.tenant_column
    );

    // Reuse one authenticated session and prepared statement per tenant.
    // A 10-tenant matrix therefore uses at most 10 connections per run,
    // rather than opening a new connection for each of the 100 checks.
    for tenant in &config.tenants {
        let client = match connect_tenant(config, tenant).await {
            Ok(client) => client,
            Err(error) => {
                return (
                    "error".into(),
                    json!({"scenario_id":SCENARIO_ID,"checks":checks,"error":error}),
                );
            }
        };
        let statement = match client.prepare(&query).await {
            Ok(statement) => statement,
            Err(error) => {
                return (
                    "error".into(),
                    json!({"scenario_id":SCENARIO_ID,"checks":checks,"error":format!("tenant {tenant} could not prepare the canary query: {error}")}),
                );
            }
        };

        match client.query_one(&statement, &[&tenant.as_str()]).await {
            Ok(row) => {
                let count: i64 = row.get(0);
                checks.push(json!({"tenant":tenant,"target":tenant,"kind":"own_canary_visible","expected":"positive","observed":count}));
                if count == 0 {
                    review = true;
                }
            }
            Err(error) => {
                return (
                    "error".into(),
                    json!({"scenario_id":SCENARIO_ID,"checks":checks,"error":format!("tenant {tenant} own-canary query failed: {error}")}),
                );
            }
        }

        for target in config.tenants.iter().filter(|target| *target != tenant) {
            match client.query_one(&statement, &[&target.as_str()]).await {
                Ok(row) => {
                    let count: i64 = row.get(0);
                    checks.push(json!({"tenant":tenant,"target":target,"kind":"cross_tenant_canary_hidden","expected":0,"observed":count}));
                    if count > 0 {
                        failed = true;
                    }
                }
                Err(error) => {
                    return (
                        "error".into(),
                        json!({"scenario_id":SCENARIO_ID,"checks":checks,"error":format!("tenant {tenant} cross-tenant query for {target} failed: {error}")}),
                    );
                }
            }
        }
    }
    let status = if failed {
        "fail"
    } else if review {
        "review"
    } else {
        "pass"
    };
    (
        status.into(),
        json!({
            "scenario_id":SCENARIO_ID,
            "checks":checks,
            "check_count":checks.len(),
            "summary":if failed {"Cross-tenant canary data was visible to another tenant."} else if review {"At least one tenant could not see its own canary record; fixture or policy requires review."} else {"All own-tenant and cross-tenant canary checks matched expectations."}
        }),
    )
}

async fn report(
    config: &CanaryConfig,
    client: &reqwest::Client,
    status: &str,
    evidence: Value,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let observed_at = now_unix();
    let evidence_json = evidence.to_string();
    let evidence_hash = hex::encode(Sha256::digest(evidence_json.as_bytes()));
    let message = format!(
        "report|{}|{}|{}|{}|{}",
        config.node_id, SCENARIO_ID, status, observed_at, evidence_hash
    );
    let body = json!({
        "node_id":config.node_id,"scenario_id":SCENARIO_ID,"status":status,
        "observed_at":observed_at,"evidence":evidence,"evidence_hash":evidence_hash,
        "signature":hmac_hex(&config.node_signing_key,&message)
    });
    client
        .post(&config.report_url)
        .bearer_auth(&config.api_token)
        .json(&body)
        .send()
        .await
        .map_err(|error| format!("probe report request failed: {error}"))?
        .error_for_status()
        .map_err(|error| format!("probe report rejected: {error}"))?;
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn run_if_enabled(proxy: SocketAddr) {
    let config = match CanaryConfig::from_env(proxy) {
        Ok(Some(config)) => config,
        Ok(None) => return,
        Err(error) => {
            tracing::error!(%error, "tenant canary is enabled but configuration is invalid");
            return;
        }
    };
    let client = match reqwest::Client::builder()
        .timeout(Duration::from_secs(10))
        .build()
    {
        Ok(client) => client,
        Err(error) => {
            tracing::error!(%error, "tenant canary HTTP client unavailable");
            return;
        }
    };
    loop {
        match register(&config, &client).await {
            Ok(()) => break,
            Err(error) => {
                tracing::error!(%error, "tenant canary registration failed; retrying in 30 seconds");
                tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(30)).await;
            }
        }
    }
    tracing::info!(
        scenario = SCENARIO_ID,
        interval_seconds = config.interval_seconds,
        "tenant canary runner registered"
    );
    let mut ticker = interval(Duration::from_secs(config.interval_seconds));
    loop {
        ticker.tick().await;
        let (status, evidence) = execute_scenario(&config).await;
        if status == "fail" {
            tracing::error!(
                scenario = SCENARIO_ID,
                "tenant canary detected cross-tenant visibility"
            );
        } else if status == "pass" {
            tracing::info!(scenario = SCENARIO_ID, "tenant canary scenario passed");
        } else {
            tracing::warn!(
                scenario = SCENARIO_ID,
                status,
                "tenant canary requires attention"
            );
        }
        if let Err(error) = report(&config, &client, &status, evidence).await {
            tracing::error!(%error, scenario = SCENARIO_ID, "tenant canary outcome could not be delivered");
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn control_plane_urls_require_https_except_for_explicit_local_development() {
        assert!(control_plane_url_allowed(
            "https://control.example.com/api/v1/security/events",
            false
        ));
        assert!(control_plane_url_allowed(
            "http://127.0.0.1:8080/api/v1/security/events",
            false
        ));
        assert!(control_plane_url_allowed(
            "http://localhost:8080/api/v1/security/events",
            false
        ));
        assert!(!control_plane_url_allowed(
            "http://proxima-control:8080/api/v1/security/events",
            false
        ));
        assert!(control_plane_url_allowed(
            "http://proxima-control:8080/api/v1/security/events",
            true
        ));
        assert!(!control_plane_url_allowed(
            "ftp://control.example.com/events",
            true
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn identifier_validation_accepts_only_simple_sql_identifiers() {
        for valid in ["records", "_records2", "proxima_security"] {
            assert!(valid_identifier(valid));
        }
        for invalid in [
            "",
            "records; DROP TABLE users",
            "schema.records",
            "x--",
            "2records",
        ] {
            assert!(!valid_identifier(invalid));
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn tenant_user_environment_name_is_deterministic() {
        assert_eq!(
            user_env_name("tenant_a"),
            "PROXIMA_TENANT_CANARY_USER_TENANT_A"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            user_env_name("tenant-a"),
            "PROXIMA_TENANT_CANARY_USER_TENANT_A"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn tenant_default_user_name_is_safe_and_deterministic() {
        assert_eq!(default_user_name("tenant_a"), "proxima_tenant_tenant_a");
        assert_eq!(default_user_name("tenant-a"), "proxima_tenant_tenant_a");
        assert!(valid_identifier(&default_user_name("tenant-a")));
    }

    #[test]
    fn tenant_password_environment_name_is_deterministic() {
        assert_eq!(
            password_env_name("tenant_a"),
            "PROXIMA_TENANT_CANARY_PASSWORD_TENANT_A"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            password_env_name("tenant-a"),
            "PROXIMA_TENANT_CANARY_PASSWORD_TENANT_A"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn report_signature_binds_status_and_evidence_hash() {
        let key = [7u8; 32];
        let hash = "ab".repeat(32);
        let signed = format!("report|node|scenario|pass|123|{hash}");
        let signature = hmac_hex(&key, &signed);
        assert_eq!(signature.len(), 64);
        assert_ne!(
            signature,
            hmac_hex(&key, &format!("report|node|scenario|fail|123|{hash}"))
        );
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\cassandra_tenant.rs ==========
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum CassandraTenantError {
    InvalidTenantId,
    WeakPassword,
    InvalidReplicationFactor,
    InvalidDatacenter,
}
impl std::fmt::Display for CassandraTenantError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::InvalidTenantId => write!(f, "invalid Cassandra tenant identifier"),
            Self::WeakPassword => write!(
                f,
                "Cassandra tenant password must contain at least 24 printable ASCII characters"
            ),
            Self::InvalidReplicationFactor => {
                write!(f, "Cassandra replication factor must be between 1 and 5")
            }
            Self::InvalidDatacenter => write!(f, "invalid Cassandra datacenter identifier"),
        }
    }
}
impl std::error::Error for CassandraTenantError {}

/// Cassandra keyspace-per-tenant profile. Provisioning statements must run
/// through a separately authenticated administrative session.
pub struct CassandraTenant {
    tenant_id: String,
    keyspace: String,
    role: String,
    password: String,
}
impl CassandraTenant {
    pub fn new(tenant_id: &str, password: &str) -> Result<Self, CassandraTenantError> {
        if tenant_id.is_empty()
            || tenant_id.len() > 32
            || !tenant_id
                .bytes()
                .all(|b| b.is_ascii_lowercase() || b.is_ascii_digit() || b == b'_')
        {
            return Err(CassandraTenantError::InvalidTenantId);
        }
        if password.len() < 24 || !password.bytes().all(|b| (33..=126).contains(&b)) {
            return Err(CassandraTenantError::WeakPassword);
        }
        let id = tenant_id.to_ascii_lowercase();
        Ok(Self {
            tenant_id: id.clone(),
            keyspace: format!("proxima_{id}"),
            role: format!("proxima_{id}_app"),
            password: password.to_owned(),
        })
    }
    pub fn tenant_id(&self) -> &str {
        &self.tenant_id
    }
    pub fn keyspace(&self) -> &str {
        &self.keyspace
    }
    pub fn role(&self) -> &str {
        &self.role
    }
    /// Generates a dedicated keyspace, login role, and data-only grants.
    pub fn provisioning_cql(
        &self,
        datacenter: &str,
        replication_factor: u8,
    ) -> Result<Vec<String>, CassandraTenantError> {
        if datacenter.is_empty()
            || datacenter.len() > 48
            || !datacenter
                .bytes()
                .all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'_')
        {
            return Err(CassandraTenantError::InvalidDatacenter);
        }
        if !(1..=5).contains(&replication_factor) {
            return Err(CassandraTenantError::InvalidReplicationFactor);
        }
        let password = self.password.replace('\'', "''");
        Ok(vec![
