                        message.request_id,
                        "admin",
                        "ping requires an authenticated principal and the admin database",
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                }
                write_frame(&mut upstream, &frame).await?;
            }
            "endSessions" => {
                let Some(principal) = identity.as_ref() else {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        "admin",
                        "authentication is required",
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                };
                if !authenticated || message.body.get_str("$db").unwrap_or_default() != "admin" {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        "admin",
                        "endSessions requires an authenticated principal on the admin database",
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                }
                let end_sessions = match message.body.get_array("endSessions") {
                    Ok(values) if values.len() <= 10_000 => values,
                    _ => {
                        send_command_error(
                            &mut client,
                            message.request_id,
                            "admin",
                            "endSessions must contain at most 10000 logical session identifiers",
                        )
                        .await?;
                        continue;
                    }
                };
                let mut invalid_session = None;
                for value in end_sessions {
                    let Some(lsid) = value.as_document() else {
                        invalid_session = Some(
                            "endSessions contains a non-document session identifier".to_owned(),
                        );
                        break;
                    };
                    if let Err(error) = sessions.release_if_owned(&principal.tenant_id, lsid) {
                        invalid_session = Some(error.to_string());
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if let Some(error) = invalid_session {
                    send_command_error(&mut client, message.request_id, "admin", &error).await?;
                    continue;
                }
                write_frame(&mut upstream, &frame).await?;
            }
            _ => {
                let Some(principal) = identity.as_ref() else {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        "admin",
                        "authentication is required before data commands",
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                };
                if !authenticated {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        "admin",
                        "SCRAM authentication has not completed",
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                }
                let mut policy_command = message.body.clone();
                if let Err(error) = merge_sequences(&mut policy_command, &message.sequences) {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        &principal.database,
                        &error.to_string(),
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                }
                if matches!(policy_command.get_bool("startTransaction"), Ok(true))
                    || matches!(policy_command.get_bool("autocommit"), Ok(false))
                {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        &principal.database,
                        "MongoDB transactions are not supported by this gateway profile",
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                }
                if let Ok(lsid) = policy_command.get_document("lsid") {
                    if let Err(error) = sessions.bind(&principal.tenant_id, lsid) {
                        send_command_error(
                            &mut client,
                            message.request_id,
                            &principal.database,
                            &error.to_string(),
                        )
                        .await?;
                        continue;
                    }
                    if let Err(error) = sessions.authorize(&principal.tenant_id, lsid) {
                        send_command_error(
                            &mut client,
                            message.request_id,
                            &principal.database,
                            &error.to_string(),
                        )
                        .await?;
                        continue;
                    }
                }
                let policy = MongoTenantPolicy::new(&principal.tenant_id, &principal.database)
                    .map_err(|error| {
                        io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidData, error.to_string())
                    })?;
                if let Err(error) = policy.authorize(&policy_command) {
                    send_command_error(
                        &mut client,
                        message.request_id,
                        &principal.database,
                        &error.to_string(),
                    )
                    .await?;
                    continue;
                }
                write_frame(&mut upstream, &frame).await?;
            }
        }

        let response = read_frame(&mut upstream).await?;
        if response.len() < 16 {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                "truncated MongoDB upstream response",
            ));
        }
        let response_to = i32::from_le_bytes(response[8..12].try_into().unwrap());
        if response_to != message.request_id {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                "MongoDB upstream response correlation mismatch",
            ));
        }

        if matches!(command_name.as_str(), "saslStart" | "saslContinue") {
            if let Ok(reply) = decode_op_msg(&response) {
                let ok = match reply.body.get("ok") {
                    Some(Bson::Double(value)) => *value == 1.0,
                    Some(Bson::Int32(value)) => *value == 1,
                    Some(Bson::Int64(value)) => *value == 1,
                    _ => false,
                };
                if ok && matches!(reply.body.get_bool("done"), Ok(true)) {
                    authenticated = true;
                }
            }
        }

        let mut client_response = response;
        if matches!(command_name.as_str(), "hello" | "isMaster") {
            let mut reply = decode_op_msg(&client_response)?;
            let writable_primary = matches!(reply.body.get_bool("isWritablePrimary"), Ok(true))
                || matches!(reply.body.get_bool("ismaster"), Ok(true));
            if !writable_primary {
                send_command_error(
                    &mut client,
                    message.request_id,
                    "admin",
                    "configured MongoDB upstream is not a writable primary or standalone node",
                )
                .await?;
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                    "MongoDB upstream is not writable",
                ));
            }
            if matches!(reply.body.get_str("msg"), Ok("isdbgrid")) {
                send_command_error(
                    &mut client,
                    message.request_id,
                    "admin",
                    "sharded MongoDB upstreams are not supported by this gateway profile",
                )
                .await?;
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::Unsupported,
                    "sharded MongoDB upstream is not supported",
                ));
            }
            for field in [
                "compression",
                "speculativeAuthenticate",
                "hosts",
                "passives",
                "arbiters",
                "primary",
                "setName",
                "setVersion",
                "electionId",
                "topologyVersion",
                "me",
                "msg",
                "serviceId",
                "secondary",
                "arbiterOnly",
                "hidden",
                "passive",
            ] {
                reply.body.remove(field);
            }
            client_response = encode_op_msg(reply.request_id, reply.response_to, &reply.body)?;
        }
        write_frame(&mut client, &client_response).await?;
    }
}

struct MongoMessage {
    request_id: i32,
    response_to: i32,
    body: Document,
    sequences: Vec<(String, Vec<Document>)>,
}

fn request_id(frame: &[u8]) -> i32 {
    if frame.len() >= 8 {
        i32::from_le_bytes(frame[4..8].try_into().unwrap())
    } else {
        0
    }
}

async fn read_frame(stream: &mut BoxedMongoIo) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut header = [0u8; 4];
    stream.read_exact(&mut header).await?;
    let length = i32::from_le_bytes(header) as usize;
    if !(16..=MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES).contains(&length) {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            format!("invalid MongoDB wire message length: {length}"),
        ));
    }
    let mut frame = vec![0u8; length];
    frame[..4].copy_from_slice(&header);
    stream.read_exact(&mut frame[4..]).await?;
    Ok(frame)
}

fn decode_op_msg(frame: &[u8]) -> io::Result<MongoMessage> {
    if frame.len() < 21 {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "truncated MongoDB OP_MSG",
        ));
    }
    let length = i32::from_le_bytes(frame[..4].try_into().unwrap()) as usize;
    if length != frame.len() || length > MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "MongoDB OP_MSG length mismatch",
        ));
    }
    let request_id = i32::from_le_bytes(frame[4..8].try_into().unwrap());
    let response_to = i32::from_le_bytes(frame[8..12].try_into().unwrap());
    let opcode = i32::from_le_bytes(frame[12..16].try_into().unwrap());
    if opcode != OP_MSG {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::Unsupported,
            format!("MongoDB opcode {opcode} is not supported; only OP_MSG is accepted"),
        ));
    }
    let flags = i32::from_le_bytes(frame[16..20].try_into().unwrap());
    if flags != 0 {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::Unsupported,
            "MongoDB OP_MSG checksums are not supported",
        ));
    }

    let mut offset = 20usize;
    let mut body = None;
    let mut sequences = Vec::new();
    while offset < frame.len() {
        let kind = frame[offset];
        offset += 1;
        match kind {
            0 => {
                let (document, consumed) = read_bson_document(frame, offset, frame.len())?;
                if body.replace(document).is_some() {
                    return Err(io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                        "MongoDB OP_MSG contains multiple body sections",
                    ));
                }
                offset = consumed;
            }
            1 => {
                let section_start = offset;
                if offset + 4 > frame.len() {
                    return Err(io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                        "truncated MongoDB document sequence",
                    ));
                }
                let section_size =
                    i32::from_le_bytes(frame[offset..offset + 4].try_into().unwrap());
                if section_size < 5 {
                    return Err(io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                        "invalid MongoDB document sequence size",
                    ));
                }
                let section_end = section_start
                    .checked_add(section_size as usize)
                    .filter(|end| *end <= frame.len())
                    .ok_or_else(|| {
                        io::Error::new(
                            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                            "MongoDB document sequence exceeds frame",
                        )
                    })?;
                offset += 4;
                let nul = frame[offset..section_end]
                    .iter()
                    .position(|byte| *byte == 0)
                    .ok_or_else(|| {
                        io::Error::new(
                            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                            "MongoDB document sequence identifier is unterminated",
                        )
                    })?;
                let identifier = std::str::from_utf8(&frame[offset..offset + nul])
                    .map_err(|_| {
                        io::Error::new(
                            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                            "MongoDB document sequence identifier is not UTF-8",
                        )
                    })?
                    .to_owned();
                offset += nul + 1;
                let mut documents = Vec::new();
                while offset < section_end {
                    if documents.len() >= MAX_SEQUENCE_DOCUMENTS {
                        return Err(io::Error::new(
                            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                            "MongoDB document sequence exceeds document limit",
                        ));
                    }
                    let (document, consumed) = read_bson_document(frame, offset, section_end)?;
                    documents.push(document);
                    offset = consumed;
                }
                if offset != section_end {
                    return Err(io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                        "MongoDB document sequence boundary mismatch",
                    ));
                }
                sequences.push((identifier, documents));
            }
            other => {
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                    format!("unknown MongoDB OP_MSG section kind: {other}"),
                ))
            }
        }
    }
    let body = body.ok_or_else(|| {
        io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "MongoDB OP_MSG is missing its body section",
        )
    })?;
    Ok(MongoMessage {
        request_id,
        response_to,
        body,
        sequences,
    })
}

fn read_bson_document(frame: &[u8], offset: usize, limit: usize) -> io::Result<(Document, usize)> {
    if offset + 4 > limit {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "truncated BSON document length",
        ));
    }
    let length = i32::from_le_bytes(frame[offset..offset + 4].try_into().unwrap()) as usize;
    if length < 5 || offset.checked_add(length).is_none_or(|end| end > limit) {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "invalid BSON document length",
        ));
    }
    let end = offset + length;
    let document = bson::from_slice::<Document>(&frame[offset..end]).map_err(|error| {
        io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            format!("invalid MongoDB BSON document: {error}"),
        )
    })?;
    Ok((document, end))
}

fn merge_sequences(
    command: &mut Document,
    sequences: &[(String, Vec<Document>)],
) -> io::Result<()> {
    for (identifier, documents) in sequences {
        if !matches!(identifier.as_str(), "documents" | "updates" | "deletes") {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                format!("MongoDB document sequence {identifier} is not admitted"),
            ));
        }
        if command.contains_key(identifier) {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                format!("MongoDB command contains duplicate {identifier} payload"),
            ));
        }
        command.insert(
            identifier,
            Bson::Array(documents.iter().cloned().map(Bson::Document).collect()),
        );
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn encode_op_msg(request_id: i32, response_to: i32, body: &Document) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let bson = bson::to_vec(body).map_err(|error| {
        io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            format!("could not encode MongoDB BSON: {error}"),
        )
    })?;
    let length = 16usize
        .checked_add(4)
        .and_then(|value| value.checked_add(1))
        .and_then(|value| value.checked_add(bson.len()))
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                "MongoDB response length overflow",
            )
        })?;
    if length > MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
            "MongoDB response exceeds maximum wire message size",
        ));
    }
    let mut frame = Vec::with_capacity(length);
    frame.extend_from_slice(&(length as i32).to_le_bytes());
    frame.extend_from_slice(&request_id.to_le_bytes());
    frame.extend_from_slice(&response_to.to_le_bytes());
    frame.extend_from_slice(&OP_MSG.to_le_bytes());
    frame.extend_from_slice(&0i32.to_le_bytes());
    frame.push(0);
    frame.extend_from_slice(&bson);
    Ok(frame)
}

async fn send_command_error(
    stream: &mut BoxedMongoIo,
    request_id: i32,
    database: &str,
    message: &str,
) -> io::Result<()> {
    let mut response = Document::new();
    response.insert("ok", 0.0);
    response.insert("errmsg", message);
    response.insert("code", 13);
    response.insert("codeName", "Unauthorized");
    response.insert("$db", database);
    let frame = encode_op_msg(request_id.wrapping_add(1), request_id, &response)?;
    write_frame(stream, &frame).await
}

async fn write_frame(stream: &mut BoxedMongoIo, frame: &[u8]) -> io::Result<()> {
    stream.write_all(frame).await?;
    stream.flush().await
}

fn scram_username(command: &Document) -> io::Result<String> {
    let payload = match command.get("payload") {
        Some(Bson::Binary(binary)) => &binary.bytes,
        _ => {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                "SCRAM saslStart payload must be BSON binary",
            ))
        }
    };
    let payload = std::str::from_utf8(payload).map_err(|_| {
        io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "SCRAM client-first message is not UTF-8",
        )
    })?;
    let attributes = payload.strip_prefix("n,,").ok_or_else(|| {
        io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
            "SCRAM channel binding is not supported by this gateway",
        )
    })?;
    let encoded = attributes
        .split(',')
        .find_map(|attribute| attribute.strip_prefix("n="))
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                "SCRAM client-first message has no username",
            )
        })?;
    let username = encoded.replace("=2C", ",").replace("=3D", "=");
    if username.is_empty() || username.len() > 128 {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "SCRAM username is invalid",
        ));
    }
    Ok(username)
}

fn build_server_tls(cert_path: &str, key_path: &str) -> io::Result<TlsAcceptor> {
    let certs = load_certs(cert_path)?;
    let key = load_private_key(key_path)?;
    let config = ServerConfig::builder()
        .with_no_client_auth()
        .with_single_cert(certs, key)
        .map_err(|error| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                format!("invalid MongoDB gateway TLS certificate/key: {error}"),
            )
        })?;
    Ok(TlsAcceptor::from(Arc::new(config)))
}

fn build_client_tls(ca_path: &str) -> io::Result<TlsConnector> {
    let mut roots = RootCertStore::empty();
    for certificate in load_certs(ca_path)? {
        roots.add(certificate).map_err(|error| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                format!("invalid MongoDB upstream CA: {error}"),
            )
        })?;
    }
    let config = ClientConfig::builder()
        .with_root_certificates(roots)
        .with_no_client_auth();
    Ok(TlsConnector::from(Arc::new(config)))
}

fn load_certs(path: &str) -> io::Result<Vec<CertificateDer<'static>>> {
    let file = File::open(path)?;
    rustls_pemfile::certs(&mut BufReader::new(file))
        .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
        .map_err(|error| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                format!("invalid certificate PEM: {error}"),
            )
        })
}

fn load_private_key(path: &str) -> io::Result<PrivateKeyDer<'static>> {
    rustls_pemfile::private_key(&mut BufReader::new(File::open(path)?))
        .map_err(|error| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                format!("invalid private key PEM: {error}"),
            )
        })?
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                "MongoDB gateway private key is missing",
            )
        })
}

fn env_required(name: &str) -> io::Result<String> {
    std::env::var(name)
        .map_err(|_| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, format!("{name} is required")))
        .and_then(|value| {
            if value.trim().is_empty() {
                Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                    format!("{name} cannot be empty"),
                ))
            } else {
                Ok(value)
            }
        })
}

fn env_required_or(name: &str, default: &str) -> io::Result<String> {
    Ok(std::env::var(name).unwrap_or_else(|_| default.to_owned()))
}

fn env_optional(name: &str) -> Option<String> {
    std::env::var(name)
        .ok()
        .filter(|value| !value.trim().is_empty())
}

fn env_bool(name: &str, default: bool) -> io::Result<bool> {
    match std::env::var(name) {
        Ok(value) => match value.trim().to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
            "true" | "1" | "yes" | "on" => Ok(true),
            "false" | "0" | "no" | "off" => Ok(false),
            other => Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                format!("invalid {name}: {other}"),
            )),
        },
        Err(_) => Ok(default),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use mongodb::bson::doc;

    #[test]
    fn op_msg_round_trip_preserves_command_document() {
        let command =
            doc! { "find": "records", "filter": { "active": true }, "$db": "tenant_a_db" };
        let frame = encode_op_msg(7, 0, &command).unwrap();
        let parsed = decode_op_msg(&frame).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(parsed.request_id, 7);
        assert_eq!(parsed.body, command);
    }

    #[test]
    fn hello_reply_removes_upstream_topology_addresses() {
        let mut reply = doc! {
            "ok": 1.0,
            "isWritablePrimary": true,
            "setName": "rs0",
            "hosts": ["mongo-a.internal:27017", "mongo-b.internal:27017"],
            "primary": "mongo-a.internal:27017",
            "topologyVersion": { "counter": 1_i64 }
        };
        for field in [
            "hosts",
            "passives",
            "arbiters",
            "primary",
            "setName",
            "setVersion",
            "electionId",
            "topologyVersion",
            "me",
            "msg",
            "serviceId",
        ] {
            reply.remove(field);
        }
        assert!(reply.get("hosts").is_none());
        assert!(reply.get("setName").is_none());
        assert_eq!(reply.get_bool("isWritablePrimary").unwrap(), true);
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_non_op_msg_and_checksum_flags() {
        let mut frame = encode_op_msg(7, 0, &Document::new()).unwrap();
        frame[12..16].copy_from_slice(&2004i32.to_le_bytes());
        assert!(decode_op_msg(&frame).is_err());

        let mut frame = encode_op_msg(7, 0, &Document::new()).unwrap();
        frame[16..20].copy_from_slice(&1i32.to_le_bytes());
        assert!(decode_op_msg(&frame).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn merges_insert_document_sequences_for_policy_validation() {
        let mut command = doc! { "insert": "records", "$db": "tenant_a_db" };
        merge_sequences(
            &mut command,
            &[("documents".into(), vec![doc! { "secret": "a" }])],
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(command.get_array("documents").unwrap().len(), 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn scram_username_is_decoded_from_client_first_message() {
        let command = doc! {
            "saslStart": 1,
            "mechanism": SCRAM_SHA_256,
            "payload": bson::Binary { subtype: bson::spec::BinarySubtype::Generic, bytes: b"n,,n=tenant=2Ca,r=nonce".to_vec() }
        };
        assert_eq!(scram_username(&command).unwrap(), "tenant,a");
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\mongodb_policy.rs ==========
use mongodb::bson::{self, Bson, Document};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::{
    atomic::{AtomicI64, Ordering},
    Mutex,
};
use uuid::Uuid;

const RESERVED_TENANT_FIELD: &str = "_proxima_tenant_id";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum MongoPolicyError {
    InvalidTenant,
    InvalidDatabase,
    MissingDatabase,
    WrongDatabase,
    MissingCommand,
    UnsupportedCommand(String),
    InvalidCommand(String),
    UnsafeOperator(String),
    UnsafePipelineStage(String),
    CrossDatabaseStage,
    CrossTenantSession,
    UnknownSession,
    CrossTenantCursor,
    UnknownCursor,
    CursorCollision,
    RegistryUnavailable,
}

impl std::fmt::Display for MongoPolicyError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::InvalidTenant => write!(f, "invalid MongoDB tenant identifier"),
            Self::InvalidDatabase => write!(f, "invalid MongoDB database name"),
            Self::MissingDatabase => write!(f, "MongoDB command is missing $db"),
            Self::WrongDatabase => write!(
                f,
                "MongoDB command targets a database outside the authenticated tenant"
            ),
            Self::MissingCommand => write!(f, "MongoDB command document is empty"),
            Self::UnsupportedCommand(name) => write!(f, "MongoDB command is not admitted: {name}"),
            Self::InvalidCommand(reason) => write!(f, "invalid MongoDB command: {reason}"),
            Self::UnsafeOperator(name) => write!(f, "MongoDB operator is not admitted: {name}"),
            Self::UnsafePipelineStage(name) => {
                write!(f, "MongoDB aggregation stage is not admitted: {name}")
            }
            Self::CrossDatabaseStage => {
                write!(f, "cross-database aggregation references are denied")
            }
            Self::CrossTenantSession => write!(f, "MongoDB session belongs to another tenant"),
            Self::UnknownSession => write!(f, "MongoDB session is not registered"),
            Self::CrossTenantCursor => {
                write!(f, "MongoDB cursor belongs to another tenant or namespace")
            }
            Self::UnknownCursor => write!(f, "MongoDB cursor is not registered"),
            Self::CursorCollision => {
                write!(f, "could not allocate a unique MongoDB cursor identifier")
            }
            Self::RegistryUnavailable => {
                write!(f, "MongoDB session or cursor registry is unavailable")
            }
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for MongoPolicyError {}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum MongoCommandKind {
    Find,
    Aggregate,
    Insert,
    Update,
    Delete,
    Count,
    Distinct,
    FindAndModify,
    GetMore,
    KillCursors,
    ListCollections,
    ListIndexes,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct MongoTenantPolicy {
    tenant_id: String,
    database: String,
}

impl MongoTenantPolicy {
    pub fn new(
        tenant_id: impl Into<String>,
        database: impl Into<String>,
    ) -> Result<Self, MongoPolicyError> {
        let tenant_id = tenant_id.into();
        let database = database.into();
        if tenant_id.is_empty()
            || tenant_id.len() > 128
            || !tenant_id
                .bytes()
                .all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || matches!(b, b'-' | b'_' | b'.'))
        {
            return Err(MongoPolicyError::InvalidTenant);
        }
        if !valid_database_name(&database) {
            return Err(MongoPolicyError::InvalidDatabase);
        }
        Ok(Self {
            tenant_id,
            database,
        })
    }

    pub fn tenant_id(&self) -> &str {
        &self.tenant_id
    }
    pub fn database(&self) -> &str {
        &self.database
    }

    /// Admits a deliberately small, fail-closed command surface. Each command
    /// must target the principal's one assigned database. Cross-database
    /// aggregation and server-administration commands are never forwarded.
    pub fn authorize(&self, command: &Document) -> Result<MongoCommandKind, MongoPolicyError> {
        let (name, value) = command
            .iter()
            .next()
            .ok_or(MongoPolicyError::MissingCommand)?;
        let database = command
            .get_str("$db")
            .map_err(|_| MongoPolicyError::MissingDatabase)?;
        if database != self.database {
            return Err(MongoPolicyError::WrongDatabase);
        }

        match name.as_str() {
            "find" => {
                validate_collection(value, name)?;
                validate_optional_query(command, "filter")?;
                Ok(MongoCommandKind::Find)
            }
            "aggregate" => {
                validate_collection(value, name)?;
                let pipeline = command.get_array("pipeline").map_err(|_| {
                    MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("aggregate.pipeline must be an array".into())
                })?;
                validate_pipeline(pipeline, &self.database)?;
                Ok(MongoCommandKind::Aggregate)
            }
            "insert" => {
                validate_collection(value, name)?;
                let documents = command.get_array("documents").map_err(|_| {
                    MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("insert.documents must be an array".into())
                })?;
                if documents.is_empty() {
                    return Err(MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(
                        "insert.documents cannot be empty".into(),
                    ));
                }
                for document in documents {
                    let document = document.as_document().ok_or_else(|| {
                        MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(
                            "insert document must be a BSON document".into(),
                        )
                    })?;
                    validate_query_document(document)?;
                    if document.contains_key(RESERVED_TENANT_FIELD) {
                        return Err(MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(
                            "reserved tenant metadata cannot be supplied by clients".into(),
                        ));
                    }
                }
                Ok(MongoCommandKind::Insert)
            }
            "update" => {
                validate_collection(value, name)?;
                let updates = command.get_array("updates").map_err(|_| {
                    MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("update.updates must be an array".into())
                })?;
                for update in updates {
                    let update = update.as_document().ok_or_else(|| {
                        MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("update entry must be a document".into())
                    })?;
                    let filter = update.get_document("q").map_err(|_| {
                        MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(
                            "update entry is missing q document".into(),
                        )
                    })?;
                    validate_query_document(filter)?;
                    if update.get_bool("upsert").unwrap_or(false) {
                        return Err(MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(
                            "upsert is denied until tenant-safe upsert semantics are enabled"
                                .into(),
                        ));
                    }
                    validate_update_value(
                        update.get("u").ok_or_else(|| {
                            MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("update entry is missing u".into())
                        })?,
                        &self.database,
                    )?;
                }
                Ok(MongoCommandKind::Update)
            }
            "delete" => {
                validate_collection(value, name)?;
                let deletes = command.get_array("deletes").map_err(|_| {
                    MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("delete.deletes must be an array".into())
                })?;
                for delete in deletes {
                    let delete = delete.as_document().ok_or_else(|| {
                        MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("delete entry must be a document".into())
                    })?;
                    validate_query_document(delete.get_document("q").map_err(|_| {
                        MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(
                            "delete entry is missing q document".into(),
                        )
                    })?)?;
                }
                Ok(MongoCommandKind::Delete)
            }
            "count" | "distinct" => {
                validate_collection(value, name)?;
                validate_optional_query(command, "query")?;
                if name == "count" {
                    Ok(MongoCommandKind::Count)
                } else {
                    Ok(MongoCommandKind::Distinct)
                }
            }
            "findAndModify" => {
                validate_collection(value, name)?;
                if let Ok(query) = command.get_document("query") {
                    validate_query_document(query)?;
                }
                if command.get_bool("upsert").unwrap_or(false) {
                    return Err(MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(
                        "findAndModify upsert is denied".into(),
                    ));
                }
                if let Some(update) = command.get("update") {
                    validate_update_value(update, &self.database)?;
                }
                Ok(MongoCommandKind::FindAndModify)
            }
            "getMore" => {
                validate_collection(
                    command.get("collection").ok_or_else(|| {
                        MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("getMore.collection is required".into())
                    })?,
                    "getMore",
                )?;
                if command.get_i64("getMore").is_err() {
                    return Err(MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(
                        "getMore requires an int64 cursor id".into(),
                    ));
                }
                Ok(MongoCommandKind::GetMore)
            }
            "killCursors" => {
                validate_collection(value, name)?;
                let cursors = command.get_array("cursors").map_err(|_| {
                    MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("killCursors.cursors must be an array".into())
                })?;
                if cursors.iter().any(|cursor| cursor.as_i64().is_none()) {
                    return Err(MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(
                        "killCursors cursor ids must be int64".into(),
                    ));
                }
                Ok(MongoCommandKind::KillCursors)
            }
            "listCollections" => Ok(MongoCommandKind::ListCollections),
            "listIndexes" => {
                validate_collection(value, name)?;
                Ok(MongoCommandKind::ListIndexes)
            }
            other => Err(MongoPolicyError::UnsupportedCommand(other.to_owned())),
        }
    }

    /// Optional defense-in-depth for schemas that reserve a tenant metadata
    /// field. Existing databases must be migrated before enabling this mode.
    pub fn stamp_insert_document(&self, document: &mut Document) -> Result<(), MongoPolicyError> {
        if document.contains_key(RESERVED_TENANT_FIELD) {
            return Err(MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(
                "reserved tenant metadata cannot be supplied by clients".into(),
            ));
        }
        document.insert(RESERVED_TENANT_FIELD, self.tenant_id.clone());
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn tenant_filter(&self) -> Document {
        bson::doc! { RESERVED_TENANT_FIELD: &self.tenant_id }
    }
}

fn valid_database_name(value: &str) -> bool {
    !value.is_empty()
        && value.len() <= 63
        && !value
            .chars()
            .any(|c| matches!(c, '/' | '\\' | '.' | ' ' | '"' | '$' | '\0'))
        && value != "admin"
        && value != "local"
        && value != "config"
}

fn validate_collection(value: &Bson, command: &str) -> Result<(), MongoPolicyError> {
    let name = value.as_str().ok_or_else(|| {
        MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(format!("{command} must name a collection"))
    })?;
    if name.is_empty()
        || name.len() > 120
        || name.starts_with("system.")
        || name.contains('$')
        || name.contains('\0')
    {
        return Err(MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(format!(
            "{command} uses a reserved or invalid collection name"
        )));
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn validate_optional_query(command: &Document, field: &str) -> Result<(), MongoPolicyError> {
    if let Some(value) = command.get(field) {
        let document = value.as_document().ok_or_else(|| {
            MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(format!("{field} must be a BSON document"))
        })?;
        validate_query_document(document)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn validate_query_document(document: &Document) -> Result<(), MongoPolicyError> {
    for (key, value) in document {
        if matches!(
            key.as_str(),
            "$where"
                | "$function"
                | "$accumulator"
                | "$out"
                | "$merge"
                | "$changeStream"
                | "$currentOp"
                | "$listSessions"
                | "$listLocalSessions"
        ) {
            return Err(MongoPolicyError::UnsafeOperator(key.clone()));
        }
        validate_nested_value(value)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn validate_nested_value(value: &Bson) -> Result<(), MongoPolicyError> {
    match value {
        Bson::Document(document) => validate_query_document(document),
        Bson::Array(values) => {
            for value in values {
                validate_nested_value(value)?;
            }
            Ok(())
        }
        _ => Ok(()),
    }
}

fn validate_update_value(value: &Bson, tenant_database: &str) -> Result<(), MongoPolicyError> {
    match value {
        Bson::Document(document) => {
            for (operator, payload) in document {
                if operator == RESERVED_TENANT_FIELD
                    || operator.ends_with(&format!(".{RESERVED_TENANT_FIELD}"))
                {
                    return Err(MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(
                        "updates cannot modify reserved tenant metadata".into(),
                    ));
                }
                if matches!(
                    operator.as_str(),
                    "$where" | "$function" | "$accumulator" | "$rename"
                ) {
                    return Err(MongoPolicyError::UnsafeOperator(operator.clone()));
                }
                if let Bson::Document(fields) = payload {
                    if fields.keys().any(|field| {
                        field == RESERVED_TENANT_FIELD
                            || field.ends_with(&format!(".{RESERVED_TENANT_FIELD}"))
                    }) {
                        return Err(MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(
                            "updates cannot modify reserved tenant metadata".into(),
                        ));
                    }
                }
                validate_nested_value(payload)?;
            }
            Ok(())
        }
        Bson::Array(pipeline) => validate_pipeline(pipeline, tenant_database),
        _ => Err(MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(
            "update.u must be a document or aggregation pipeline".into(),
        )),
    }
}

fn validate_pipeline(pipeline: &[Bson], tenant_database: &str) -> Result<(), MongoPolicyError> {
    for stage in pipeline {
        let stage = stage.as_document().ok_or_else(|| {
            MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("aggregation stages must be documents".into())
        })?;
        if stage.len() != 1 {
            return Err(MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(
                "each aggregation stage must contain exactly one operator".into(),
            ));
        }
        let (name, value) = stage.iter().next().unwrap();
        match name.as_str() {
            "$match" | "$redact" => {
                let document = value.as_document().ok_or_else(|| {
                    MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(format!("{name} must contain a document"))
                })?;
                validate_query_document(document)?;
            }
            "$project" | "$addFields" | "$set" | "$unset" | "$sort" | "$limit" | "$skip"
            | "$unwind" | "$replaceRoot" | "$replaceWith" | "$group" | "$count" | "$bucket"
            | "$bucketAuto" | "$sortByCount" | "$sample" | "$setWindowFields" | "$densify"
            | "$fill" => {
                validate_nested_value(value)?;
            }
            "$facet" => {
                let facets = value.as_document().ok_or_else(|| {
                    MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("$facet must contain named pipelines".into())
                })?;
                for pipeline in facets.values() {
                    let pipeline = pipeline.as_array().ok_or_else(|| {
                        MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("$facet values must be arrays".into())
                    })?;
                    validate_pipeline(pipeline, tenant_database)?;
                }
            }
            "$lookup" => {
                let lookup = value.as_document().ok_or_else(|| {
                    MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("$lookup must be a document".into())
                })?;
                validate_foreign_namespace(lookup.get("from"), tenant_database)?;
                if let Some(variables) = lookup.get("let") {
                    let variables = variables.as_document().ok_or_else(|| {
                        MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("$lookup.let must be a document".into())
                    })?;
                    validate_nested_value(&Bson::Document(variables.clone()))?;
                }
                if let Some(pipeline) = lookup.get("pipeline") {
                    let pipeline = pipeline.as_array().ok_or_else(|| {
                        MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("$lookup.pipeline must be an array".into())
                    })?;
                    validate_pipeline(pipeline, tenant_database)?;
                }
            }
            "$unionWith" => match value {
                Bson::String(collection) => {
                    validate_collection(&Bson::String(collection.clone()), "$unionWith")?
                }
                Bson::Document(union) => {
                    validate_foreign_namespace(
                        union.get("coll").or_else(|| union.get("from")),
                        tenant_database,
                    )?;
                    if let Some(pipeline) = union.get("pipeline") {
                        let pipeline = pipeline.as_array().ok_or_else(|| {
                            MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(
                                "$unionWith.pipeline must be an array".into(),
                            )
                        })?;
                        validate_pipeline(pipeline, tenant_database)?;
                    }
                }
                _ => {
                    return Err(MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(
                        "$unionWith must name a collection".into(),
                    ))
                }
            },
            "$graphLookup" => {
                let graph = value.as_document().ok_or_else(|| {
                    MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("$graphLookup must be a document".into())
                })?;
                validate_foreign_namespace(graph.get("from"), tenant_database)?;
                if let Some(filter) = graph.get("restrictSearchWithMatch") {
                    let filter = filter.as_document().ok_or_else(|| {
                        MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(
                            "$graphLookup.restrictSearchWithMatch must be a document".into(),
                        )
                    })?;
                    validate_query_document(filter)?;
                }
            }
            "$geoNear" => {
                let geo = value.as_document().ok_or_else(|| {
                    MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("$geoNear must be a document".into())
                })?;
                if let Some(query) = geo.get("query") {
                    let query = query.as_document().ok_or_else(|| {
                        MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("$geoNear.query must be a document".into())
                    })?;
                    validate_query_document(query)?;
                }
            }
            "$out" | "$merge" | "$changeStream" | "$currentOp" | "$indexStats" | "$collStats"
            | "$planCacheStats" | "$listSessions" | "$listLocalSessions" | "$search"
            | "$vectorSearch" | "$documents" | "$function" | "$accumulator" => {
                return Err(MongoPolicyError::UnsafePipelineStage(name.clone()));
            }
            other => return Err(MongoPolicyError::UnsafePipelineStage(other.to_owned())),
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn validate_foreign_namespace(
    value: Option<&Bson>,
    tenant_database: &str,
) -> Result<(), MongoPolicyError> {
    let value = value.ok_or_else(|| {
        MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("aggregation foreign namespace is missing".into())
    })?;
    match value {
        Bson::String(collection) => {
            validate_collection(&Bson::String(collection.clone()), "aggregation namespace")
        }
        Bson::Document(namespace) => {
            if namespace
                .get_str("db")
                .is_ok_and(|db| db != tenant_database)
            {
                return Err(MongoPolicyError::CrossDatabaseStage);
            }
            let collection = namespace
                .get("coll")
                .or_else(|| namespace.get("collection"));
            validate_collection(
                collection.ok_or_else(|| {
                    MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("aggregation namespace is missing coll".into())
                })?,
                "aggregation namespace",
            )
        }
        _ => Err(MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(
            "aggregation namespace must be a collection or namespace document".into(),
        )),
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
struct CursorBinding {
    tenant_id: String,
    database: String,
    collection: String,
    upstream_cursor_id: i64,
}

pub struct MongoCursorRegistry {
    next_id: AtomicI64,
    cursors: Mutex<HashMap<i64, CursorBinding>>,
}

impl Default for MongoCursorRegistry {
    fn default() -> Self {
        let seed = (Uuid::new_v4().as_u128() as u64 & i64::MAX as u64).max(1) as i64;
        Self {
            next_id: AtomicI64::new(seed),
            cursors: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()),
        }
    }
}

impl MongoCursorRegistry {
    pub fn register(
        &self,
        tenant_id: &str,
        database: &str,
        collection: &str,
        upstream_cursor_id: i64,
    ) -> Result<i64, MongoPolicyError> {
        let mut cursors = self
            .cursors
            .lock()
            .map_err(|_| MongoPolicyError::RegistryUnavailable)?;
        for _ in 0..32 {
            let candidate = self.next_id.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed).max(1);
            if let std::collections::hash_map::Entry::Vacant(entry) = cursors.entry(candidate) {
                entry.insert(CursorBinding {
                    tenant_id: tenant_id.to_owned(),
                    database: database.to_owned(),
                    collection: collection.to_owned(),
                    upstream_cursor_id,
                });
                return Ok(candidate);
            }
        }
        Err(MongoPolicyError::CursorCollision)
    }

    pub fn resolve_get_more(
        &self,
        tenant_id: &str,
        database: &str,
        collection: &str,
        client_cursor_id: i64,
    ) -> Result<i64, MongoPolicyError> {
        let cursors = self
            .cursors
            .lock()
            .map_err(|_| MongoPolicyError::RegistryUnavailable)?;
        let binding = cursors
            .get(&client_cursor_id)
            .ok_or(MongoPolicyError::UnknownCursor)?;
        if binding.tenant_id != tenant_id
            || binding.database != database
            || binding.collection != collection
        {
            return Err(MongoPolicyError::CrossTenantCursor);
        }
        Ok(binding.upstream_cursor_id)
    }

    pub fn authorize_kill(
        &self,
        tenant_id: &str,
        database: &str,
        collection: &str,
        client_cursor_ids: &[i64],
    ) -> Result<Vec<i64>, MongoPolicyError> {
        let mut cursors = self
            .cursors
            .lock()
            .map_err(|_| MongoPolicyError::RegistryUnavailable)?;
        let mut upstream_ids = Vec::with_capacity(client_cursor_ids.len());
        for id in client_cursor_ids {
            let binding = cursors.get(id).ok_or(MongoPolicyError::UnknownCursor)?;
            if binding.tenant_id != tenant_id
                || binding.database != database
                || binding.collection != collection
            {
                return Err(MongoPolicyError::CrossTenantCursor);
            }
            upstream_ids.push(binding.upstream_cursor_id);
        }
        for id in client_cursor_ids {
            cursors.remove(id);
        }
        Ok(upstream_ids)
    }

    pub fn release(&self, tenant_id: &str, client_cursor_id: i64) -> Result<i64, MongoPolicyError> {
        let mut cursors = self
            .cursors
            .lock()
            .map_err(|_| MongoPolicyError::RegistryUnavailable)?;
        let binding = cursors
            .get(&client_cursor_id)
            .ok_or(MongoPolicyError::UnknownCursor)?;
        if binding.tenant_id != tenant_id {
            return Err(MongoPolicyError::CrossTenantCursor);
        }
        Ok(cursors
            .remove(&client_cursor_id)
            .expect("cursor was checked")
            .upstream_cursor_id)
    }
}

const SESSION_IDLE_TTL: std::time::Duration = std::time::Duration::from_secs(30 * 60);
const MAX_SESSION_BINDINGS: usize = 100_000;

struct SessionBinding {
    tenant_id: String,
    last_seen: std::time::Instant,
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct MongoSessionRegistry {
    sessions: Mutex<HashMap<Vec<u8>, SessionBinding>>,
}

impl MongoSessionRegistry {
    fn key(lsid: &Document) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MongoPolicyError> {
        bson::to_vec(lsid)
            .map_err(|_| MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand("lsid could not be serialized".into()))
    }

    fn prune_expired(sessions: &mut HashMap<Vec<u8>, SessionBinding>, now: std::time::Instant) {
        sessions.retain(|_, binding| {
            now.saturating_duration_since(binding.last_seen) < SESSION_IDLE_TTL
        });
    }

    pub fn bind(&self, tenant_id: &str, lsid: &Document) -> Result<(), MongoPolicyError> {
        let key = Self::key(lsid)?;
        let now = std::time::Instant::now();
        let mut sessions = self
            .sessions
            .lock()
            .map_err(|_| MongoPolicyError::RegistryUnavailable)?;
        Self::prune_expired(&mut sessions, now);
        match sessions.get_mut(&key) {
            Some(binding) if binding.tenant_id != tenant_id => {
                Err(MongoPolicyError::CrossTenantSession)
            }
            Some(binding) => {
                binding.last_seen = now;
                Ok(())
            }
            None => {
                if sessions.len() >= MAX_SESSION_BINDINGS {
                    return Err(MongoPolicyError::RegistryUnavailable);
                }
                sessions.insert(
                    key,
                    SessionBinding {
                        tenant_id: tenant_id.to_owned(),
                        last_seen: now,
                    },
                );
                Ok(())
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn authorize(&self, tenant_id: &str, lsid: &Document) -> Result<(), MongoPolicyError> {
        let key = Self::key(lsid)?;
        let now = std::time::Instant::now();
        let mut sessions = self
            .sessions
            .lock()
            .map_err(|_| MongoPolicyError::RegistryUnavailable)?;
        Self::prune_expired(&mut sessions, now);
        match sessions.get_mut(&key) {
            Some(binding) if binding.tenant_id == tenant_id => {
                binding.last_seen = now;
                Ok(())
            }
            Some(_) => Err(MongoPolicyError::CrossTenantSession),
            None => Err(MongoPolicyError::UnknownSession),
        }
    }

    pub fn release_if_owned(
        &self,
        tenant_id: &str,
        lsid: &Document,
    ) -> Result<(), MongoPolicyError> {
        let key = Self::key(lsid)?;
        let now = std::time::Instant::now();
        let mut sessions = self
            .sessions
            .lock()
            .map_err(|_| MongoPolicyError::RegistryUnavailable)?;
        Self::prune_expired(&mut sessions, now);
        match sessions.get(&key) {
            Some(binding) if binding.tenant_id != tenant_id => {
                Err(MongoPolicyError::CrossTenantSession)
            }
            Some(_) => {
                sessions.remove(&key);
                Ok(())
            }
            None => Ok(()),
        }
    }

    pub fn release(&self, tenant_id: &str, lsid: &Document) -> Result<(), MongoPolicyError> {
        let key = Self::key(lsid)?;
        let now = std::time::Instant::now();
        let mut sessions = self
            .sessions
            .lock()
            .map_err(|_| MongoPolicyError::RegistryUnavailable)?;
        Self::prune_expired(&mut sessions, now);
        match sessions.get(&key) {
            Some(binding) if binding.tenant_id != tenant_id => {
                Err(MongoPolicyError::CrossTenantSession)
            }
            Some(_) => {
                sessions.remove(&key);
                Ok(())
            }
            None => Err(MongoPolicyError::UnknownSession),
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use mongodb::bson::doc;

    fn policy() -> MongoTenantPolicy {
        MongoTenantPolicy::new("tenant-a", "tenant_a_db").unwrap()
    }

    fn command(name: &str, value: Bson) -> Document {
        let mut command = Document::new();
        command.insert(name, value);
        command.insert("$db", "tenant_a_db");
        command
    }

    #[test]
    fn requires_exact_tenant_database() {
        let mut command = command("find", Bson::String("records".into()));
        command.insert("$db", "tenant_b_db");
        assert_eq!(
            policy().authorize(&command),
            Err(MongoPolicyError::WrongDatabase)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn denies_admin_and_unknown_commands() {
        for name in [
            "listDatabases",
            "dropDatabase",
            "createUser",
            "mapReduce",
            "eval",
            "renameCollection",
        ] {
            let command = command(name, Bson::Int32(1));
            assert!(matches!(
                policy().authorize(&command),
                Err(MongoPolicyError::UnsupportedCommand(_))
            ));
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn allows_scoped_find_and_rejects_javascript_predicates() {
        let mut safe = command("find", Bson::String("records".into()));
        safe.insert("filter", doc! { "status": "active" });
        assert_eq!(policy().authorize(&safe), Ok(MongoCommandKind::Find));

        let mut unsafe_query = command("find", Bson::String("records".into()));
        unsafe_query.insert("filter", doc! { "$where": "return true" });
        assert!(matches!(
            policy().authorize(&unsafe_query),
            Err(MongoPolicyError::UnsafeOperator(_))
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn aggregate_allows_safe_pipeline_and_rejects_cross_database_lookup() {
        let mut safe = command("aggregate", Bson::String("records".into()));
        safe.insert(
            "pipeline",
            vec![
                doc! { "$match": { "status": "active" } },
                doc! { "$limit": 5 },
            ],
        );
        assert_eq!(policy().authorize(&safe), Ok(MongoCommandKind::Aggregate));

        let mut cross_db = command("aggregate", Bson::String("records".into()));
        cross_db.insert("pipeline", vec![doc! { "$lookup": { "from": { "db": "tenant_b_db", "coll": "records" }, "pipeline": [], "as": "joined" } }]);
        assert_eq!(
            policy().authorize(&cross_db),
            Err(MongoPolicyError::CrossDatabaseStage)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn aggregate_validates_nested_lookup_and_graph_filters() {
        let mut lookup = command("aggregate", Bson::String("records".into()));
        lookup.insert(
            "pipeline",
            vec![doc! {
                "$lookup": {
                    "from": "records",
                    "let": { "unsafe_value": { "$function": {
                        "body": "function() { return true; }",
                        "args": [],
                        "lang": "js"
                    } } },
                    "pipeline": [],
                    "as": "joined"
                }
            }],
        );
        assert!(matches!(
            policy().authorize(&lookup),
            Err(MongoPolicyError::UnsafeOperator(_))
        ));

        let mut graph = command("aggregate", Bson::String("records".into()));
        graph.insert(
            "pipeline",
            vec![doc! {
                "$graphLookup": {
                    "from": "records",
                    "startWith": "$parent",
                    "connectFromField": "parent",
                    "connectToField": "_id",
                    "as": "graph",
                    "restrictSearchWithMatch": { "$where": "return true" }
                }
            }],
        );
        assert!(matches!(
            policy().authorize(&graph),
            Err(MongoPolicyError::UnsafeOperator(_))
        ));

        let mut malformed_geo = command("aggregate", Bson::String("records".into()));
        malformed_geo.insert(
            "pipeline",
            vec![doc! {
                "$geoNear": { "near": {}, "distanceField": "distance", "query": "not-a-document" }
            }],
        );
        assert!(matches!(
            policy().authorize(&malformed_geo),
            Err(MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(_))
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn aggregate_rejects_output_and_change_stream_stages() {
        for stage in [
            doc! { "$out": "exfiltration" },
            doc! { "$merge": "exfiltration" },
            doc! { "$changeStream": {} },
        ] {
            let mut command = command("aggregate", Bson::String("records".into()));
            command.insert("pipeline", vec![stage]);
            assert!(matches!(
                policy().authorize(&command),
                Err(MongoPolicyError::UnsafePipelineStage(_))
            ));
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn update_upsert_and_reserved_tenant_field_mutations_are_denied() {
        let mut upsert = command("update", Bson::String("records".into()));
        upsert.insert(
            "updates",
            vec![doc! { "q": {}, "u": { "$set": { "x": 1 } }, "upsert": true }],
        );
        assert!(matches!(
            policy().authorize(&upsert),
            Err(MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(_))
        ));

        let mut reserved = command("update", Bson::String("records".into()));
        reserved.insert(
            "updates",
            vec![doc! { "q": {}, "u": { "$set": { "_proxima_tenant_id": "tenant-b" } } }],
        );
        assert!(matches!(
            policy().authorize(&reserved),
            Err(MongoPolicyError::InvalidCommand(_))
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn insert_metadata_is_stamped_by_policy_not_client() {
        let mut document = doc! { "value": "record" };
        policy().stamp_insert_document(&mut document).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(document.get_str(RESERVED_TENANT_FIELD).unwrap(), "tenant-a");

        let mut spoofed = Document::new();
        spoofed.insert(RESERVED_TENANT_FIELD, "tenant-b");
        assert!(policy().stamp_insert_document(&mut spoofed).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn session_ids_are_bound_to_one_tenant() {
        let registry = MongoSessionRegistry::default();
        let lsid = doc! { "id": bson::Binary { subtype: bson::spec::BinarySubtype::Uuid, bytes: Uuid::new_v4().as_bytes().to_vec() } };
        registry.bind("tenant-a", &lsid).unwrap();
        registry.authorize("tenant-a", &lsid).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            registry.authorize("tenant-b", &lsid),
            Err(MongoPolicyError::CrossTenantSession)
        );
        assert_eq!(
            registry.bind("tenant-b", &lsid),
            Err(MongoPolicyError::CrossTenantSession)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn ending_sessions_is_idempotent_but_never_cross_tenant() {
        let registry = MongoSessionRegistry::default();
        let lsid = doc! { "id": bson::Binary { subtype: bson::spec::BinarySubtype::Uuid, bytes: Uuid::new_v4().as_bytes().to_vec() } };
        registry.bind("tenant-a", &lsid).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            registry.release_if_owned("tenant-b", &lsid),
            Err(MongoPolicyError::CrossTenantSession)
        );
        registry.release_if_owned("tenant-a", &lsid).unwrap();
        registry.release_if_owned("tenant-a", &lsid).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            registry.authorize("tenant-a", &lsid),
            Err(MongoPolicyError::UnknownSession)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn cursor_ids_are_opaque_and_tenant_bound() {
        let registry = MongoCursorRegistry::default();
        let client_id = registry
            .register("tenant-a", "tenant_a_db", "records", 991)
            .unwrap();
        assert_ne!(client_id, 991);
        assert_eq!(
            registry
                .resolve_get_more("tenant-a", "tenant_a_db", "records", client_id)
                .unwrap(),
            991
        );
        assert_eq!(
            registry.resolve_get_more("tenant-b", "tenant_a_db", "records", client_id),
            Err(MongoPolicyError::CrossTenantCursor)
        );
        assert_eq!(
            registry.release("tenant-b", client_id),
            Err(MongoPolicyError::CrossTenantCursor)
        );
        assert_eq!(registry.release("tenant-a", client_id).unwrap(), 991);
    }

    #[test]
    fn kill_cursors_is_all_or_nothing_across_tenants() {
        let registry = MongoCursorRegistry::default();
        let a = registry
            .register("tenant-a", "tenant_a_db", "records", 1001)
            .unwrap();
        let b = registry
            .register("tenant-b", "tenant_b_db", "records", 2002)
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            registry.authorize_kill("tenant-a", "tenant_a_db", "records", &[a, b]),
            Err(MongoPolicyError::CrossTenantCursor)
        );
        assert_eq!(
            registry
                .resolve_get_more("tenant-a", "tenant_a_db", "records", a)
                .unwrap(),
            1001
        );
        assert_eq!(
            registry
                .resolve_get_more("tenant-b", "tenant_b_db", "records", b)
                .unwrap(),
            2002
        );
        assert_eq!(
            registry
                .authorize_kill("tenant-a", "tenant_a_db", "records", &[a])
                .unwrap(),
            vec![1001]
        );
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\mysql_gateway.rs ==========
use crate::mysql_identity::MysqlIdentityRegistry;
use crate::mysql_policy::classify_statement;
use crate::mysql_session::MysqlConnectionIdentity;
use crate::mysql_upstream::MysqlUpstream;

use futures_util::StreamExt;
use mysql_async::prelude::Queryable;
use mysql_async::{Conn, Row, Statement, Value as UpstreamValue};

use opensrv_mysql::{
    AsyncMysqlIntermediary, AsyncMysqlShim, Column, ColumnFlags, ColumnType, ErrorKind, InitWriter,
    IntermediaryOptions, OkResponse, ParamParser, QueryResultWriter, StatementMetaWriter,
    StatusFlags, ToMysqlValue, ValueInner,
};

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::io::{self, Write};
use std::net::SocketAddr;
use std::panic::{catch_unwind, AssertUnwindSafe};
use std::sync::Arc;

use tokio::net::TcpListener;
use tokio::sync::{Mutex, Semaphore};
use tokio::task;
use uuid::Uuid;

const SERVER_VERSION: &str = "8.0.0-agata-proxima";
const UPSTREAM_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_SECS: u64 = 10;
const QUERY_TIMEOUT_SECS: u64 = 30;
const DEFAULT_MAX_CONNECTIONS: usize = 128;

/// One upstream connection and its prepared statements belong to one
/// authenticated client session. The pool is retained for the lifetime
/// of the session; the checked-out connection is never returned to it.
struct MysqlUpstreamSession {
    _pool: MysqlUpstream,
    connection: Conn,
    statements: HashMap<u32, Statement>,
}

impl MysqlUpstreamSession {
    fn new(pool: MysqlUpstream, connection: Conn) -> Self {
        Self {
            _pool: pool,
            connection,
            statements: HashMap::new(),
        }
    }
}

/// One gateway session represents exactly one client connection.
///
/// The authenticated principal is immutable. The upstream connection
/// is established only after successful authentication and remains
/// bound to that principal for the session lifetime.
struct MysqlGatewaySession {
    registry: Arc<MysqlIdentityRegistry>,
    identity: MysqlConnectionIdentity,
    upstream: Mutex<Option<MysqlUpstreamSession>>,
    salt: [u8; 20],
}

impl MysqlGatewaySession {
    fn new(registry: Arc<MysqlIdentityRegistry>) -> Self {
        Self {
            registry,
            identity: MysqlConnectionIdentity::new(),
            upstream: Mutex::new(None),
            salt: {
                // Use raw UUID bytes rather than hexadecimal text so the
                // 20-byte MySQL nonce retains 160 bits of random material.
                let first = Uuid::new_v4();
                let second = Uuid::new_v4();
                let mut salt = [0u8; 20];
                salt[..16].copy_from_slice(first.as_bytes());
                salt[16..].copy_from_slice(&second.as_bytes()[..4]);
                salt
            },
        }
    }

    fn authenticated(&self) -> bool {
        self.identity.is_authenticated()
    }

    async fn upstream_ready(&self) -> bool {
        self.upstream.lock().await.is_some()
    }
}

/// Convert a protocol parameter into an owned mysql_async value.
///
/// Date/time binary parameter payloads are decoded according to the MySQL
/// binary protocol and rejected when their lengths or component ranges are invalid.
fn decode_date_payload(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<(u16, u8, u8), ()> {
    if bytes.is_empty() {
        return Ok((0, 0, 0));
    }
    if bytes.len() != 4 {
        return Err(());
    }
    let year = u16::from_le_bytes([bytes[0], bytes[1]]);
    let (month, day) = (bytes[2], bytes[3]);
    if month > 12 || day > 31 {
        return Err(());
    }
    Ok((year, month, day))
}

fn decode_datetime_payload(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<(u16, u8, u8, u8, u8, u8, u32), ()> {
    if bytes.is_empty() {
        return Ok((0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0));
    }
    if !matches!(bytes.len(), 4 | 7 | 11) {
        return Err(());
    }
    let (year, month, day) = decode_date_payload(&bytes[..4])?;
    let (hour, minute, second) = if bytes.len() >= 7 {
        (bytes[4], bytes[5], bytes[6])
    } else {
        (0, 0, 0)
    };
    let micros = if bytes.len() == 11 {
        u32::from_le_bytes(bytes[7..11].try_into().map_err(|_| ())?)
    } else {
        0
    };
    if hour > 23 || minute > 59 || second > 59 || micros > 999_999 {
        return Err(());
    }
    Ok((year, month, day, hour, minute, second, micros))
}

fn decode_time_payload(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<(bool, u32, u8, u8, u8, u32), ()> {
    if bytes.is_empty() {
        return Ok((false, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0));
    }
    if !matches!(bytes.len(), 8 | 12) || bytes[0] > 1 {
        return Err(());
    }
    let days = u32::from_le_bytes(bytes[1..5].try_into().map_err(|_| ())?);
    let (hours, minutes, seconds) = (bytes[5], bytes[6], bytes[7]);
    let micros = if bytes.len() == 12 {
        u32::from_le_bytes(bytes[8..12].try_into().map_err(|_| ())?)
    } else {
        0
    };
    if hours > 23 || minutes > 59 || seconds > 59 || micros > 999_999 {
        return Err(());
    }
    Ok((bytes[0] == 1, days, hours, minutes, seconds, micros))
}

fn convert_parameter(value: ValueInner<'_>) -> Result<UpstreamValue, ()> {
    match value {
        ValueInner::NULL => Ok(UpstreamValue::NULL),
        ValueInner::Bytes(bytes) => Ok(UpstreamValue::Bytes(bytes.to_vec())),
        ValueInner::Int(value) => Ok(UpstreamValue::Int(value)),
        ValueInner::UInt(value) => Ok(UpstreamValue::UInt(value)),
        ValueInner::Double(value) => Ok(UpstreamValue::Double(value)),
        ValueInner::Date(bytes) => {
            let (year, month, day) = decode_date_payload(bytes)?;
            Ok(UpstreamValue::Date(year, month, day, 0, 0, 0, 0))
        }
        ValueInner::Datetime(bytes) => {
            let (year, month, day, hour, minute, second, micros) = decode_datetime_payload(bytes)?;
            Ok(UpstreamValue::Date(
                year, month, day, hour, minute, second, micros,
            ))
        }
        ValueInner::Time(bytes) => {
            let (negative, days, hours, minutes, seconds, micros) = decode_time_payload(bytes)?;
            Ok(UpstreamValue::Time(
                negative, days, hours, minutes, seconds, micros,
            ))
        }
    }
}

/// opensrv-mysql 0.7.0's ParamParser contains unchecked parsing paths.
/// Catch parser panics at this protocol boundary so malformed client
/// packets cannot unwind through the gateway session task.
fn decode_parameters(params: ParamParser<'_>) -> Result<Vec<UpstreamValue>, ()> {
    catch_unwind(AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
        params
            .into_iter()
            .map(|parameter| convert_parameter(parameter.value.into_inner()))
            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
    }))
    .unwrap_or(Err(()))
}

/// A typed row value that implements opensrv-mysql's output encoder.
/// This avoids converting BLOBs to UTF-8 or hex text and emits the proper
/// MySQL binary representation for temporal values in prepared results.
#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
enum MysqlOutputValue {
    Bytes(Vec<u8>),
    Int(i64),
    UInt(u64),
    Float(f32),
    Double(f64),
    Date(u16, u8, u8, u8, u8, u8, u32),
    Time(bool, u32, u8, u8, u8, u32),
}

impl ToMysqlValue for MysqlOutputValue {
    fn to_mysql_text<W: Write>(&self, writer: &mut W) -> io::Result<()> {
        match self {
            Self::Bytes(value) => value.to_mysql_text(writer),
            Self::Int(value) => value.to_mysql_text(writer),
            Self::UInt(value) => value.to_mysql_text(writer),
            Self::Float(value) => value.to_mysql_text(writer),
            Self::Double(value) => value.to_mysql_text(writer),
            Self::Date(y, m, d, h, min, sec, micros) => {
                let text = if *micros == 0 {
                    format!("{y:04}-{m:02}-{d:02} {h:02}:{min:02}:{sec:02}")
                } else {
                    format!("{y:04}-{m:02}-{d:02} {h:02}:{min:02}:{sec:02}.{micros:06}")
                };
                writer.write_all(text.as_bytes())
            }
            Self::Time(negative, days, hours, minutes, seconds, micros) => {
                let total_hours = u64::from(*days) * 24 + u64::from(*hours);
                let sign = if *negative { "-" } else { "" };
                let text = if *micros == 0 {
                    format!("{sign}{total_hours:02}:{minutes:02}:{seconds:02}")
                } else {
                    format!("{sign}{total_hours:02}:{minutes:02}:{seconds:02}.{micros:06}")
                };
                writer.write_all(text.as_bytes())
            }
        }
    }

    fn to_mysql_bin<W: Write>(&self, writer: &mut W, column: &Column) -> io::Result<()> {
        match self {
            Self::Bytes(value) => value.to_mysql_bin(writer, column),
            Self::Int(value) => value.to_mysql_bin(writer, column),
            Self::UInt(value) => value.to_mysql_bin(writer, column),
            Self::Float(value) => value.to_mysql_bin(writer, column),
            Self::Double(value) => value.to_mysql_bin(writer, column),
            Self::Date(year, month, day, hour, minute, second, micros) => match column.coltype {
                ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_DATE | ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_NEWDATE => {
                    writer.write_all(&[4])?;
                    writer.write_all(&year.to_le_bytes())?;
                    writer.write_all(&[*month, *day])
                }
                ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_DATETIME
                | ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_TIMESTAMP
                | ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_DATETIME2
                | ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_TIMESTAMP2 => {
                    let len = if *micros != 0 {
                        11
                    } else if *hour != 0 || *minute != 0 || *second != 0 {
                        7
                    } else {
                        4
                    };
                    writer.write_all(&[len])?;
                    writer.write_all(&year.to_le_bytes())?;
                    writer.write_all(&[*month, *day])?;
                    if len >= 7 {
                        writer.write_all(&[*hour, *minute, *second])?;
                    }
                    if len == 11 {
                        writer.write_all(&micros.to_le_bytes())?;
                    }
                    Ok(())
                }
                _ => Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                    "temporal value does not match result column type",
                )),
            },
            Self::Time(negative, days, hours, minutes, seconds, micros) => {
                if !matches!(
                    column.coltype,
                    ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_TIME | ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_TIME2
                ) {
                    return Err(io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                        "time value does not match result column type",
                    ));
                }
                let len = if *days == 0
                    && *hours == 0
                    && *minutes == 0
                    && *seconds == 0
                    && *micros == 0
                {
                    0
                } else if *micros == 0 {
                    8
                } else {
                    12
                };
                writer.write_all(&[len])?;
                if len == 0 {
                    return Ok(());
                }
                writer.write_all(&[u8::from(*negative)])?;
                writer.write_all(&days.to_le_bytes())?;
                writer.write_all(&[*hours, *minutes, *seconds])?;
                if len == 12 {
                    writer.write_all(&micros.to_le_bytes())?;
                }
                Ok(())
            }
        }
    }
}

fn upstream_value_to_output(value: UpstreamValue) -> Option<MysqlOutputValue> {
    Some(match value {
        UpstreamValue::NULL => return None,
        UpstreamValue::Bytes(bytes) => MysqlOutputValue::Bytes(bytes),
        UpstreamValue::Int(value) => MysqlOutputValue::Int(value),
        UpstreamValue::UInt(value) => MysqlOutputValue::UInt(value),
        UpstreamValue::Float(value) => MysqlOutputValue::Float(value),
        UpstreamValue::Double(value) => MysqlOutputValue::Double(value),
        UpstreamValue::Date(year, month, day, hour, minute, second, micros) => {
            MysqlOutputValue::Date(year, month, day, hour, minute, second, micros)
        }
        UpstreamValue::Time(negative, days, hours, minutes, seconds, micros) => {
            MysqlOutputValue::Time(negative, days, hours, minutes, seconds, micros)
        }
    })
}

/// Preserve the upstream MySQL protocol type code. Both driver and server
/// enums follow the MySQL wire-protocol numeric assignments; unknown future
/// types fall back to VAR_STRING rather than emitting an invalid type code.
fn output_column_type(column_type: mysql_async::consts::ColumnType) -> ColumnType {
    ColumnType::try_from(column_type as u8).unwrap_or(ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_VAR_STRING)
}

fn output_column(column: &mysql_async::Column) -> Column {
    Column {
        table: column.table_str().into_owned(),
        column: column.name_str().into_owned(),
        coltype: output_column_type(column.column_type()),
        colflags: ColumnFlags::from_bits_truncate(column.flags().bits()),
    }
}

#[async_trait::async_trait]
impl<W> AsyncMysqlShim<W> for MysqlGatewaySession
where
    W: tokio::io::AsyncWrite + Send + Unpin,
{
    type Error = io::Error;

    fn version(&self) -> String {
        SERVER_VERSION.to_string()
    }

    fn connect_id(&self) -> u32 {
        let uuid = Uuid::new_v4();
        let bytes = uuid.as_bytes();
        u32::from_le_bytes([bytes[0], bytes[1], bytes[2], bytes[3]])
    }

    fn default_auth_plugin(&self) -> &str {
        "caching_sha2_password"
    }

    async fn auth_plugin_for_username(&self, user: &[u8]) -> &str {
        self.registry.auth_plugin_for_username(user)
    }

    fn salt(&self) -> [u8; 20] {
        self.salt
    }

    async fn authenticate(
        &self,
        auth_plugin: &str,
        username: &[u8],
        salt: &[u8],
        auth_data: &[u8],
    ) -> bool {
        if self.authenticated() || self.upstream_ready().await {
