            return false;
        }

        let Some(principal) = self
            .registry
            .authenticate(auth_plugin, username, salt, auth_data)
        else {
            return false;
        };

        // The registry entry selects the only upstream identity this
        // client session is permitted to use.
        let pool = match MysqlUpstream::from_url(&principal.upstream_url) {
            Ok(pool) => pool,
            Err(_) => return false,
        };

        // Establish the real upstream connection before accepting
        // authentication. All later work uses this exact connection.
        let mut connection = match tokio::time::timeout(
            std::time::Duration::from_secs(UPSTREAM_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_SECS),
            pool.get_connection(),
        )
        .await
        {
            Ok(Ok(connection)) => connection,
            _ => return false,
        };

        // Check the database selected by the live upstream session as a second
        // guard against a registry/driver configuration mismatch.
        let selected_database: Option<String> = match tokio::time::timeout(
            std::time::Duration::from_secs(UPSTREAM_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_SECS),
            connection.query_first("SELECT DATABASE()"),
        )
        .await
        {
            Ok(Ok(database)) => database,
            _ => return false,
        };
        if selected_database.as_deref() != Some(principal.database.as_str()) {
            tracing::warn!(
                username = %principal.username,
                expected_database = %principal.database,
                selected_database = ?selected_database,
                "MySQL upstream database did not match the tenant registry"
            );
            return false;
        }

        if self.identity.bind_authenticated(principal).is_err() {
            return false;
        }

        let mut guard = self.upstream.lock().await;

        if guard.is_some() {
            return false;
        }

        *guard = Some(MysqlUpstreamSession::new(pool, connection));

        true
    }

    async fn on_prepare<'a>(
        &'a mut self,
        query: &'a str,
        info: StatementMetaWriter<'a, W>,
    ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
        if !self.authenticated() {
            return info
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_ACCESS_DENIED_ERROR,
                    &b"authentication required"[..],
                )
                .await;
        }

        if classify_statement(query).is_err() {
            return info
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_NOT_SUPPORTED_YET,
                    &b"prepared statement rejected by Proxima SQL admission policy"[..],
                )
                .await;
        }

        let mut guard = self.upstream.lock().await;
        let Some(session) = guard.as_mut() else {
            return info
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                    &b"tenant upstream connection unavailable"[..],
                )
                .await;
        };

        let statement = match tokio::time::timeout(
            std::time::Duration::from_secs(QUERY_TIMEOUT_SECS),
            session.connection.prep(query),
        )
        .await
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| Err(mysql_async::DriverError::ConnectionClosed.into()))
        {
            Ok(statement) => statement,
            Err(_) => {
                return info
                    .error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                        &b"upstream statement preparation failed"[..],
                    )
                    .await;
            }
        };

        let client_statement_id = statement.id();

        if session.statements.contains_key(&client_statement_id) {
            let _ = session.connection.close(statement).await;
            return info
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                    &b"prepared statement identifier collision"[..],
                )
                .await;
        }

        let parameter_columns: Vec<Column> = statement.params().iter().map(output_column).collect();

        let result_metadata = statement.columns();

        let result_columns: Vec<Column> = result_metadata.iter().map(output_column).collect();

        session.statements.insert(client_statement_id, statement);

        let reply_result = info
            .reply(
                client_statement_id,
                parameter_columns.iter(),
                result_columns.iter(),
            )
            .await;

        if reply_result.is_err() {
            if let Some(statement) = session.statements.remove(&client_statement_id) {
                let _ = session.connection.close(statement).await;
            }
        }

        reply_result
    }

    async fn on_execute<'a>(
        &'a mut self,
        id: u32,
        params: ParamParser<'a>,
        results: QueryResultWriter<'a, W>,
    ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
        if !self.authenticated() {
            return results
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_ACCESS_DENIED_ERROR,
                    &b"authentication required"[..],
                )
                .await;
        }

        let values = match decode_parameters(params) {
            Ok(values) => values,
            Err(()) => {
                return results
                    .error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_WRONG_ARGUMENTS,
                        &b"invalid or unsupported prepared statement parameters"[..],
                    )
                    .await;
            }
        };

        let mut guard = self.upstream.lock().await;
        let Some(session) = guard.as_mut() else {
            return results
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                    &b"tenant upstream connection unavailable"[..],
                )
                .await;
        };

        let Some(statement) = session.statements.get(&id) else {
            return results
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_STMT_HANDLER,
                    &b"unknown prepared statement for this tenant session"[..],
                )
                .await;
        };

        if values.len() != statement.num_params() as usize {
            return results
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_WRONG_ARGUMENTS,
                    &b"prepared statement parameter count mismatch"[..],
                )
                .await;
        }

        let mut upstream_result = match tokio::time::timeout(
            std::time::Duration::from_secs(QUERY_TIMEOUT_SECS),
            session.connection.exec_iter(statement, values),
        )
        .await
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| Err(mysql_async::DriverError::ConnectionClosed.into()))
        {
            Ok(result) => result,
            Err(error) => {
                tracing::warn!(
                    error = %error,
                    "MySQL upstream prepared statement execution failed"
                );

                return results
                    .error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                        &b"upstream prepared statement execution failed"[..],
                    )
                    .await;
            }
        };

        let upstream_columns = upstream_result.columns_ref();

        if upstream_columns.is_empty() {
            let affected_rows = upstream_result.affected_rows();
            let last_insert_id = upstream_result.last_insert_id().unwrap_or(0);
            let warnings = upstream_result.warnings();

            if upstream_result.drop_result().await.is_err() {
                return results
                    .error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                        &b"upstream result completion failed"[..],
                    )
                    .await;
            }

            return results
                .completed(OkResponse {
                    header: 0x00,
                    affected_rows,
                    last_insert_id,
                    status_flags: StatusFlags::empty(),
                    warnings,
                    info: String::new(),
                    session_state_info: String::new(),
                })
                .await;
        }

        let output_columns: Vec<Column> = upstream_columns.iter().map(output_column).collect();

        let mut output = results.start(&output_columns).await?;

        let mut rows = match upstream_result.stream::<Row>().await {
            Ok(Some(rows)) => rows,
            Ok(None) => return output.finish().await,
            Err(_) => {
                return output
                    .finish_error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                        &b"upstream prepared result stream failed".to_vec(),
                    )
                    .await;
            }
        };

        while let Some(row_result) = match tokio::time::timeout(
            std::time::Duration::from_secs(QUERY_TIMEOUT_SECS),
            rows.next(),
        )
        .await
        {
            Ok(next) => next,
            Err(_) => {
                return output
                    .finish_error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                        &b"upstream row stream timed out".to_vec(),
                    )
                    .await
            }
        } {
            let row = match row_result {
                Ok(row) => row,
                Err(_) => {
                    return output
                        .finish_error(
                            ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                            &b"upstream row retrieval failed".to_vec(),
                        )
                        .await;
                }
            };

            let values: Vec<Option<MysqlOutputValue>> = row
                .unwrap()
                .into_iter()
                .map(upstream_value_to_output)
                .collect();

            output.write_row(values).await?;
        }

        output.finish().await
    }

    async fn on_close<'a>(&'a mut self, stmt: u32)
    where
        W: 'async_trait,
    {
        let mut guard = self.upstream.lock().await;

        if let Some(session) = guard.as_mut() {
            if let Some(statement) = session.statements.remove(&stmt) {
                let _ = session.connection.close(statement).await;
            }
        }
    }

    async fn on_init<'a>(
        &'a mut self,
        database: &'a str,
        writer: InitWriter<'a, W>,
    ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
        match self.identity.authorize_database(database) {
            Ok(()) => writer.ok().await,
            Err(_) => {
                writer
                    .error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_DBACCESS_DENIED_ERROR,
                        &b"database access denied by Proxima tenant policy"[..],
                    )
                    .await
            }
        }
    }

    async fn on_query<'a>(
        &'a mut self,
        query: &'a str,
        results: QueryResultWriter<'a, W>,
    ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
        if !self.authenticated() {
            return results
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_ACCESS_DENIED_ERROR,
                    &b"authentication required"[..],
                )
                .await;
        }

        if classify_statement(query).is_err() {
            return results
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_NOT_SUPPORTED_YET,
                    &b"statement rejected by Proxima SQL admission policy"[..],
                )
                .await;
        }

        let mut guard = self.upstream.lock().await;
        let Some(session) = guard.as_mut() else {
            return results
                .error(
                    ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                    &b"tenant upstream connection unavailable"[..],
                )
                .await;
        };

        let mut upstream_result = match tokio::time::timeout(
            std::time::Duration::from_secs(QUERY_TIMEOUT_SECS),
            session.connection.query_iter(query),
        )
        .await
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| Err(mysql_async::DriverError::ConnectionClosed.into()))
        {
            Ok(result) => result,
            Err(_) => {
                return results
                    .error(ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR, &b"upstream query failed"[..])
                    .await;
            }
        };

        let upstream_columns = upstream_result.columns_ref();

        if upstream_columns.is_empty() {
            let affected_rows = upstream_result.affected_rows();
            let last_insert_id = upstream_result.last_insert_id().unwrap_or(0);
            let warnings = upstream_result.warnings();

            if upstream_result.drop_result().await.is_err() {
                return results
                    .error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                        &b"upstream result completion failed"[..],
                    )
                    .await;
            }

            return results
                .completed(OkResponse {
                    header: 0x00,
                    affected_rows,
                    last_insert_id,
                    status_flags: StatusFlags::empty(),
                    warnings,
                    info: String::new(),
                    session_state_info: String::new(),
                })
                .await;
        }

        let output_columns: Vec<Column> = upstream_columns.iter().map(output_column).collect();

        let mut output = results.start(&output_columns).await?;

        let mut rows = match upstream_result.stream::<Row>().await {
            Ok(Some(rows)) => rows,
            Ok(None) => return output.finish().await,
            Err(_) => {
                return output
                    .finish_error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                        &b"upstream result stream failed".to_vec(),
                    )
                    .await;
            }
        };

        while let Some(row_result) = match tokio::time::timeout(
            std::time::Duration::from_secs(QUERY_TIMEOUT_SECS),
            rows.next(),
        )
        .await
        {
            Ok(next) => next,
            Err(_) => {
                return output
                    .finish_error(
                        ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                        &b"upstream row stream timed out".to_vec(),
                    )
                    .await
            }
        } {
            let row = match row_result {
                Ok(row) => row,
                Err(_) => {
                    return output
                        .finish_error(
                            ErrorKind::ER_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
                            &b"upstream row retrieval failed".to_vec(),
                        )
                        .await;
                }
            };

            let values: Vec<Option<MysqlOutputValue>> = row
                .unwrap()
                .into_iter()
                .map(upstream_value_to_output)
                .collect();

            output.write_row(values).await?;
        }

        output.finish().await
    }
}

/// Accept only loopback binding in this first gateway release.
/// A remote listener requires client-facing TLS and hardened deployment
/// configuration before it can be enabled.
pub async fn run_mysql_gateway(
    address: SocketAddr,
    registry: Arc<MysqlIdentityRegistry>,
) -> io::Result<()> {
    if !address.ip().is_loopback() {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
            "Proxima MySQL gateway currently requires a loopback bind address",
        ));
    }

    let max_connections = std::env::var("PROXIMA_MYSQL_MAX_CONNECTIONS")
        .ok()
        .map(|value| value.parse::<usize>())
        .transpose()
        .map_err(|error| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, error))?
        .unwrap_or(DEFAULT_MAX_CONNECTIONS);
    if max_connections == 0 || max_connections > 65_536 {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
            "PROXIMA_MYSQL_MAX_CONNECTIONS must be between 1 and 65536",
        ));
    }

    let listener = TcpListener::bind(address).await?;
    let connection_slots = Arc::new(Semaphore::new(max_connections));

    tracing::info!(
        address = %listener.local_addr()?,
        max_connections,
        "Proxima MySQL gateway listening"
    );

    loop {
        let (stream, peer) = listener.accept().await?;
        let permit = match Arc::clone(&connection_slots).try_acquire_owned() {
            Ok(permit) => permit,
            Err(_) => {
                tracing::warn!(
                    peer = %peer,
                    max_connections,
                    "MySQL connection rejected because the gateway connection limit is reached"
                );
                drop(stream);
                continue;
            }
        };
        let registry = Arc::clone(&registry);

        task::spawn(async move {
            let _connection_permit = permit;
            let (reader, writer) = stream.into_split();
            let session = MysqlGatewaySession::new(registry);

            let options = IntermediaryOptions {
                process_use_statement_on_query: false,
                reject_connection_on_dbname_absence: true,
            };

            if let Err(error) =
                AsyncMysqlIntermediary::run_with_options(session, reader, writer, &options).await
            {
                tracing::debug!(
                    peer = %peer,
                    error = %error,
                    "MySQL client session ended"
                );
            }
        });
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn preserves_upstream_column_type_codes() {
        assert_eq!(
            output_column_type(mysql_async::consts::ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_LONG),
            ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_LONG
        );
        assert_eq!(
            output_column_type(mysql_async::consts::ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_NEWDECIMAL),
            ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_NEWDECIMAL
        );
        assert_eq!(
            output_column_type(mysql_async::consts::ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_BLOB),
            ColumnType::MYSQL_TYPE_BLOB
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn preserves_null_values() {
        assert_eq!(upstream_value_to_output(UpstreamValue::NULL), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn preserves_signed_integer_values() {
        assert!(matches!(
            upstream_value_to_output(UpstreamValue::Int(-42)),
            Some(MysqlOutputValue::Int(-42))
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn preserves_unsigned_integer_values() {
        assert!(matches!(
            upstream_value_to_output(UpstreamValue::UInt(42)),
            Some(MysqlOutputValue::UInt(42))
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn preserves_binary_payloads_byte_for_byte() {
        let bytes = vec![0xff, 0x00, 0x80, 0x41];
        assert!(
            matches!(upstream_value_to_output(UpstreamValue::Bytes(bytes.clone())), Some(MysqlOutputValue::Bytes(value)) if value == bytes)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn preserves_datetime_values_as_typed_values() {
        assert!(matches!(
            upstream_value_to_output(UpstreamValue::Date(2026, 11, 19, 12, 30, 45, 123456)),
            Some(MysqlOutputValue::Date(2026, 11, 19, 12, 30, 45, 123456))
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn converts_signed_prepared_parameter() {
        assert!(matches!(
            convert_parameter(ValueInner::Int(-42)),
            Ok(UpstreamValue::Int(-42))
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn converts_unsigned_prepared_parameter() {
        assert!(matches!(
            convert_parameter(ValueInner::UInt(42)),
            Ok(UpstreamValue::UInt(42))
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn converts_string_prepared_parameter() {
        assert!(matches!(
            convert_parameter(ValueInner::Bytes(b"tenant-a")),
            Ok(UpstreamValue::Bytes(value)) if value == b"tenant-a"
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn converts_null_prepared_parameter() {
        assert!(matches!(
            convert_parameter(ValueInner::NULL),
            Ok(UpstreamValue::NULL)
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn decodes_binary_date_parameter() {
        assert_eq!(
            decode_date_payload(&[0xe8, 0x07, 11, 19]),
            Ok((2024, 11, 19))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn decodes_binary_datetime_with_microseconds() {
        let bytes = [0xe8, 0x07, 11, 19, 12, 30, 45, 0x40, 0x0d, 0x03, 0x00];
        assert_eq!(
            decode_datetime_payload(&bytes),
            Ok((2024, 11, 19, 12, 30, 45, 200000))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn decodes_binary_time_with_days_and_microseconds() {
        let bytes = [1, 2, 0, 0, 0, 3, 4, 5, 0x40, 0x0d, 0x03, 0x00];
        assert_eq!(decode_time_payload(&bytes), Ok((true, 2, 3, 4, 5, 200000)));
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_invalid_binary_temporal_values() {
        assert!(decode_date_payload(&[0xe8, 0x07, 13, 19]).is_err());
        assert!(decode_datetime_payload(&[0; 5]).is_err());
        assert!(decode_time_payload(&[2, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn decodes_binary_date_parameter_into_upstream_value() {
        assert_eq!(
            convert_parameter(ValueInner::Date(&[0xe8, 0x07, 11, 19])),
            Ok(UpstreamValue::Date(2024, 11, 19, 0, 0, 0, 0))
        );
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\mysql_identity.rs ==========
use sha1::{Digest, Sha1};
use sha2::Sha256;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::fs;
use std::path::Path;

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct TenantPrincipal {
    pub username: String,
    pub tenant_id: String,
    pub database: String,
    pub upstream_url: String,
    pub(crate) auth_plugin: &'static str,
    pub(crate) password_sha1: Option<[u8; 20]>,
    pub(crate) password_sha256: Option<[u8; 32]>,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum IdentityError {
    Io(std::io::Error),
    InvalidRegistry(String),
}

impl std::fmt::Display for IdentityError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::Io(error) => write!(f, "identity registry I/O error: {error}"),
            Self::InvalidRegistry(message) => {
                write!(f, "invalid MySQL identity registry: {message}")
            }
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for IdentityError {}

impl From<std::io::Error> for IdentityError {
    fn from(value: std::io::Error) -> Self {
        Self::Io(value)
    }
}

pub struct MysqlIdentityRegistry {
    principals: HashMap<String, TenantPrincipal>,
}

impl MysqlIdentityRegistry {
    pub fn load(path: impl AsRef<Path>) -> Result<Self, IdentityError> {
        let contents = fs::read_to_string(path)?;
        Self::from_json(&contents)
    }

    pub fn from_json(contents: &str) -> Result<Self, IdentityError> {
        let root: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(contents)
            .map_err(|error| IdentityError::InvalidRegistry(error.to_string()))?;

        let users = root
            .get("users")
            .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_array)
            .ok_or_else(|| IdentityError::InvalidRegistry("users must be an array".into()))?;

        if users.is_empty() {
            return Err(IdentityError::InvalidRegistry(
                "at least one user must be provisioned".into(),
            ));
        }

        let mut principals = HashMap::new();

        for (index, user) in users.iter().enumerate() {
            let field = |name: &str| -> Result<String, IdentityError> {
                user.get(name)
                    .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)
                    .map(str::trim)
                    .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
                    .map(str::to_owned)
                    .ok_or_else(|| {
                        IdentityError::InvalidRegistry(format!("users[{index}].{name} is required"))
                    })
            };

            let username = field("username")?;
            let tenant_id = field("tenant_id")?;
            let database = field("database")?;
            let upstream_url = field("upstream_url")?;
            let password_sha1_hex = user
                .get("password_sha1")
                .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)
                .map(str::trim)
                .filter(|v| !v.is_empty());
            let password_sha256_hex = user
                .get("password_sha256")
                .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)
                .map(str::trim)
                .filter(|v| !v.is_empty());
            if password_sha1_hex.is_some() == password_sha256_hex.is_some() {
                return Err(IdentityError::InvalidRegistry(format!(
                    "users[{index}] must contain exactly one of password_sha1 or password_sha256"
                )));
            }
            let auth_plugin = user
                .get("auth_plugin")
                .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)
                .unwrap_or(if password_sha256_hex.is_some() {
                    "caching_sha2_password"
                } else {
                    "mysql_native_password"
                });
            let (password_sha1, password_sha256, expected_plugin) =
                if let Some(hash) = password_sha1_hex {
                    let decoded = hex::decode(hash).map_err(|_| {
                        IdentityError::InvalidRegistry(format!(
                            "users[{index}].password_sha1 must be hexadecimal"
                        ))
                    })?;
                    let hash: [u8; 20] = decoded.try_into().map_err(|_| {
                        IdentityError::InvalidRegistry(format!(
                            "users[{index}].password_sha1 must contain exactly 20 bytes"
                        ))
                    })?;
                    (Some(hash), None, "mysql_native_password")
                } else {
                    let decoded = hex::decode(password_sha256_hex.unwrap()).map_err(|_| {
                        IdentityError::InvalidRegistry(format!(
                            "users[{index}].password_sha256 must be hexadecimal"
                        ))
                    })?;
                    let hash: [u8; 32] = decoded.try_into().map_err(|_| {
                        IdentityError::InvalidRegistry(format!(
                            "users[{index}].password_sha256 must contain exactly 32 bytes"
                        ))
                    })?;
                    (None, Some(hash), "caching_sha2_password")
                };
            if auth_plugin != expected_plugin {
                return Err(IdentityError::InvalidRegistry(format!(
                    "users[{index}].auth_plugin does not match its verifier format"
                )));
            }

            if !valid_database_name(&database) {
                return Err(IdentityError::InvalidRegistry(format!(
                    "users[{index}].database contains invalid characters"
                )));
            }

            if tenant_id.len() > 128 || username.len() > 128 || database.len() > 64 {
                return Err(IdentityError::InvalidRegistry(format!(
                    "users[{index}] contains an identifier that exceeds its length limit"
                )));
            }

            if !upstream_url.starts_with("mysql://") {
                return Err(IdentityError::InvalidRegistry(format!(
                    "users[{index}].upstream_url must use the mysql:// scheme supported by mysql_async"
                )));
            }

            let upstream_options = mysql_async::Opts::from_url(&upstream_url).map_err(|error| {
                IdentityError::InvalidRegistry(format!(
                    "users[{index}].upstream_url is invalid: {error}"
                ))
            })?;

            if upstream_options.db_name() != Some(database.as_str()) {
                return Err(IdentityError::InvalidRegistry(format!(
                    "users[{index}].database must match the database selected by upstream_url"
                )));
            }

            if !matches!(
                upstream_options.ip_or_hostname(),
                "localhost" | "127.0.0.1" | "::1"
            ) && upstream_options.ssl_opts().is_none()
            {
                return Err(IdentityError::InvalidRegistry(format!(
                    "users[{index}].upstream_url must require verified TLS for non-loopback upstream hosts (add require_ssl=true)"
                )));
            }

            if upstream_options.user().is_none() || upstream_options.pass().is_none() {
                return Err(IdentityError::InvalidRegistry(format!(
                    "users[{index}].upstream_url must include explicit upstream credentials"
                )));
            }

            let principal = TenantPrincipal {
                username: username.clone(),
                tenant_id,
                database,
                upstream_url,
                auth_plugin: expected_plugin,
                password_sha1,
                password_sha256,
            };

            if principals.insert(username.clone(), principal).is_some() {
                return Err(IdentityError::InvalidRegistry(format!(
                    "duplicate username: {username}"
                )));
            }
        }

        Ok(Self { principals })
    }

    /// Verifies a MySQL client challenge response using the selected plugin.
    /// Only one-way password verifiers are stored; plaintext passwords are never retained.
    pub fn authenticate(
        &self,
        auth_plugin: &str,
        username: &[u8],
        salt: &[u8],
        auth_data: &[u8],
    ) -> Option<TenantPrincipal> {
        if salt.len() != 20 {
            return None;
        }
        let username = std::str::from_utf8(username).ok()?;
        let principal = self.principals.get(username)?;
        if auth_plugin != principal.auth_plugin {
            return None;
        }
        let valid = match (
            auth_plugin,
            principal.password_sha1.as_ref(),
            principal.password_sha256.as_ref(),
        ) {
            ("mysql_native_password", Some(hash), None) => {
                verify_native_password(hash, salt, auth_data)
            }
            ("caching_sha2_password", None, Some(hash)) => {
                verify_caching_sha2_password(hash, salt, auth_data)
            }
            _ => false,
        };
        valid.then(|| principal.clone())
    }

    pub fn auth_plugin_for_username(&self, username: &[u8]) -> &'static str {
        std::str::from_utf8(username)
            .ok()
            .and_then(|name| self.principals.get(name))
            .map(|principal| principal.auth_plugin)
            .unwrap_or("caching_sha2_password")
    }

    pub fn principal_count(&self) -> usize {
        self.principals.len()
    }
}

fn valid_database_name(value: &str) -> bool {
    !value.is_empty()
        && value
            .bytes()
            .all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || byte == b'_' || byte == b'$')
}

fn verify_native_password(stored_stage1: &[u8; 20], salt: &[u8], auth_data: &[u8]) -> bool {
    if auth_data.is_empty() {
        let empty_password_hash: [u8; 20] = Sha1::digest([]).into();
        return constant_time_equal(stored_stage1, &empty_password_hash);
    }

    if auth_data.len() != 20 {
        return false;
    }

    // mysql_native_password:
    // stage1 = SHA1(password)
    // stage2 = SHA1(stage1)
    // challenge = SHA1(salt || stage2)
    // response = stage1 XOR challenge
    let stage2 = Sha1::digest(stored_stage1);
    let mut challenge_input = Vec::with_capacity(salt.len() + stage2.len());
    challenge_input.extend_from_slice(salt);
    challenge_input.extend_from_slice(&stage2);

    let challenge = Sha1::digest(&challenge_input);

    let mut recovered_stage1 = [0u8; 20];
    for index in 0..20 {
        recovered_stage1[index] = auth_data[index] ^ challenge[index];
    }

    let recovered_stage2 = Sha1::digest(recovered_stage1);
    constant_time_equal(&recovered_stage2, &stage2)
}

fn verify_caching_sha2_password(stored_stage2: &[u8; 32], salt: &[u8], auth_data: &[u8]) -> bool {
    if auth_data.is_empty() {
        let stage1 = Sha256::digest([]);
        let empty_stage2 = Sha256::digest(stage1);
        return constant_time_equal(stored_stage2, &empty_stage2);
    }
    if auth_data.len() != 32 {
        return false;
    }
    // MySQL caching_sha2_password fast-auth:
    // stage1 = SHA256(password), stage2 = SHA256(stage1)
    // challenge = SHA256(stage2 || nonce), response = stage1 XOR challenge.
    let challenge = {
        let mut input = Vec::with_capacity(stored_stage2.len() + salt.len());
        input.extend_from_slice(stored_stage2);
        input.extend_from_slice(salt);
        Sha256::digest(input)
    };
    let mut recovered_stage1 = [0u8; 32];
    for index in 0..32 {
        recovered_stage1[index] = auth_data[index] ^ challenge[index];
    }
    let recovered_stage2 = Sha256::digest(recovered_stage1);
    constant_time_equal(&recovered_stage2, stored_stage2)
}

fn constant_time_equal(left: &[u8], right: &[u8]) -> bool {
    if left.len() != right.len() {
        return false;
    }

    let mut difference = 0u8;
    for (a, b) in left.iter().zip(right.iter()) {
        difference |= a ^ b;
    }

    difference == 0
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    const SALT: &[u8; 20] = b"proxima-test-salt-12";

    fn password_stage1(password: &str) -> [u8; 20] {
        Sha1::digest(password.as_bytes()).into()
    }

    fn native_response(password: &str, salt: &[u8]) -> Vec<u8> {
        let stage1 = password_stage1(password);
        let stage2 = Sha1::digest(stage1);

        let mut input = Vec::new();
        input.extend_from_slice(salt);
        input.extend_from_slice(&stage2);

        let challenge = Sha1::digest(input);

        stage1
            .iter()
            .zip(challenge.iter())
            .map(|(a, b)| a ^ b)
            .collect()
    }

    fn registry_json() -> String {
        let hash = hex::encode(password_stage1("correct-password"));

        format!(
            r#"{{
                "users": [
                    {{
                        "username": "tenant_a_app",
                        "tenant_id": "tenant-a",
                        "database": "tenant_a_db",
                        "upstream_url": "mysql://tenant_a:secret@127.0.0.1:3306/tenant_a_db",
                        "password_sha1": "{hash}"
                    }}
                ]
            }}"#
        )
    }

    fn caching_stage2(password: &str) -> [u8; 32] {
        let stage1 = Sha256::digest(password.as_bytes());
        Sha256::digest(stage1).into()
    }

    fn caching_response(password: &str, salt: &[u8]) -> Vec<u8> {
        let stage1 = Sha256::digest(password.as_bytes());
        let stage2 = Sha256::digest(stage1);
        let mut input = Vec::new();
        input.extend_from_slice(&stage2);
        input.extend_from_slice(salt);
        let challenge = Sha256::digest(input);
        stage1
            .iter()
            .zip(challenge.iter())
            .map(|(a, b)| a ^ b)
            .collect()
    }

    fn caching_registry_json() -> String {
        let hash = hex::encode(caching_stage2("correct-password"));
        format!(
            r#"{{"users":[{{"username":"tenant_sha2","tenant_id":"tenant-sha2","database":"tenant_sha2_db","upstream_url":"mysql://tenant_sha2:secret@127.0.0.1:3306/tenant_sha2_db","auth_plugin":"caching_sha2_password","password_sha256":"{hash}"}}]}}"#
        )
    }

    #[test]
    fn authenticates_caching_sha2_password_with_stage2_verifier() {
        let registry = MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json(&caching_registry_json()).unwrap();
        let response = caching_response("correct-password", SALT);
        let principal = registry
            .authenticate("caching_sha2_password", b"tenant_sha2", SALT, &response)
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(principal.tenant_id, "tenant-sha2");
        assert_eq!(
            registry.auth_plugin_for_username(b"tenant_sha2"),
            "caching_sha2_password"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_wrong_caching_sha2_password() {
        let registry = MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json(&caching_registry_json()).unwrap();
        let response = caching_response("wrong-password", SALT);
        assert!(registry
            .authenticate("caching_sha2_password", b"tenant_sha2", SALT, &response)
            .is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_malformed_caching_sha2_response() {
        let registry = MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json(&caching_registry_json()).unwrap();
        assert!(registry
            .authenticate("caching_sha2_password", b"tenant_sha2", SALT, b"short")
            .is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_mismatched_auth_plugin_and_verifier() {
        let registry = MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json(&caching_registry_json()).unwrap();
        let response = caching_response("correct-password", SALT);
        assert!(registry
            .authenticate("mysql_native_password", b"tenant_sha2", SALT, &response)
            .is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn loads_valid_registry() {
        let registry = MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json(&registry_json()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(registry.principal_count(), 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn authenticates_valid_native_password() {
        let registry = MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json(&registry_json()).unwrap();
        let response = native_response("correct-password", SALT);

        let principal =
            registry.authenticate("mysql_native_password", b"tenant_a_app", SALT, &response);

        assert_eq!(principal.unwrap().tenant_id, "tenant-a");
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_wrong_password() {
        let registry = MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json(&registry_json()).unwrap();
        let response = native_response("wrong-password", SALT);

        assert!(registry
            .authenticate("mysql_native_password", b"tenant_a_app", SALT, &response,)
            .is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_unknown_username() {
        let registry = MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json(&registry_json()).unwrap();
        let response = native_response("correct-password", SALT);

        assert!(registry
            .authenticate("mysql_native_password", b"tenant_b_app", SALT, &response,)
            .is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_unsupported_auth_plugin() {
        let registry = MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json(&registry_json()).unwrap();
        let response = native_response("correct-password", SALT);

        assert!(registry
            .authenticate("caching_sha2_password", b"tenant_a_app", SALT, &response,)
            .is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_wrong_salt_length() {
        let registry = MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json(&registry_json()).unwrap();
        let response = native_response("correct-password", SALT);

        assert!(registry
            .authenticate(
                "mysql_native_password",
                b"tenant_a_app",
                b"short",
                &response,
            )
            .is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_malformed_auth_response() {
        let registry = MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json(&registry_json()).unwrap();

        assert!(registry
            .authenticate("mysql_native_password", b"tenant_a_app", SALT, b"short",)
            .is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_duplicate_usernames() {
        let hash = hex::encode(password_stage1("password"));
        let json = format!(
            r#"{{
                "users": [
                    {{
                        "username":"same",
                        "tenant_id":"a",
                        "database":"db_a",
                        "upstream_url":"mysql://user:pass@localhost/db_a",
                        "password_sha1":"{hash}"
                    }},
                    {{
                        "username":"same",
                        "tenant_id":"b",
                        "database":"db_b",
                        "upstream_url":"mysql://user:pass@localhost/db_b",
                        "password_sha1":"{hash}"
                    }}
                ]
            }}"#
        );

        assert!(MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json(&json).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_remote_upstream_without_tls() {
        let json = registry_json().replace("127.0.0.1:3306", "db.example.com:3306");
        assert!(MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json(&json).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn accepts_remote_upstream_with_tls() {
        let json = registry_json().replace(
            "127.0.0.1:3306/tenant_a_db",
            "db.example.com:3306/tenant_a_db?require_ssl=true",
        );
        assert!(MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json(&json).is_ok());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_upstream_database_mismatch() {
        let json =
            registry_json().replace("127.0.0.1:3306/tenant_a_db", "127.0.0.1:3306/tenant_b_db");
        assert!(MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json(&json).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_upstream_url_without_database() {
        let json = registry_json().replace("127.0.0.1:3306/tenant_a_db", "127.0.0.1:3306/");
        assert!(MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json(&json).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_mysqls_scheme_not_supported_by_driver() {
        let json = registry_json().replace("mysql://", "mysqls://");
        assert!(MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json(&json).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_invalid_database_identifier() {
        let json = registry_json().replace("tenant_a_db", "tenant_a; DROP TABLE users");

        assert!(MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json(&json).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_missing_registry_users() {
        assert!(MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json("{}").is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_invalid_password_hash_length() {
        let json =
            registry_json().replace(&hex::encode(password_stage1("correct-password")), "abcd");

        assert!(MysqlIdentityRegistry::from_json(&json).is_err());
    }
}

impl std::fmt::Debug for TenantPrincipal {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("TenantPrincipal")
            .field("username", &self.username)
            .field("tenant_id", &self.tenant_id)
            .field("database", &self.database)
            .field("upstream_url", &"[REDACTED]")
            .field("auth_plugin", &self.auth_plugin)
            .field("password_sha1", &"[REDACTED]")
            .field("password_sha256", &"[REDACTED]")
            .finish()
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\mysql_policy.rs ==========
use sqlparser::ast::{Expr, ObjectName, Select, Visit, Visitor};
use sqlparser::dialect::MySqlDialect;
use sqlparser::parser::Parser;
use std::ops::ControlFlow;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum MysqlOperation {
    Read,
    Write,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum MysqlPolicyError {
    EmptyStatement,
    MultipleStatements,
    InvalidSql(String),
    UnsupportedOperation,
}

impl std::fmt::Display for MysqlPolicyError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::EmptyStatement => write!(f, "empty SQL statement"),
            Self::MultipleStatements => write!(f, "multiple SQL statements are not permitted"),
            Self::InvalidSql(message) => write!(f, "SQL parsing failed: {message}"),
            Self::UnsupportedOperation => {
                write!(f, "SQL operation is not permitted by the MySQL policy")
            }
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for MysqlPolicyError {}

#[derive(Default)]
struct AdmissionVisitor {
    rejected: bool,
}

impl Visitor for AdmissionVisitor {
    type Break = ();

    fn pre_visit_select(&mut self, select: &Select) -> ControlFlow<Self::Break> {
        if select.into.is_some() {
            self.rejected = true;
            return ControlFlow::Break(());
        }
        ControlFlow::Continue(())
    }

    fn pre_visit_relation(&mut self, relation: &ObjectName) -> ControlFlow<Self::Break> {
        let name = relation.to_string();
        // The initial tenant contract is a single default database. Fail
        // closed on qualified names and system schemas, including nested queries.
        if name.split('.').count() > 1 {
            self.rejected = true;
            return ControlFlow::Break(());
        }
        let schema = name.trim_matches('`').to_ascii_lowercase();
        if matches!(
            schema.as_str(),
            "mysql" | "information_schema" | "performance_schema" | "sys"
        ) {
            self.rejected = true;
            return ControlFlow::Break(());
        }
        ControlFlow::Continue(())
    }

    fn pre_visit_expr(&mut self, expr: &Expr) -> ControlFlow<Self::Break> {
        if let Expr::Function(function) = expr {
            let full_name = function.name.to_string().to_ascii_lowercase();
            let name = full_name
                .rsplit('.')
                .next()
                .unwrap_or(&full_name)
                .trim_matches('`');
            if matches!(
                name,
                "load_file"
                    | "sleep"
                    | "benchmark"
                    | "get_lock"
                    | "release_lock"
                    | "is_free_lock"
                    | "master_pos_wait"
                    | "sys_exec"
                    | "sys_eval"
            ) {
                self.rejected = true;
                return ControlFlow::Break(());
            }
        }
        ControlFlow::Continue(())
    }
}

/// Parses one statement and classifies its operation, then applies conservative
/// AST restrictions. This is an admission layer, not the isolation boundary:
/// tenant-specific upstream credentials and database grants remain mandatory.
pub fn classify_statement(sql: &str) -> Result<MysqlOperation, MysqlPolicyError> {
    if sql.trim().is_empty() {
        return Err(MysqlPolicyError::EmptyStatement);
    }

    let dialect = MySqlDialect {};
    let statements = Parser::parse_sql(&dialect, sql)
        .map_err(|error| MysqlPolicyError::InvalidSql(error.to_string()))?;

    if statements.is_empty() {
        return Err(MysqlPolicyError::EmptyStatement);
    }
    if statements.len() != 1 {
        return Err(MysqlPolicyError::MultipleStatements);
    }

    let operation = match &statements[0] {
        sqlparser::ast::Statement::Query(_) => MysqlOperation::Read,
        sqlparser::ast::Statement::Insert(_)
        | sqlparser::ast::Statement::Update(_)
        | sqlparser::ast::Statement::Delete(_) => MysqlOperation::Write,
        _ => return Err(MysqlPolicyError::UnsupportedOperation),
    };

    let mut visitor = AdmissionVisitor::default();
    let _ = statements.visit(&mut visitor);
    if visitor.rejected {
        return Err(MysqlPolicyError::UnsupportedOperation);
    }

    Ok(operation)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn accepts_select_as_read() {
        assert_eq!(
            classify_statement("SELECT id, name FROM customers WHERE id = 7"),
            Ok(MysqlOperation::Read)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn accepts_cte_select_as_read() {
        assert_eq!(
            classify_statement("WITH recent AS (SELECT id FROM customers) SELECT id FROM recent"),
            Ok(MysqlOperation::Read)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn accepts_insert_as_write() {
        assert_eq!(
            classify_statement("INSERT INTO customers (name) VALUES ('test')"),
            Ok(MysqlOperation::Write)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn accepts_update_as_write() {
        assert_eq!(
            classify_statement("UPDATE customers SET name = 'updated' WHERE id = 1"),
            Ok(MysqlOperation::Write)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn accepts_delete_as_write() {
        assert_eq!(
            classify_statement("DELETE FROM customers WHERE id = 1"),
            Ok(MysqlOperation::Write)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_multiple_statements() {
        assert_eq!(
            classify_statement("SELECT 1; DELETE FROM customers"),
            Err(MysqlPolicyError::MultipleStatements)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_database_switching() {
        assert_eq!(
            classify_statement("USE another_database"),
            Err(MysqlPolicyError::UnsupportedOperation)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_session_mutation() {
        assert_eq!(
            classify_statement("SET SESSION sql_mode = 'ANSI'"),
            Err(MysqlPolicyError::UnsupportedOperation)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_ddl() {
        assert_eq!(
            classify_statement("DROP TABLE customers"),
            Err(MysqlPolicyError::UnsupportedOperation)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_select_into() {
        assert!(
            classify_statement("SELECT id FROM customers INTO OUTFILE '/tmp/export.txt'").is_err(),
            "SELECT INTO OUTFILE must fail closed regardless of parser error classification"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_qualified_cross_database_references() {
        assert_eq!(
            classify_statement("SELECT * FROM tenant_b.records"),
            Err(MysqlPolicyError::UnsupportedOperation)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_system_schema_references() {
        assert_eq!(
            classify_statement("SELECT user FROM mysql.user"),
            Err(MysqlPolicyError::UnsupportedOperation)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_privileged_and_resource_intensive_functions() {
        for sql in [
            "SELECT LOAD_FILE('/etc/passwd')",
            "SELECT SLEEP(10)",
            "SELECT BENCHMARK(1000000, SHA2('x', 256))",
            "SELECT GET_LOCK('proxima', 1)",
        ] {
            assert_eq!(
                classify_statement(sql),
                Err(MysqlPolicyError::UnsupportedOperation),
                "unexpected admission for: {sql}"
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_malformed_sql() {
        assert!(matches!(
            classify_statement("SELECT FROM WHERE"),
            Err(MysqlPolicyError::InvalidSql(_))
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_empty_sql() {
        assert_eq!(
            classify_statement("   "),
            Err(MysqlPolicyError::EmptyStatement)
        );
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\mysql_session.rs ==========
use crate::mysql_identity::TenantPrincipal;
use std::sync::OnceLock;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum MysqlSessionIdentityError {
    NotAuthenticated,
    AlreadyBound,
    DatabaseSwitchDenied,
}

impl std::fmt::Display for MysqlSessionIdentityError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::NotAuthenticated => write!(f, "MySQL session is not authenticated"),
            Self::AlreadyBound => write!(f, "MySQL session identity is already bound"),
            Self::DatabaseSwitchDenied => {
                write!(f, "database selection is not authorized for this tenant")
            }
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for MysqlSessionIdentityError {}

/// Immutable tenant identity for one client connection.
///
/// A connection may be bound once after successful authentication.
/// It cannot be rebound to another tenant.
pub struct MysqlConnectionIdentity {
    principal: OnceLock<TenantPrincipal>,
}

impl MysqlConnectionIdentity {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            principal: OnceLock::new(),
        }
    }

    pub fn bind_authenticated(
        &self,
        principal: TenantPrincipal,
    ) -> Result<(), MysqlSessionIdentityError> {
        self.principal
            .set(principal)
            .map_err(|_| MysqlSessionIdentityError::AlreadyBound)
    }

    pub fn principal(&self) -> Result<&TenantPrincipal, MysqlSessionIdentityError> {
        self.principal
            .get()
            .ok_or(MysqlSessionIdentityError::NotAuthenticated)
    }

    /// Enforces the database assigned to this authenticated principal.
    pub fn authorize_database(
        &self,
        requested_database: &str,
    ) -> Result<(), MysqlSessionIdentityError> {
        let principal = self.principal()?;

        if requested_database != principal.database {
            return Err(MysqlSessionIdentityError::DatabaseSwitchDenied);
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn is_authenticated(&self) -> bool {
        self.principal.get().is_some()
    }
}

impl Default for MysqlConnectionIdentity {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn principal(tenant: &str, database: &str) -> TenantPrincipal {
        TenantPrincipal {
            username: format!("{tenant}_user"),
            tenant_id: tenant.to_string(),
            database: database.to_string(),
            upstream_url: "mysql://user:secret@127.0.0.1:3306/db".to_string(),
            auth_plugin: "mysql_native_password",
            password_sha1: Some([0; 20]),
            password_sha256: None,
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_access_before_authentication() {
        let identity = MysqlConnectionIdentity::new();

        assert_eq!(
            identity.authorize_database("tenant_a_db"),
            Err(MysqlSessionIdentityError::NotAuthenticated)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn binds_authenticated_tenant_once() {
        let identity = MysqlConnectionIdentity::new();

        identity
            .bind_authenticated(principal("tenant_a", "tenant_a_db"))
            .unwrap();

        assert_eq!(identity.principal().unwrap().tenant_id, "tenant_a");
        assert!(identity.is_authenticated());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_identity_rebinding() {
        let identity = MysqlConnectionIdentity::new();

        identity
            .bind_authenticated(principal("tenant_a", "tenant_a_db"))
            .unwrap();

        assert_eq!(
            identity.bind_authenticated(principal("tenant_b", "tenant_b_db")),
            Err(MysqlSessionIdentityError::AlreadyBound)
        );

        assert_eq!(identity.principal().unwrap().tenant_id, "tenant_a");
    }

    #[test]
    fn permits_only_assigned_database() {
        let identity = MysqlConnectionIdentity::new();

        identity
            .bind_authenticated(principal("tenant_a", "tenant_a_db"))
            .unwrap();

        assert!(identity.authorize_database("tenant_a_db").is_ok());

        assert_eq!(
            identity.authorize_database("tenant_b_db"),
            Err(MysqlSessionIdentityError::DatabaseSwitchDenied)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn principal_debug_redacts_upstream_credentials() {
        let identity = MysqlConnectionIdentity::new();

        identity
            .bind_authenticated(principal("tenant_a", "tenant_a_db"))
            .unwrap();

        let debug = format!("{:?}", identity.principal().unwrap());

        assert!(!debug.contains("secret"));
        assert!(debug.contains("[REDACTED]"));
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\mysql_upstream.rs ==========
use mysql_async::prelude::Queryable;
use mysql_async::{Conn, Pool};

/// A dedicated upstream MySQL connection pool.
///
/// Construct one instance per authenticated tenant principal.
/// Never share a pool between principals with different upstream credentials.
pub struct MysqlUpstream {
    pool: Pool,
}

impl MysqlUpstream {
    /// Creates a pool from a MySQL connection URL.
    pub fn from_url(url: &str) -> Result<Self, mysql_async::Error> {
        let pool = Pool::from_url(url)?;
        Ok(Self { pool })
    }

    /// Acquires a real upstream connection.
    pub async fn get_connection(&self) -> Result<Conn, mysql_async::Error> {
        self.pool.get_conn().await
    }

    /// Executes a statement against the configured upstream.
    ///
    /// Admission and tenant authorization must happen before this method
    /// is called by the gateway.
    pub async fn execute(&self, sql: &str) -> Result<u64, mysql_async::Error> {
        let mut connection = self.pool.get_conn().await?;
        connection.query_drop(sql).await?;
        Ok(connection.affected_rows())
    }

    /// Disconnects the pool and waits for active connections to finish.
    pub async fn disconnect(self) -> Result<(), mysql_async::Error> {
        self.pool.disconnect().await
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\opensearch_tenant.rs ==========
//! OpenSearch Security-plugin role descriptor helpers.
//!
//! This module builds an OpenSearch-specific role payload. It is deliberately
//! separate from Elasticsearch's native role API; it does not provision roles,
//! create API keys, or connect to a cluster.
use serde_json::{json, Value};

pub fn valid_tenant_id(value: &str) -> bool {
    !value.is_empty()
        && value.len() <= 48
        && value
            .bytes()
            .all(|b| b.is_ascii_lowercase() || b.is_ascii_digit() || b"_-".contains(&b))
}

pub fn tenant_index_pattern(tenant_id: &str) -> Option<String> {
    valid_tenant_id(tenant_id).then(|| format!("tenant-{tenant_id}-*"))
}

/// Build a Security-plugin role body with an exact tenant index pattern,
/// explicit data-plane actions, DLS and FLS. The authenticated caller must
/// still bind the resulting role to a tenant-specific backend role or API key.
pub fn opensearch_role_descriptor(tenant_id: &str) -> Option<Value> {
    let pattern = tenant_index_pattern(tenant_id)?;
    let dls = json!({"term":{"tenant_id":tenant_id}}).to_string();
    Some(json!({
        "cluster_permissions": [],
        "index_permissions": [{
            "index_patterns": [pattern],
            "allowed_actions": [
                "indices:data/read/search",
                "indices:data/read/get",
                "indices:data/write/index",
                "indices:data/write/update",
                "indices:data/write/delete"
            ],
            "dls": dls,
            "fls": ["tenant_id", "id", "created_at", "updated_at", "data"]
        }],
        "tenant_permissions": [],
        "description": format!("Proxima tenant-scoped role for {tenant_id}")
    }))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn role_is_scoped_and_has_no_cluster_permissions() {
        let role = opensearch_role_descriptor("org_01").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(role["cluster_permissions"], json!([]));
        assert_eq!(role["tenant_permissions"], json!([]));
        assert_eq!(
            role["index_permissions"][0]["index_patterns"][0],
            "tenant-org_01-*"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            role["index_permissions"][0]["fls"],
            json!(["tenant_id", "id", "created_at", "updated_at", "data"])
        );
        let dls: Value =
            serde_json::from_str(role["index_permissions"][0]["dls"].as_str().unwrap()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(dls["term"]["tenant_id"], "org_01");
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_ambiguous_or_injected_tenant_ids() {
        for value in ["", "Org_01", "../org", "org/a", "org a", &"x".repeat(49)] {
            assert!(opensearch_role_descriptor(value).is_none());
        }
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\oracle_tenant.rs ==========
pub struct OracleTenant {
    tenant_id: String,
    username: String,
    password: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum OracleTenantError {
    InvalidTenantId,
    WeakPassword,
}
impl std::fmt::Display for OracleTenantError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::InvalidTenantId => write!(f, "invalid Oracle tenant identifier"),
            Self::WeakPassword => write!(
                f,
                "Oracle tenant password must contain at least 24 characters"
            ),
        }
    }
}
impl std::error::Error for OracleTenantError {}

/// Oracle schema-per-tenant profile. Each tenant uses a dedicated database
/// user as its schema owner; provisioning SQL runs only on a trusted admin session.
impl OracleTenant {
    pub fn new(tenant_id: &str, password: &str) -> Result<Self, OracleTenantError> {
        if tenant_id.is_empty()
            || tenant_id.len() > 24
            || !tenant_id
                .bytes()
                .all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'_')
        {
            return Err(OracleTenantError::InvalidTenantId);
        }
        if password.len() < 24 || !password.bytes().all(|b| (33..=126).contains(&b)) {
            return Err(OracleTenantError::WeakPassword);
        }
        let tenant_id = tenant_id.to_ascii_uppercase();
        Ok(Self {
            username: format!("PROXIMA_{}_OWNER", tenant_id),
            tenant_id,
            password: password.to_owned(),
        })
    }
    pub fn tenant_id(&self) -> &str {
        &self.tenant_id
    }
    pub fn username(&self) -> &str {
        &self.username
    }
    pub fn connect_user(&self) -> &str {
        &self.username
    }

    /// Statements create a schema owner with bounded tablespace quota and no
    /// elevated roles or cross-schema ANY privileges.
    pub fn provisioning_sql(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        let password = self.password.replace('"', "\"\"");
        vec![
            format!("CREATE USER {} IDENTIFIED BY \"{}\" DEFAULT TABLESPACE USERS TEMPORARY TABLESPACE TEMP QUOTA 100M ON USERS", self.username, password),
            format!("GRANT CREATE SESSION, CREATE TABLE, CREATE SEQUENCE, CREATE VIEW, CREATE PROCEDURE, CREATE TRIGGER TO {}", self.username),
            format!("ALTER USER {} DEFAULT ROLE NONE", self.username),
        ]
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn generates_dedicated_schema_principal_and_limited_grants() {
        let t = OracleTenant::new("customer_01", "a-long-random-test-password").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(t.username(), "PROXIMA_CUSTOMER_01_OWNER");
        let sql = t.provisioning_sql().join("\n");
        assert!(sql.contains("GRANT CREATE SESSION, CREATE TABLE"));
        assert!(!sql.contains("DBA"));
        assert!(!sql.contains("ANY TABLE"));
        assert!(sql.contains("DEFAULT ROLE NONE"));
    }
    #[test]
    fn rejects_identifier_injection_and_weak_password() {
        assert!(matches!(
            OracleTenant::new("A; DROP USER X", "a-long-random-test-password"),
            Err(OracleTenantError::InvalidTenantId)
        ));
        assert!(matches!(
            OracleTenant::new("tenant", "short"),
            Err(OracleTenantError::WeakPassword)
        ));
    }
    #[test]
    fn escapes_quoted_password_characters() {
        let t = OracleTenant::new("tenant", "a-long-random-password-with-\"-quote").unwrap();
        assert!(t.provisioning_sql()[0].contains("\"\""));
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\policy.rs ==========
use crate::tenant::TenantContext;
use std::fmt;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum TenantBinding {
    Unbound,
    Bound(TenantContext),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum BindingError {
    AlreadyBound,
    MissingContext,
    ContextExpired,
}

impl fmt::Display for BindingError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::AlreadyBound => write!(f, "tenant context is already bound to this session"),
            Self::MissingContext => write!(f, "tenant context is required"),
            Self::ContextExpired => write!(f, "tenant context has expired"),
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for BindingError {}

impl TenantBinding {
    pub fn bind(
        &mut self,
        context: TenantContext,
        now_unix_seconds: u64,
    ) -> Result<(), BindingError> {
        if now_unix_seconds >= context.expires_at {
            return Err(BindingError::ContextExpired);
        }

        match self {
            Self::Unbound => {
                *self = Self::Bound(context);
                Ok(())
            }
            Self::Bound(_) => Err(BindingError::AlreadyBound),
        }
    }

    pub fn tenant_id(&self, now_unix_seconds: u64) -> Result<&str, BindingError> {
        match self {
            Self::Unbound => Err(BindingError::MissingContext),
            Self::Bound(context) if now_unix_seconds >= context.expires_at => {
                Err(BindingError::ContextExpired)
            }
            Self::Bound(context) => Ok(&context.tenant_id),
        }
    }

    pub fn reset(&mut self) {
        *self = Self::Unbound;
    }

    pub fn is_bound(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, Self::Bound(_))
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn context(expires_at: u64) -> TenantContext {
        TenantContext {
            tenant_id: "tenant_a".into(),
            expires_at,
            emergency: false,
            emergency_nonce_hash: None,
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn binds_once_and_exposes_tenant() {
        let mut binding = TenantBinding::Unbound;

        binding.bind(context(2_000), 1_000).unwrap();

        assert_eq!(binding.tenant_id(1_001).unwrap(), "tenant_a");
        assert!(binding.is_bound());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_rebinding_without_session_reset() {
        let mut binding = TenantBinding::Unbound;
        binding.bind(context(2_000), 1_000).unwrap();

        let other = TenantContext {
            tenant_id: "tenant_b".into(),
            expires_at: 2_000,
            emergency: false,
            emergency_nonce_hash: None,
        };

        assert_eq!(binding.bind(other, 1_000), Err(BindingError::AlreadyBound));
        assert_eq!(binding.tenant_id(1_000).unwrap(), "tenant_a");
    }

    #[test]
    fn expired_context_is_not_usable() {
        let mut binding = TenantBinding::Unbound;
        binding.bind(context(2_000), 1_000).unwrap();

        assert_eq!(binding.tenant_id(2_000), Err(BindingError::ContextExpired));
    }

    #[test]
    fn reset_allows_a_new_session_context() {
        let mut binding = TenantBinding::Unbound;
        binding.bind(context(2_000), 1_000).unwrap();
        binding.reset();

        let other = TenantContext {
            tenant_id: "tenant_b".into(),
            expires_at: 3_000,
            emergency: false,
            emergency_nonce_hash: None,
        };

        binding.bind(other, 1_000).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(binding.tenant_id(1_001).unwrap(), "tenant_b");
    }

    #[test]
    fn missing_context_fails_closed() {
        let binding = TenantBinding::Unbound;
        assert_eq!(binding.tenant_id(1_000), Err(BindingError::MissingContext));
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\protocol.rs ==========
use bytes::{Buf, Bytes, BytesMut};
use std::fmt;
use std::io;

pub const SSL_REQUEST_CODE: i32 = 80877103;
pub const CANCEL_REQUEST_CODE: i32 = 80877102;
pub const PROTOCOL_3_0: i32 = 196608;

const MAX_FRAME_LENGTH: usize = 16 * 1024 * 1024;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum StartupPacket {
    Startup {
        protocol_version: i32,
        parameters: Vec<(String, String)>,
    },
    SslRequest,
    CancelRequest {
        process_id: i32,
        secret_key: i32,
    },
    Unknown {
        code: i32,
        payload: Bytes,
    },
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct FrontendMessage {
    pub tag: u8,
    pub payload: Bytes,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct BackendMessage {
    pub tag: u8,
    pub payload: Bytes,
}

pub const AUTHENTICATION_TAG: u8 = b'R';
pub const ERROR_RESPONSE_TAG: u8 = b'E';
pub const READY_FOR_QUERY_TAG: u8 = b'Z';
pub const AUTH_OK: i32 = 0;
pub const AUTH_CLEARTEXT_PASSWORD: i32 = 3;
pub const AUTH_MD5_PASSWORD: i32 = 5;
pub const AUTH_SASL: i32 = 10;
pub const AUTH_SASL_CONTINUE: i32 = 11;
pub const AUTH_SASL_FINAL: i32 = 12;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum FrameError {
    Incomplete,
    InvalidLength(i32),
    FrameTooLarge(usize),
}

impl fmt::Display for FrameError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::Incomplete => write!(f, "incomplete PostgreSQL frame"),
            Self::InvalidLength(length) => write!(f, "invalid PostgreSQL frame length: {length}"),
            Self::FrameTooLarge(length) => {
                write!(f, "PostgreSQL frame exceeds maximum length: {length}")
            }
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for FrameError {}

pub fn parse_startup_packet(input: &[u8]) -> Result<Option<(StartupPacket, usize)>, FrameError> {
    if input.len() < 4 {
        return Ok(None);
    }

    let length = i32::from_be_bytes(input[0..4].try_into().unwrap());
    if length < 8 {
        return Err(FrameError::InvalidLength(length));
    }
    let length = length as usize;
    if length > MAX_FRAME_LENGTH {
        return Err(FrameError::FrameTooLarge(length));
    }
    if input.len() < length {
        return Ok(None);
    }

    let mut body = &input[4..length];
    let code = body.get_i32();

    let packet = match code {
        SSL_REQUEST_CODE if length == 8 => StartupPacket::SslRequest,
        CANCEL_REQUEST_CODE if length == 16 => StartupPacket::CancelRequest {
            process_id: body.get_i32(),
            secret_key: body.get_i32(),
        },
        PROTOCOL_3_0 => {
            let mut parameters = Vec::new();
            while body.has_remaining() {
                let key =
                    read_cstring(&mut body).ok_or(FrameError::InvalidLength(length as i32))?;
                if key.is_empty() {
                    break;
                }
                let value =
                    read_cstring(&mut body).ok_or(FrameError::InvalidLength(length as i32))?;
                parameters.push((key, value));
            }
            StartupPacket::Startup {
                protocol_version: code,
                parameters,
            }
        }
        _ => StartupPacket::Unknown {
            code,
            payload: Bytes::copy_from_slice(body),
        },
    };

    Ok(Some((packet, length)))
}

pub fn parse_backend_frame(input: &[u8]) -> Result<Option<(BackendMessage, usize)>, FrameError> {
    if input.len() < 5 {
        return Ok(None);
    }

    let tag = input[0];
    let length = i32::from_be_bytes(input[1..5].try_into().unwrap());

    if length < 4 {
        return Err(FrameError::InvalidLength(length));
    }

    let length = length as usize;
    if length > MAX_FRAME_LENGTH {
        return Err(FrameError::FrameTooLarge(length));
    }

    let total = 1usize
        .checked_add(length)
        .ok_or(FrameError::FrameTooLarge(length))?;

    if input.len() < total {
        return Ok(None);
    }

    Ok(Some((
        BackendMessage {
            tag,
            payload: Bytes::copy_from_slice(&input[5..total]),
        },
        total,
    )))
}

pub fn parse_frontend_frame(input: &[u8]) -> Result<Option<(FrontendMessage, usize)>, FrameError> {
    if input.len() < 5 {
        return Ok(None);
    }

    let tag = input[0];
    let length = i32::from_be_bytes(input[1..5].try_into().unwrap());

    if length < 4 {
        return Err(FrameError::InvalidLength(length));
    }

    let length = length as usize;
    if length > MAX_FRAME_LENGTH {
        return Err(FrameError::FrameTooLarge(length));
    }

    let total = 1usize
        .checked_add(length)
        .ok_or(FrameError::FrameTooLarge(length))?;

    if input.len() < total {
        return Ok(None);
    }

    Ok(Some((
        FrontendMessage {
            tag,
            payload: Bytes::copy_from_slice(&input[5..total]),
        },
        total,
    )))
}

fn read_cstring(input: &mut &[u8]) -> Option<String> {
    let nul = input.iter().position(|byte| *byte == 0)?;
    let value = std::str::from_utf8(&input[..nul]).ok()?.to_owned();
    *input = &input[nul + 1..];
    Some(value)
}

pub fn encode_frontend_frame(tag: u8, payload: &[u8]) -> io::Result<Bytes> {
    let length = payload
        .len()
        .checked_add(4)
        .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, "payload too large"))?;

    if length > MAX_FRAME_LENGTH {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
            "payload exceeds Proxima frame limit",
        ));
    }

    let length = u32::try_from(length)
        .map_err(|_| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, "frame length overflow"))?;

    let mut output = BytesMut::with_capacity(1 + 4 + payload.len());
    output.extend_from_slice(&[tag]);
    output.extend_from_slice(&length.to_be_bytes());
    output.extend_from_slice(payload);
    Ok(output.freeze())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use proptest::prelude::*;

    #[test]
    fn parses_postgres_startup_packet() {
        let mut packet = Vec::new();
        packet.extend_from_slice(&[0, 0, 0, 33]);
        packet.extend_from_slice(&PROTOCOL_3_0.to_be_bytes());
        packet.extend_from_slice(b"user\0alice\0database\0app\0\0");

        let (startup, consumed) = parse_startup_packet(&packet).unwrap().unwrap();

        assert_eq!(consumed, packet.len());
        assert_eq!(
            startup,
            StartupPacket::Startup {
                protocol_version: PROTOCOL_3_0,
                parameters: vec![
                    ("user".into(), "alice".into()),
                    ("database".into(), "app".into())
                ]
            }
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn recognizes_ssl_request() {
        let packet = [0, 0, 0, 8, 4, 210, 22, 47];

        let (startup, consumed) = parse_startup_packet(&packet).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(startup, StartupPacket::SslRequest);
        assert_eq!(consumed, 8);
    }

    #[test]
    fn recognizes_cancel_request() {
        let mut packet = Vec::new();
        packet.extend_from_slice(&16i32.to_be_bytes());
        packet.extend_from_slice(&CANCEL_REQUEST_CODE.to_be_bytes());
        packet.extend_from_slice(&42i32.to_be_bytes());
        packet.extend_from_slice(&99i32.to_be_bytes());

        let (startup, _) = parse_startup_packet(&packet).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            startup,
            StartupPacket::CancelRequest {
                process_id: 42,
                secret_key: 99
            }
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_startup_is_not_an_error() {
        let packet = [0, 0, 0, 33, 0];
        assert_eq!(parse_startup_packet(&packet).unwrap(), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_short_startup_length() {
        let packet = [0, 0, 0, 4, 0, 0, 0, 0];
        assert_eq!(
            parse_startup_packet(&packet),
            Err(FrameError::InvalidLength(4))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_backend_authentication_message() {
        let frame = [b'R', 0, 0, 0, 8, 0, 0, 0, 0];
        let (message, consumed) = parse_backend_frame(&frame).unwrap().unwrap();

        assert_eq!(message.tag, AUTHENTICATION_TAG);
        assert_eq!(&message.payload[..], &AUTH_OK.to_be_bytes());
        assert_eq!(consumed, frame.len());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_invalid_backend_length() {
        let frame = [b'Z', 0, 0, 0, 3, 0];
        assert_eq!(
            parse_backend_frame(&frame),
            Err(FrameError::InvalidLength(3))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_frontend_message() {
        let frame = [b'Q', 0, 0, 0, 11, b'S', b'E', b'L', b'E', b'C', b'T', 0];
        let (message, consumed) = parse_frontend_frame(&frame).unwrap().unwrap();

        assert_eq!(message.tag, b'Q');
        assert_eq!(&message.payload[..], b"SELECT\0");
        assert_eq!(consumed, frame.len());
    }

    #[test]
    fn frontend_frame_waits_for_complete_payload() {
        let frame = [b'Q', 0, 0, 0, 11, b'S'];
        assert_eq!(parse_frontend_frame(&frame).unwrap(), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_invalid_frontend_length() {
        let frame = [b'Q', 0, 0, 0, 3, 0];
        assert_eq!(
            parse_frontend_frame(&frame),
            Err(FrameError::InvalidLength(3))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn encodes_frontend_frame() {
        let frame = encode_frontend_frame(b'Q', b"SELECT").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(&frame[..], b"Q\0\0\0\nSELECT");
    }

    proptest! {
        #[test]
        fn random_bytes_never_panic_startup_or_frontend(
            input in proptest::collection::vec(any::<u8>(), 0..1024)
        ) {
            let _ = parse_startup_packet(&input);
            let _ = parse_frontend_frame(&input);
            let _ = parse_backend_frame(&input);
        }
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\redis_gateway.rs ==========
use crate::redis_protocol::{encode_command, parse_command, rewrite_keys, MAX_FRAME_BYTES};
use rustls::pki_types::{CertificateDer, PrivateKeyDer, ServerName};
use rustls::{ClientConfig, RootCertStore, ServerConfig};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use std::{
    collections::HashMap,
    fs::File,
    future::Future,
    io::{self, BufReader},
    net::SocketAddr,
    path::Path,
    pin::Pin,
    sync::Arc,
    time::Duration,
};
use tokio::{
    io::{AsyncRead, AsyncReadExt, AsyncWrite, AsyncWriteExt},
    net::{TcpListener, TcpStream},
    sync::Semaphore,
    time::timeout,
};
use tokio_rustls::{TlsAcceptor, TlsConnector};
use tracing::{info, warn};

trait RedisIo: AsyncRead + AsyncWrite + Unpin + Send {}
impl<T: AsyncRead + AsyncWrite + Unpin + Send> RedisIo for T {}
type Io = Box<dyn RedisIo>;

#[derive(Clone)]
struct Principal {
    tenant_id: String,
    client_hash: [u8; 32],
    upstream_user: String,
    upstream_password: String,
}
pub struct RedisIdentityRegistry {
    users: HashMap<String, Principal>,
}
impl RedisIdentityRegistry {
    pub fn load(path: impl AsRef<Path>) -> io::Result<Self> {
        let raw = std::fs::read(path)?;
        let root: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&raw).map_err(invalid)?;
        let records = root
            .get("users")
            .and_then(|v| v.as_array())
            .ok_or_else(|| invalid("users array required"))?;
        if records.is_empty() {
            return Err(invalid("at least one Redis principal is required"));
        }
        let mut users = HashMap::new();
        let mut tenant_users = HashMap::new();
        let mut upstream_tenants = HashMap::new();
        for (i, r) in records.iter().enumerate() {
            let field = |k: &str| -> io::Result<String> {
                r.get(k)
                    .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                    .map(str::trim)
                    .filter(|v| !v.is_empty())
                    .map(str::to_owned)
                    .ok_or_else(|| invalid(format!("users[{i}].{k} is required")))
            };
            let username = field("username")?;
            let tenant_id = field("tenant_id")?;
            let hash = hex::decode(field("client_password_sha256")?).map_err(invalid)?;
            let client_hash: [u8; 32] = hash.try_into().map_err(|_| {
                invalid(format!(
                    "users[{i}].client_password_sha256 must be 32 bytes"
                ))
            })?;
            let upstream_user = field("upstream_username")?;
            let upstream_password = field("upstream_password")?;
            if username.len() > 128
                || tenant_id.len() > 256
                || upstream_user.len() > 128
                || username.bytes().any(|b| !b.is_ascii_graphic())
