                || upstream_user.bytes().any(|b| !b.is_ascii_graphic())
            {
                return Err(invalid(format!(
                    "users[{i}] contains an invalid identifier"
                )));
            }
            if users.contains_key(&username) {
                return Err(invalid("duplicate Redis gateway username"));
            }
            if let Some(old) = tenant_users.insert(tenant_id.clone(), upstream_user.clone()) {
                if old != upstream_user {
                    return Err(invalid(
                        "one tenant cannot map to multiple upstream Redis ACL users",
                    ));
                }
            }
            if let Some(old_tenant) =
                upstream_tenants.insert(upstream_user.clone(), tenant_id.clone())
            {
                if old_tenant != tenant_id {
                    return Err(invalid(
                        "upstream Redis ACL users must be unique per tenant",
                    ));
                }
            }
            users.insert(
                username,
                Principal {
                    tenant_id,
                    client_hash,
                    upstream_user,
                    upstream_password,
                },
            );
        }
        Ok(Self { users })
    }
    fn authenticate(&self, user: &str, password: &[u8]) -> Option<Principal> {
        let p = self.users.get(user)?;
        let got = Sha256::digest(password);
        let mut diff = 0u8;
        for (a, b) in got.iter().zip(p.client_hash.iter()) {
            diff |= a ^ b;
        }
        if diff == 0 {
            Some(p.clone())
        } else {
            None
        }
    }
    pub fn principal_count(&self) -> usize {
        self.users.len()
    }
}
fn invalid(e: impl std::fmt::Display) -> io::Error {
    io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidData, e.to_string())
}

struct Config {
    listen: SocketAddr,
    upstream: String,
    upstream_name: String,
    ca: String,
    cert: String,
    key: String,
    max: usize,
}
impl Config {
    fn load() -> io::Result<Self> {
        let get = |k: &str| {
            std::env::var(k).map_err(|_| {
                io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, format!("{k} is required"))
            })
        };
        let listen = std::env::var("PROXIMA_REDIS_LISTEN_ADDR")
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| "127.0.0.1:6380".into())
            .parse()
            .map_err(invalid)?;
        let max = std::env::var("PROXIMA_REDIS_MAX_CONNECTIONS")
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| "128".into())
            .parse::<usize>()
            .map_err(invalid)?;
        if max == 0 {
            return Err(invalid("PROXIMA_REDIS_MAX_CONNECTIONS must be positive"));
        }
        Ok(Self {
            listen,
            upstream: get("PROXIMA_REDIS_UPSTREAM_ADDR")?,
            upstream_name: get("PROXIMA_REDIS_UPSTREAM_TLS_SERVER_NAME")?,
            ca: get("PROXIMA_REDIS_UPSTREAM_TLS_CA_FILE")?,
            cert: get("PROXIMA_REDIS_TLS_CERT_FILE")?,
            key: get("PROXIMA_REDIS_TLS_KEY_FILE")?,
            max,
        })
    }
}
pub async fn run_redis_gateway() -> io::Result<()> {
    let _ = rustls::crypto::ring::default_provider().install_default();
    let cfg = Config::load()?;
    let path = std::env::var("PROXIMA_REDIS_IDENTITY_REGISTRY").map_err(invalid)?;
    let registry = Arc::new(RedisIdentityRegistry::load(path)?);
    let acceptor = TlsAcceptor::from(Arc::new(server_tls(&cfg.cert, &cfg.key)?));
    let connector = TlsConnector::from(Arc::new(client_tls(&cfg.ca)?));
    let listener = TcpListener::bind(cfg.listen).await?;
    let permits = Arc::new(Semaphore::new(cfg.max));
    info!(listen=%cfg.listen,upstream=%cfg.upstream,principals=registry.principal_count(),max_connections=cfg.max,"Proxima Redis RESP2 gateway listening");
    loop {
        let (socket, peer) = listener.accept().await?;
        let permit = match permits.clone().try_acquire_owned() {
            Ok(p) => p,
            Err(_) => {
                warn!(%peer,"Redis gateway connection limit reached");
                drop(socket);
                continue;
            }
        };
        let (a, c, r, u, n) = (
            acceptor.clone(),
            connector.clone(),
            registry.clone(),
            cfg.upstream.clone(),
            cfg.upstream_name.clone(),
        );
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            let _permit = permit;
            if let Err(e) = session(socket, a, c, r, &u, &n).await {
                warn!(%peer,%e,"Redis gateway session closed");
            }
        });
    }
}
async fn session(
    socket: TcpStream,
    acceptor: TlsAcceptor,
    connector: TlsConnector,
    registry: Arc<RedisIdentityRegistry>,
    upstream_addr: &str,
    server_name: &str,
) -> io::Result<()> {
    let mut client: Io = Box::new(
        timeout(Duration::from_secs(10), acceptor.accept(socket))
            .await
            .map_err(|_| io::ErrorKind::TimedOut)??,
    );
    let mut input = Vec::new();
    let mut auth_attempts = 0usize;
    let principal = loop {
        auth_attempts += 1;
        if auth_attempts > 3 {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                "Redis authentication attempt limit reached",
            ));
        }
        let args = timeout(
            Duration::from_secs(10),
            read_command(&mut *client, &mut input),
        )
        .await
        .map_err(|_| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::TimedOut, "Redis authentication timed out"))??;
        let cmd = std::str::from_utf8(args.first().ok_or_else(|| invalid("empty command"))?)
            .map_err(invalid)?
            .to_ascii_uppercase();
        if cmd != "AUTH" {
            write_error(&mut *client, "authentication required").await?;
            continue;
        }
        let (user, password) = match args.as_slice() {
            [_, p] => ("default", p.as_slice()),
            [_, u, p] => (std::str::from_utf8(u).map_err(invalid)?, p.as_slice()),
            _ => {
                write_error(&mut *client, "invalid AUTH arguments").await?;
                continue;
            }
        };
        if let Some(p) = registry.authenticate(user, password) {
            break p;
        }
        write_error(&mut *client, "invalid credentials").await?;
    };
    let tcp = timeout(Duration::from_secs(10), TcpStream::connect(upstream_addr))
        .await
        .map_err(|_| io::ErrorKind::TimedOut)??;
    let name = ServerName::try_from(server_name.to_owned()).map_err(invalid)?;
    let mut upstream: Io = Box::new(
        timeout(Duration::from_secs(10), connector.connect(name, tcp))
            .await
            .map_err(|_| io::ErrorKind::TimedOut)??,
    );
    let auth = vec![
        b"AUTH".to_vec(),
        principal.upstream_user.as_bytes().to_vec(),
        principal.upstream_password.as_bytes().to_vec(),
    ];
    upstream.write_all(&encode_command(&auth)).await?;
    upstream.flush().await?;
    let reply = timeout(Duration::from_secs(30), read_resp_frame(&mut *upstream, 0))
        .await
        .map_err(|_| {
            io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::TimedOut, "Redis upstream response timed out")
        })??;
    if !reply.starts_with(b"+OK\r\n") {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
            "upstream Redis ACL authentication failed",
        ));
    }
    client.write_all(b"+OK\r\n").await?;
    client.flush().await?;
    loop {
        let mut args = match timeout(
            Duration::from_secs(60),
            read_command(&mut *client, &mut input),
        )
        .await
        {
            Ok(Ok(v)) => v,
            Ok(Err(e)) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::UnexpectedEof => return Ok(()),
            Ok(Err(e)) => return Err(e),
            Err(_) => {
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::TimedOut,
                    "Redis client idle timeout",
                ))
            }
        };
        let cmd = std::str::from_utf8(&args[0])
            .map_err(invalid)?
            .to_ascii_uppercase();
        match cmd.as_str() {
            "PING" => {
                if args.len() > 2 {
                    write_error(&mut *client, "wrong number of arguments").await?;
                } else if args.len() == 2 {
                    let mut out = b"$".to_vec();
                    out.extend_from_slice(args[1].len().to_string().as_bytes());
                    out.extend_from_slice(b"\r\n");
                    out.extend_from_slice(&args[1]);
                    out.extend_from_slice(b"\r\n");
                    client.write_all(&out).await?;
                } else {
                    client.write_all(b"+PONG\r\n").await?;
                }
                client.flush().await?;
                continue;
            }
            "QUIT" => {
                client.write_all(b"+OK\r\n").await?;
                client.flush().await?;
                return Ok(());
            }
            "CLIENT"
                if args.len() == 4
                    && args[1].eq_ignore_ascii_case(b"SETINFO")
                    && (args[2].eq_ignore_ascii_case(b"LIB-NAME")
                        || args[2].eq_ignore_ascii_case(b"LIB-VER")) =>
            {
                client.write_all(b"+OK\r\n").await?;
                client.flush().await?;
                continue;
            }
            _ => {}
        }
        if rewrite_keys(&mut args, &principal.tenant_id).is_err() {
            write_error(&mut *client, "command is not in the tenant-safe allowlist").await?;
            continue;
        }
        let frame = encode_command(&args);
        if frame.len() > MAX_FRAME_BYTES {
            return Err(invalid("rewritten Redis frame exceeds limit"));
        }
        timeout(Duration::from_secs(10), upstream.write_all(&frame))
            .await
            .map_err(|_| {
                io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::TimedOut, "Redis upstream write timed out")
            })??;
        upstream.flush().await?;
        let reply = timeout(Duration::from_secs(30), read_resp_frame(&mut *upstream, 0))
            .await
            .map_err(|_| {
                io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::TimedOut, "Redis upstream response timed out")
            })??;
        client.write_all(&reply).await?;
        client.flush().await?;
    }
}
async fn read_command<R: AsyncRead + Unpin + ?Sized>(
    r: &mut R,
    buf: &mut Vec<u8>,
) -> io::Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>> {
    loop {
        match parse_command(buf) {
            Ok(Some((args, n))) => {
                buf.drain(..n);
                return Ok(args);
            }
            Ok(None) => {}
            Err(e) => return Err(invalid(format!("invalid RESP command: {e:?}"))),
        }
        if buf.len() >= MAX_FRAME_BYTES {
            return Err(invalid("RESP request exceeds frame limit"));
        }
        let mut chunk = [0u8; 8192];
        let n = r.read(&mut chunk).await?;
        if n == 0 {
            return Err(io::ErrorKind::UnexpectedEof.into());
        }
        buf.extend_from_slice(&chunk[..n]);
    }
}
async fn line<R: AsyncRead + Unpin + ?Sized>(r: &mut R) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut v = Vec::new();
    loop {
        let b = r.read_u8().await?;
        v.push(b);
        if v.len() > 128 {
            return Err(invalid("RESP line too long"));
        }
        if v.ends_with(b"\r\n") {
            v.truncate(v.len() - 2);
            return Ok(v);
        }
    }
}
fn read_resp_frame<'a, R: AsyncRead + Unpin + Send + ?Sized>(
    r: &'a mut R,
    depth: usize,
) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = io::Result<Vec<u8>>> + Send + 'a>> {
    Box::pin(async move {
        if depth > 32 {
            return Err(invalid("RESP nesting limit exceeded"));
        }
        let ty = r.read_u8().await?;
        let mut out = vec![ty];
        match ty {
            b'+' | b'-' | b':' => {
                let l = line(r).await?;
                out.extend_from_slice(&l);
                out.extend_from_slice(b"\r\n");
            }
            b'$' => {
                let l = line(r).await?;
                let n = std::str::from_utf8(&l)
                    .map_err(invalid)?
                    .parse::<i64>()
                    .map_err(invalid)?;
                out.extend_from_slice(&l);
                out.extend_from_slice(b"\r\n");
                if n >= 0 {
                    if n as usize > MAX_FRAME_BYTES {
                        return Err(invalid("RESP bulk reply too large"));
                    }
                    let mut b = vec![0; (n as usize) + 2];
                    r.read_exact(&mut b).await?;
                    if !b.ends_with(b"\r\n") {
                        return Err(invalid("invalid RESP bulk terminator"));
                    }
                    out.extend_from_slice(&b);
                }
            }
            b'*' => {
                let l = line(r).await?;
                let n = std::str::from_utf8(&l)
                    .map_err(invalid)?
                    .parse::<i64>()
                    .map_err(invalid)?;
                out.extend_from_slice(&l);
                out.extend_from_slice(b"\r\n");
                if n >= 0 {
                    if n > 1024 {
                        return Err(invalid("RESP array too large"));
                    }
                    for _ in 0..n {
                        let child = read_resp_frame(r, depth + 1).await?;
                        out.extend_from_slice(&child);
                        if out.len() > MAX_FRAME_BYTES {
                            return Err(invalid("RESP reply too large"));
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            _ => {
                return Err(invalid(
                    "upstream returned unsupported RESP3 or invalid frame",
                ))
            }
        }
        if out.len() > MAX_FRAME_BYTES {
            return Err(invalid("RESP reply too large"));
        }
        Ok(out)
    })
}
async fn write_error<W: AsyncWrite + Unpin + ?Sized>(w: &mut W, msg: &str) -> io::Result<()> {
    let safe = msg.replace('\r', " ").replace('\n', " ");
    w.write_all(format!("-ERR {safe}\r\n").as_bytes()).await?;
    w.flush().await
}
fn server_tls(cert: &str, key: &str) -> io::Result<ServerConfig> {
    let certs = rustls_pemfile::certs(&mut BufReader::new(File::open(cert)?))
        .collect::<Result<Vec<CertificateDer<'static>>, _>>()
        .map_err(invalid)?;
    let key = rustls_pemfile::private_key(&mut BufReader::new(File::open(key)?))
        .map_err(invalid)?
        .ok_or_else(|| invalid("TLS private key missing"))?;
    ServerConfig::builder()
        .with_no_client_auth()
        .with_single_cert(certs, key)
        .map_err(invalid)
}
fn client_tls(ca: &str) -> io::Result<ClientConfig> {
    let mut roots = RootCertStore::empty();
    let certs = rustls_pemfile::certs(&mut BufReader::new(File::open(ca)?))
        .collect::<Result<Vec<CertificateDer<'static>>, _>>()
        .map_err(invalid)?;
    for cert in certs {
        roots.add(cert).map_err(invalid)?;
    }
    Ok(ClientConfig::builder()
        .with_root_certificates(roots)
        .with_no_client_auth())
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\redis_protocol.rs ==========
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
pub const MAX_FRAME_BYTES: usize = 1_048_576;
pub const MAX_ARGS: usize = 1024;
pub const MAX_BULK_BYTES: usize = 524_288;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum RespError {
    Malformed,
    LimitExceeded,
    UnsupportedCommand,
    InvalidTenant,
}

pub fn parse_command(input: &[u8]) -> Result<Option<(Vec<Vec<u8>>, usize)>, RespError> {
    if input.len() > MAX_FRAME_BYTES {
        return Err(RespError::LimitExceeded);
    }
    if input.is_empty() {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    if input[0] != b'*' {
        return Err(RespError::Malformed);
    }
    let (count, mut pos) = match decimal(input, 1)? {
        Some(v) => v,
        None => return Ok(None),
    };
    if count == 0 || count > MAX_ARGS {
        return Err(RespError::LimitExceeded);
    }
    let mut args = Vec::with_capacity(count);
    for _ in 0..count {
        if pos >= input.len() {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        if input[pos] != b'$' {
            return Err(RespError::Malformed);
        }
        let (len, start) = match decimal(input, pos + 1)? {
            Some(v) => v,
            None => return Ok(None),
        };
        if len > MAX_BULK_BYTES {
            return Err(RespError::LimitExceeded);
        }
        let end = start.checked_add(len).ok_or(RespError::LimitExceeded)?;
        let next = end.checked_add(2).ok_or(RespError::LimitExceeded)?;
        if next > input.len() {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        if &input[end..next] != b"\r\n" {
            return Err(RespError::Malformed);
        }
        args.push(input[start..end].to_vec());
        pos = next;
    }
    Ok(Some((args, pos)))
}
fn decimal(input: &[u8], start: usize) -> Result<Option<(usize, usize)>, RespError> {
    let Some(n) = input
        .get(start..)
        .and_then(|s| s.windows(2).position(|w| w == b"\r\n"))
    else {
        return Ok(None);
    };
    let end = start + n;
    let digits = &input[start..end];
    if digits.is_empty() || !digits.iter().all(u8::is_ascii_digit) {
        return Err(RespError::Malformed);
    }
    let value = std::str::from_utf8(digits)
        .map_err(|_| RespError::Malformed)?
        .parse::<usize>()
        .map_err(|_| RespError::LimitExceeded)?;
    Ok(Some((value, end + 2)))
}
pub fn tenant_prefix(tenant_id: &str) -> Result<Vec<u8>, RespError> {
    if tenant_id.trim().is_empty() || tenant_id.len() > 256 {
        return Err(RespError::InvalidTenant);
    }
    let digest = Sha256::digest(tenant_id.as_bytes());
    Ok(format!("proxima:{{{}}}:", hex::encode(&digest[..16])).into_bytes())
}
pub fn rewrite_keys(args: &mut [Vec<u8>], tenant_id: &str) -> Result<(), RespError> {
    if args.is_empty() {
        return Err(RespError::Malformed);
    }
    let cmd = std::str::from_utf8(&args[0])
        .map_err(|_| RespError::UnsupportedCommand)?
        .to_ascii_uppercase();
    let keys: Vec<usize> = match cmd.as_str() {
        "GET" | "SET" | "SETNX" | "GETSET" | "GETDEL" | "APPEND" | "STRLEN" | "INCR" | "DECR"
        | "INCRBY" | "DECRBY" | "EXPIRE" | "PEXPIRE" | "TTL" | "PTTL" | "PERSIST" | "TYPE"
        | "HGET" | "HSET" | "HSETNX" | "HMGET" | "HMSET" | "HDEL" | "HEXISTS" | "HLEN"
        | "HSTRLEN" | "HGETALL" | "LPUSH" | "RPUSH" | "LPOP" | "RPOP" | "LLEN" | "LRANGE"
        | "LINDEX" | "LSET" | "LREM" | "LTRIM" | "SADD" | "SREM" | "SISMEMBER" | "SMEMBERS"
        | "SCARD" | "SPOP" | "ZADD" | "ZREM" | "ZSCORE" | "ZRANGE" | "ZCARD" | "ZCOUNT"
        | "ZINCRBY" | "PFADD" | "GEOADD" | "XRANGE" | "XREVRANGE" | "XLEN" | "XADD" | "XDEL"
        | "XTRIM" | "XACK" => vec![1],
        "MGET" | "DEL" | "UNLINK" | "EXISTS" | "TOUCH" => {
            if args.len() < 2 {
                return Err(RespError::Malformed);
            }
            (1..args.len()).collect()
        }
        "MSET" | "MSETNX" => {
            if args.len() < 3 || args.len() % 2 == 0 {
                return Err(RespError::Malformed);
            }
            (1..args.len()).step_by(2).collect()
        }
        "RENAME" | "RENAMENX" | "RPOPLPUSH" | "SMOVE" | "COPY" => {
            if args.len() < 3 {
                return Err(RespError::Malformed);
            }
            vec![1, 2]
        }
        "BITOP" => {
            if args.len() < 4 {
                return Err(RespError::Malformed);
            }
            (2..args.len()).collect()
        }
        _ => return Err(RespError::UnsupportedCommand),
    };
    let prefix = tenant_prefix(tenant_id)?;
    for i in keys {
        let key = args.get_mut(i).ok_or(RespError::Malformed)?;
        let mut v = prefix.clone();
        v.extend_from_slice(key);
        *key = v;
    }
    Ok(())
}
pub fn encode_command(args: &[Vec<u8>]) -> Vec<u8> {
    let mut out = format!("*{}\r\n", args.len()).into_bytes();
    for arg in args {
        out.extend_from_slice(b"$");
        out.extend_from_slice(format!("{}\r\n", arg.len()).as_bytes());
        out.extend_from_slice(arg);
        out.extend_from_slice(b"\r\n");
    }
    out
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn frame(a: &[&[u8]]) -> Vec<u8> {
        encode_command(&a.iter().map(|v| v.to_vec()).collect::<Vec<_>>())
    }
    #[test]
    fn parses_binary_and_pipeline() {
        let one = frame(&[b"SET", b"k", b"a\0b"]);
        let mut data = one.clone();
        data.extend(frame(&[b"GET", b"k"]));
        let (a, n) = parse_command(&data).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(a[2], b"a\0b");
        assert_eq!(n, one.len());
        assert_eq!(parse_command(&data[n..]).unwrap().unwrap().0[0], b"GET");
    }
    #[test]
    fn incomplete_is_not_a_command() {
        assert_eq!(parse_command(b"*2\r\n$3\r\nGET\r\n$4\r\nk"), Ok(None));
    }
    #[test]
    fn tenant_prefix_is_opaque() {
        assert_ne!(tenant_prefix("a").unwrap(), tenant_prefix("b").unwrap());
        assert_eq!(tenant_prefix("a").unwrap(), tenant_prefix("a").unwrap());
    }
    #[test]
    fn mget_and_mset_namespace_keys_only() {
        let mut a = vec![b"MGET".to_vec(), b"a".to_vec(), b"b".to_vec()];
        rewrite_keys(&mut a, "t").unwrap();
        let p = tenant_prefix("t").unwrap();
        assert!(a[1].starts_with(&p) && a[2].starts_with(&p));
        let mut b = vec![b"MSET".to_vec(), b"k".to_vec(), b"v".to_vec()];
        rewrite_keys(&mut b, "t").unwrap();
        assert!(b[1].starts_with(&p));
        assert_eq!(b[2], b"v");
    }
    #[test]
    fn bitop_rewrites_destination_and_sources_but_not_operation() {
        let mut a = vec![
            b"BITOP".to_vec(),
            b"AND".to_vec(),
            b"dest".to_vec(),
            b"left".to_vec(),
            b"right".to_vec(),
        ];
        rewrite_keys(&mut a, "t").unwrap();
        let p = tenant_prefix("t").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(a[1], b"AND");
        assert!(a[2].starts_with(&p) && a[3].starts_with(&p) && a[4].starts_with(&p));
    }
    #[test]
    fn rejects_empty_and_malformed_command_shapes() {
        for mut a in [
            vec![b"GET".to_vec()],
            vec![b"MSET".to_vec(), b"k".to_vec()],
            vec![b"BITOP".to_vec(), b"AND".to_vec(), b"d".to_vec()],
        ] {
            assert_eq!(rewrite_keys(&mut a, "t"), Err(RespError::Malformed));
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn dangerous_commands_are_denied() {
        for c in [
            "FLUSHALL",
            "CONFIG",
            "ACL",
            "EVAL",
            "SUBSCRIBE",
            "PUBLISH",
            "MONITOR",
            "SELECT",
            "MODULE",
        ] {
            let mut a = vec![c.as_bytes().to_vec(), b"x".to_vec()];
            assert_eq!(
                rewrite_keys(&mut a, "t"),
                Err(RespError::UnsupportedCommand)
            );
        }
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\runtime_activation.rs ==========
use std::{collections::HashMap, io, net::SocketAddr, process::Stdio, sync::Arc, time::Duration};

use tokio::{
    net::TcpStream,
    process::{Child, Command},
    sync::Mutex,
    time::{sleep, timeout, Instant},
};

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct GatewayProcessSpec {
    pub assignment_id: String,
    pub executable: String,
    pub args: Vec<String>,
    pub env: Vec<(String, String)>,
    pub readiness_addr: SocketAddr,
    pub startup_timeout: Duration,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct RuntimeProcessSupervisor {
    children: Arc<Mutex<HashMap<String, Child>>>,
}

impl RuntimeProcessSupervisor {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            children: Arc::new(Mutex::new(HashMap::new())),
        }
    }

    pub async fn start(&self, spec: GatewayProcessSpec) -> io::Result<()> {
        if spec.assignment_id.trim().is_empty() {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                "assignment_id cannot be empty",
            ));
        }

        if !spec.readiness_addr.ip().is_loopback() {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                "gateway readiness address must be loopback",
            ));
        }

        {
            let children = self.children.lock().await;
            if children.contains_key(&spec.assignment_id) {
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists,
                    "assignment already has a supervised process",
                ));
            }
        }

        let mut command = Command::new(&spec.executable);
        command
            .args(&spec.args)
            .stdin(Stdio::null())
            .stdout(Stdio::null())
            .stderr(Stdio::null())
            .kill_on_drop(true);

        for (key, value) in &spec.env {
            command.env(key, value);
        }

        let child = command.spawn()?;

        {
            let mut children = self.children.lock().await;
            children.insert(spec.assignment_id.clone(), child);
        }

        match self
            .wait_until_ready(
                &spec.assignment_id,
                spec.readiness_addr,
                spec.startup_timeout,
            )
            .await
        {
            Ok(()) => Ok(()),
            Err(error) => {
                let _ = self.stop(&spec.assignment_id).await;
                Err(error)
            }
        }
    }

    async fn wait_until_ready(
        &self,
        assignment_id: &str,
        address: SocketAddr,
        startup_timeout: Duration,
    ) -> io::Result<()> {
        let deadline = Instant::now() + startup_timeout;

        loop {
            {
                let mut children = self.children.lock().await;
                let child = children.get_mut(assignment_id).ok_or_else(|| {
                    io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::NotFound,
                        "supervised process disappeared during startup",
                    )
                })?;

                if let Some(status) = child.try_wait()? {
                    children.remove(assignment_id);
                    return Err(io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::Other,
                        format!("gateway exited before readiness: {status}"),
                    ));
                }
            }

            if timeout(Duration::from_millis(500), TcpStream::connect(address))
                .await
                .is_ok_and(|result| result.is_ok())
            {
                return Ok(());
            }

            if Instant::now() >= deadline {
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::TimedOut,
                    "gateway did not become reachable before startup timeout",
                ));
            }

            sleep(Duration::from_millis(200)).await;
        }
    }

    pub async fn stop(&self, assignment_id: &str) -> io::Result<()> {
        let child = {
            let mut children = self.children.lock().await;
            children.remove(assignment_id)
        };

        if let Some(mut child) = child {
            match child.try_wait()? {
                Some(_) => Ok(()),
                None => {
                    child.start_kill()?;
                    let _ = child.wait().await?;
                    Ok(())
                }
            }
        } else {
            Ok(())
        }
    }

    pub async fn stop_all(&self) -> io::Result<()> {
        let assignment_ids: Vec<String> = {
            let children = self.children.lock().await;
            children.keys().cloned().collect()
        };

        for assignment_id in assignment_ids {
            self.stop(&assignment_id).await?;
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn is_running(&self, assignment_id: &str) -> bool {
        let mut children = self.children.lock().await;

        match children.get_mut(assignment_id) {
            Some(child) => matches!(child.try_wait(), Ok(None)),
            None => false,
        }
    }
}

impl Default for RuntimeProcessSupervisor {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\runtime_coordinator.rs ==========
use std::{future::Future, io, path::Path};

use crate::{
    runtime_activation::{GatewayProcessSpec, RuntimeProcessSupervisor},
    runtime_reconciliation::{self, RuntimeRecord},
};

pub struct RuntimeCoordinator {
    supervisor: RuntimeProcessSupervisor,
}

impl RuntimeCoordinator {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            supervisor: RuntimeProcessSupervisor::new(),
        }
    }

    pub async fn activate<F, Fut>(
        &self,
        state_path: &Path,
        state: &mut RuntimeRecord,
        spec: GatewayProcessSpec,
        provider_health_check: F,
    ) -> io::Result<()>
    where
        F: FnOnce() -> Fut,
        Fut: Future<Output = io::Result<()>>,
    {
        if spec.assignment_id != state.assignment_id {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                "gateway assignment does not match runtime state",
            ));
        }

        let revision = state.desired_revision;
        let credential_revision = state.credential_revision;

        state.begin_apply(revision, credential_revision)?;
        runtime_reconciliation::persist(state_path, state)?;

        if let Err(error) = self.supervisor.start(spec).await {
            state.mark_failed("GATEWAY_START_FAILED")?;
            runtime_reconciliation::persist(state_path, state)?;
            return Err(error);
        }

        if let Err(error) = provider_health_check().await {
            let _ = self.supervisor.stop(&state.assignment_id).await;
            state.mark_failed("PROVIDER_HEALTH_FAILED")?;
            runtime_reconciliation::persist(state_path, state)?;
            return Err(error);
        }

        if let Err(error) = state.mark_applied(revision, true) {
            let _ = self.supervisor.stop(&state.assignment_id).await;
            state.mark_failed("RUNTIME_STATE_TRANSITION_FAILED")?;
            runtime_reconciliation::persist(state_path, state)?;
            return Err(error);
        }

        if let Err(error) = runtime_reconciliation::persist(state_path, state) {
            let _ = self.supervisor.stop(&state.assignment_id).await;
            return Err(error);
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn revoke(&self, state_path: &Path, state: &mut RuntimeRecord) -> io::Result<()> {
        state.begin_revocation();
        runtime_reconciliation::persist(state_path, state)?;

        self.supervisor.stop(&state.assignment_id).await
    }

    pub async fn is_running(&self, assignment_id: &str) -> bool {
        self.supervisor.is_running(assignment_id).await
    }

    pub async fn shutdown(&self) -> io::Result<()> {
        self.supervisor.stop_all().await
    }
}

impl Default for RuntimeCoordinator {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\runtime_reconciliation.rs ==========
//! Durable desired-versus-applied state for node database assignments.
//!
//! This module deliberately does not equate a downloaded or decrypted bundle with
//! an applied runtime. Callers may transition to Applied only after the provider
//! runtime has been activated and its provider-specific health/isolation checks pass.

use std::{
    fs::{self, File, OpenOptions},
    io::{self, Write},
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
    time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH},
};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum RuntimePhase {
    Empty,
    Staged,
    Applying,
    Applied,
    Failed,
    Revoked,
}

impl RuntimePhase {
    fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Empty => "empty",
            Self::Staged => "staged",
            Self::Applying => "applying",
            Self::Applied => "applied",
            Self::Failed => "failed",
            Self::Revoked => "revoked",
        }
    }

    fn parse(value: &str) -> io::Result<Self> {
        match value {
            "empty" => Ok(Self::Empty),
            "staged" => Ok(Self::Staged),
            "applying" => Ok(Self::Applying),
            "applied" => Ok(Self::Applied),
            "failed" => Ok(Self::Failed),
            "revoked" => Ok(Self::Revoked),
            _ => Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                "unknown runtime phase",
            )),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct RuntimeRecord {
    pub assignment_id: String,
    pub target_id: String,
    pub provider: String,
    pub desired_revision: u64,
    pub applied_revision: Option<u64>,
    pub phase: RuntimePhase,
    pub runtime_health_passed: bool,
    pub credential_revision: u64,
    pub last_error_code: Option<String>,
    pub updated_at_unix: u64,
}

impl RuntimeRecord {
    pub fn staged(
        assignment_id: String,
        target_id: String,
        provider: String,
        revision: u64,
        credential_revision: u64,
    ) -> io::Result<Self> {
        if assignment_id.trim().is_empty()
            || target_id.trim().is_empty()
            || provider.trim().is_empty()
            || revision == 0
        {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                "invalid assignment identity or revision",
            ));
        }
        Ok(Self {
            assignment_id,
            target_id,
            provider,
            desired_revision: revision,
            applied_revision: None,
            phase: RuntimePhase::Staged,
            runtime_health_passed: false,
            credential_revision,
            last_error_code: None,
            updated_at_unix: now(),
        })
    }

    /// Begins reconciliation. Older revisions and identity changes cannot overwrite
    /// the durable record for an existing assignment.
    pub fn begin_apply(&mut self, revision: u64, credential_revision: u64) -> io::Result<()> {
        if revision != self.desired_revision || revision == 0 {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                "desired revision mismatch",
            ));
        }
        if credential_revision < self.credential_revision {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                "credential revision rollback rejected",
            ));
        }
        if self.phase == RuntimePhase::Revoked {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                "revoked assignment cannot be applied",
            ));
        }
        self.credential_revision = credential_revision;
        self.phase = RuntimePhase::Applying;
        self.runtime_health_passed = false;
        self.last_error_code = None;
        self.updated_at_unix = now();
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Reconcile a newer authenticated Cloud desired revision without claiming
    /// that the runtime is active. Preserve the last applied revision so callers
    /// can keep the previous runtime as the rollback target until replacement health
    /// checks pass. Assignment identity and both revision counters are monotonic.
    pub fn update_desired(
        &mut self,
        assignment_id: &str,
        target_id: &str,
        provider: &str,
        revision: u64,
        credential_revision: u64,
    ) -> io::Result<()> {
        if self.phase == RuntimePhase::Revoked {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                "revoked assignment cannot be reused",
            ));
        }
        if assignment_id != self.assignment_id
            || target_id != self.target_id
            || provider != self.provider
        {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                "assignment identity change requires a new runtime record",
            ));
        }
        if revision < self.desired_revision || revision == 0 {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                "configuration revision rollback rejected",
            ));
        }
        if credential_revision < self.credential_revision {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                "credential revision rollback rejected",
            ));
        }
        if revision == self.desired_revision && credential_revision == self.credential_revision {
            return Ok(());
        }
        self.desired_revision = revision;
        self.credential_revision = credential_revision;
        self.phase = RuntimePhase::Staged;
        self.runtime_health_passed = false;
        self.last_error_code = None;
        self.updated_at_unix = now();
        Ok(())
    }

    /// This is the only transition that records an applied revision. The caller
    /// must supply proof that runtime activation and provider-specific health checks
    /// have already completed successfully.
    pub fn mark_applied(&mut self, revision: u64, provider_health_passed: bool) -> io::Result<()> {
        if self.phase != RuntimePhase::Applying || revision != self.desired_revision {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                "assignment is not applying this revision",
            ));
        }
        if !provider_health_passed {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                "provider health verification is required",
            ));
        }
        self.applied_revision = Some(revision);
        self.runtime_health_passed = true;
        self.phase = RuntimePhase::Applied;
        self.last_error_code = None;
        self.updated_at_unix = now();
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn mark_failed(&mut self, error_code: &str) -> io::Result<()> {
        let code = error_code.trim();
        if code.is_empty()
            || code.len() > 96
            || !code
                .bytes()
                .all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'_' || b == b'-')
        {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                "invalid failure code",
            ));
        }
        self.phase = RuntimePhase::Failed;
        self.runtime_health_passed = false;
        self.last_error_code = Some(code.to_owned());
        self.updated_at_unix = now();
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Revocation invalidates the applied marker immediately. The caller must
    /// stop the runtime and revoke provider identities before reporting revocation
    /// as externally complete.
    pub fn begin_revocation(&mut self) {
        self.phase = RuntimePhase::Revoked;
        self.runtime_health_passed = false;
        self.applied_revision = None;
        self.last_error_code = None;
        self.updated_at_unix = now();
    }

    fn to_json(&self) -> String {
        serde_json::json!({
            "schema_version": 1,
            "assignment_id": self.assignment_id,
            "target_id": self.target_id,
            "provider": self.provider,
            "desired_revision": self.desired_revision,
            "applied_revision": self.applied_revision,
            "phase": self.phase.as_str(),
            "runtime_health_passed": self.runtime_health_passed,
            "credential_revision": self.credential_revision,
            "last_error_code": self.last_error_code,
            "updated_at_unix": self.updated_at_unix
        })
        .to_string()
    }

    fn from_json(raw: &str) -> io::Result<Self> {
        let value: serde_json::Value =
            serde_json::from_str(raw).map_err(|e| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidData, e))?;
        if value.get("schema_version").and_then(|v| v.as_u64()) != Some(1) {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                "unsupported runtime state schema",
            ));
        }
        let string = |key: &str| {
            value
                .get(key)
                .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                .map(str::to_owned)
                .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidData, format!("missing {key}")))
        };
        let number = |key: &str| {
            value
                .get(key)
                .and_then(|v| v.as_u64())
                .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidData, format!("missing {key}")))
        };
        let phase = RuntimePhase::parse(&string("phase")?)?;
        let applied_revision = match value.get("applied_revision") {
            Some(serde_json::Value::Number(n)) => n.as_u64(),
            Some(serde_json::Value::Null) | None => None,
            _ => {
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                    "invalid applied_revision",
                ))
            }
        };
        let runtime_health_passed = value
            .get("runtime_health_passed")
            .and_then(|v| v.as_bool())
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidData, "missing runtime_health_passed")
            })?;
        if runtime_health_passed
            != (phase == RuntimePhase::Applied
                && applied_revision == Some(number("desired_revision")?))
        {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                "inconsistent applied health marker",
            ));
        }
        Ok(Self {
            assignment_id: string("assignment_id")?,
            target_id: string("target_id")?,
            provider: string("provider")?,
            desired_revision: number("desired_revision")?,
            applied_revision,
            phase,
            runtime_health_passed,
            credential_revision: number("credential_revision")?,
            last_error_code: value
                .get("last_error_code")
                .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                .map(str::to_owned),
            updated_at_unix: number("updated_at_unix")?,
        })
    }
}

/// Atomically persist state using a same-directory temporary file and rename.
/// On Unix, the state file is restricted to its owner (0600).
pub fn persist(path: impl AsRef<Path>, record: &RuntimeRecord) -> io::Result<()> {
    let path = path.as_ref();
    let parent = path
        .parent()
        .filter(|p| !p.as_os_str().is_empty())
        .unwrap_or(Path::new("."));
    fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
    let mut temp = PathBuf::from(path);
    let suffix = format!("tmp-{}", std::process::id());
    let name = path
        .file_name()
        .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, "state path has no filename"))?;
    temp.set_file_name(format!("{}.{}", name.to_string_lossy(), suffix));
    let mut options = OpenOptions::new();
    options.write(true).create(true).truncate(true);
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;
        options.mode(0o600);
    }
    let result = (|| {
        let mut file = options.open(&temp)?;
        file.write_all(record.to_json().as_bytes())?;
        file.sync_all()?;
        fs::rename(&temp, path)?;
        #[cfg(unix)]
        std::fs::File::open(parent)?.sync_all()?;
        Ok(())
    })();
    if result.is_err() {
        let _ = fs::remove_file(&temp);
    }
    result
}

pub fn load(path: impl AsRef<Path>) -> io::Result<RuntimeRecord> {
    let raw = fs::read_to_string(path)?;
    RuntimeRecord::from_json(&raw)
}

fn now() -> u64 {
    SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .as_secs()
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn applied_requires_health_and_matching_revision() {
        let mut state =
            RuntimeRecord::staged("a".into(), "t".into(), "postgresql".into(), 8, 2).unwrap();
        state.begin_apply(8, 2).unwrap();
        assert!(state.mark_applied(8, false).is_err());
        state.mark_applied(8, true).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(state.applied_revision, Some(8));
        assert!(state.runtime_health_passed);
    }

    #[test]
    fn stale_and_credential_rollback_are_rejected() {
        let mut state =
            RuntimeRecord::staged("a".into(), "t".into(), "mongodb".into(), 9, 4).unwrap();
        assert!(state.begin_apply(8, 4).is_err());
        assert!(state.begin_apply(9, 3).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn desired_revision_updates_preserve_last_applied_revision_until_verified() {
        let mut state =
            RuntimeRecord::staged("a".into(), "t".into(), "mysql".into(), 4, 2).unwrap();
        state.begin_apply(4, 2).unwrap();
        state.mark_applied(4, true).unwrap();
        state.update_desired("a", "t", "mysql", 5, 3).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(state.desired_revision, 5);
        assert_eq!(state.credential_revision, 3);
        assert_eq!(state.applied_revision, Some(4));
        assert_eq!(state.phase, RuntimePhase::Staged);
        assert!(!state.runtime_health_passed);
        state.begin_apply(5, 3).unwrap();
        state.mark_applied(5, true).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(state.applied_revision, Some(5));
    }

    #[test]
    fn desired_revision_rejects_identity_and_revision_rollback() {
        let mut state =
            RuntimeRecord::staged("a".into(), "t".into(), "mongodb".into(), 7, 4).unwrap();
        assert!(state.update_desired("other", "t", "mongodb", 8, 4).is_err());
        assert!(state.update_desired("a", "other", "mongodb", 8, 4).is_err());
        assert!(state.update_desired("a", "t", "redis", 8, 4).is_err());
        assert!(state.update_desired("a", "t", "mongodb", 6, 4).is_err());
        assert!(state.update_desired("a", "t", "mongodb", 8, 3).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn revoked_assignment_cannot_be_reused_for_a_new_revision() {
        let mut state =
            RuntimeRecord::staged("a".into(), "t".into(), "redis".into(), 3, 1).unwrap();
        state.begin_revocation();
        assert!(state.update_desired("a", "t", "redis", 4, 2).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn revoked_assignment_cannot_be_reapplied() {
        let mut state =
            RuntimeRecord::staged("a".into(), "t".into(), "redis".into(), 3, 1).unwrap();
        state.begin_revocation();
        assert!(state.begin_apply(3, 1).is_err());
        assert_eq!(state.applied_revision, None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn durable_state_round_trips_and_rejects_corrupt_health_claims() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let path = dir.path().join("runtime-state.json");
        let mut state =
            RuntimeRecord::staged("a".into(), "t".into(), "postgresql".into(), 2, 1).unwrap();
        state.begin_apply(2, 1).unwrap();
        state.mark_applied(2, true).unwrap();
        persist(&path, &state).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(load(&path).unwrap(), state);
        let tampered = state.to_json().replace(
            "\"runtime_health_passed\":true",
            "\"runtime_health_passed\":false",
        );
        let tampered_path = dir.path().join("tampered-runtime-state.json");
        fs::write(&tampered_path, tampered).unwrap();
        assert!(load(&tampered_path).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn failure_never_preserves_healthy_marker() {
        let mut state =
            RuntimeRecord::staged("a".into(), "t".into(), "mysql".into(), 1, 1).unwrap();
        state.begin_apply(1, 1).unwrap();
        state.mark_failed("UPSTREAM_AUTH_FAILED").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(state.phase, RuntimePhase::Failed);
        assert!(!state.runtime_health_passed);
        assert_eq!(state.applied_revision, None);
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\search_cluster.rs ==========
use reqwest::{Client, Method, StatusCode};
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::{fmt, time::Duration};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum SearchProvider {
    Elasticsearch,
    OpenSearch,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum SearchError {
    InvalidConfiguration(&'static str),
    InvalidTenant,
    Http(String),
    Serialization(String),
    UnhealthyCluster(String),
    IdentityVerificationFailed,
}

impl fmt::Display for SearchError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::InvalidConfiguration(v) => write!(f, "invalid search cluster configuration: {v}"),
            Self::InvalidTenant => write!(f, "invalid tenant identifier"),
            Self::Http(v) => write!(f, "search cluster API request failed: {v}"),
            Self::Serialization(v) => write!(f, "search cluster response error: {v}"),
            Self::UnhealthyCluster(v) => write!(f, "search cluster is unhealthy: {v}"),
            Self::IdentityVerificationFailed => {
                write!(f, "tenant identity or role verification failed")
            }
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for SearchError {}

pub struct TenantSearchCredential {
    pub provider: SearchProvider,
    pub tenant_id: String,
    pub username: String,
    pub role_name: String,
    password: String,
}

impl TenantSearchCredential {
    /// The caller must transfer this secret directly into its encrypted
    /// credential store. Do not log it or place it in ordinary configuration.
    pub fn expose_secret(&self) -> &str {
        &self.password
    }
}

pub struct SearchClusterClient {
    provider: SearchProvider,
    endpoint: String,
    admin_username: String,
    admin_password: String,
    client: Client,
}

impl SearchClusterClient {
    pub fn connect(
        provider: SearchProvider,
        endpoint: &str,
        admin_username: &str,
        admin_password: &str,
        ca_pem: Option<&[u8]>,
    ) -> Result<Self, SearchError> {
        let parsed = reqwest::Url::parse(endpoint)
            .map_err(|_| SearchError::InvalidConfiguration("endpoint URL is invalid"))?;

        if parsed.scheme() != "https"
            || parsed.host_str().is_none()
            || !parsed.username().is_empty()
            || parsed.password().is_some()
            || parsed.query().is_some()
            || parsed.fragment().is_some()
            || !matches!(parsed.path(), "" | "/")
        {
            return Err(SearchError::InvalidConfiguration(
                "endpoint must be an HTTPS origin without embedded credentials, query, fragment or path",
            ));
        }

        if admin_username.trim().is_empty() || admin_password.is_empty() {
            return Err(SearchError::InvalidConfiguration(
                "administrative username and password are required",
            ));
        }

        let mut builder = Client::builder()
            .https_only(true)
            .connect_timeout(Duration::from_secs(10))
            .timeout(Duration::from_secs(30))
            .pool_idle_timeout(Duration::from_secs(90))
            .redirect(reqwest::redirect::Policy::none());

        if let Some(pem) = ca_pem {
            let certificate = reqwest::Certificate::from_pem(pem).map_err(|e| {
                SearchError::InvalidConfiguration(Box::leak(
                    format!("invalid CA certificate: {e}").into_boxed_str(),
                ))
            })?;
            builder = builder.add_root_certificate(certificate);
        }

        let client = builder
            .build()
            .map_err(|e| SearchError::Http(e.to_string()))?;

        Ok(Self {
            provider,
            endpoint: endpoint.trim_end_matches('/').to_owned(),
            admin_username: admin_username.to_owned(),
            admin_password: admin_password.to_owned(),
            client,
        })
    }

    fn resource_path(&self, kind: &str, name: &str) -> Result<String, SearchError> {
        if name.is_empty()
            || name.len() > 128
            || !name
                .bytes()
                .all(|b| b.is_ascii_lowercase() || b.is_ascii_digit() || b == b'_' || b == b'-')
        {
            return Err(SearchError::InvalidTenant);
        }

        let path = match (self.provider, kind) {
            (SearchProvider::Elasticsearch, "role") => format!("/_security/role/{name}"),
            (SearchProvider::Elasticsearch, "user") => format!("/_security/user/{name}"),
            (SearchProvider::OpenSearch, "role") => format!("/_plugins/_security/api/roles/{name}"),
            (SearchProvider::OpenSearch, "user") => {
                format!("/_plugins/_security/api/internalusers/{name}")
            }
            _ => return Err(SearchError::InvalidConfiguration("unknown resource type")),
        };

        Ok(path)
    }

    async fn request(
        &self,
        method: Method,
        path: &str,
        body: Option<&Value>,
        allow_not_found: bool,
    ) -> Result<Value, SearchError> {
        let url = format!("{}{}", self.endpoint, path);
        let mut request = self
            .client
            .request(method, url)
            .basic_auth(&self.admin_username, Some(&self.admin_password))
            .header(reqwest::header::ACCEPT, "application/json");

        if let Some(value) = body {
            request = request
                .header(reqwest::header::CONTENT_TYPE, "application/json")
                .json(value);
        }

        let response = request
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| SearchError::Http(e.to_string()))?;

        let status = response.status();

        if allow_not_found && status == StatusCode::NOT_FOUND {
            return Ok(json!({"already_absent": true}));
        }

        let text = response
            .text()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| SearchError::Http(e.to_string()))?;

        if !status.is_success() {
            return Err(SearchError::Http(format!(
                "HTTP {status}: {}",
                text.chars().take(1200).collect::<String>()
            )));
        }

        if text.trim().is_empty() {
            return Ok(json!({}));
        }

        serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e| SearchError::Serialization(e.to_string()))
    }

    pub async fn health_check(&self) -> Result<(), SearchError> {
        let response = self
            .request(Method::GET, "/_cluster/health", None, false)
            .await?;

        let status = response
            .get("status")
            .and_then(Value::as_str)
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                SearchError::Serialization("cluster health response has no status".into())
            })?;

        if status == "red" {
            return Err(SearchError::UnhealthyCluster(status.to_owned()));
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    fn role_body(&self, tenant_id: &str) -> Result<Value, SearchError> {
        match self.provider {
            SearchProvider::Elasticsearch => {
                crate::elasticsearch_tenant::elasticsearch_role_descriptor(tenant_id)
                    .ok_or(SearchError::InvalidTenant)
            }
            SearchProvider::OpenSearch => {
                crate::opensearch_tenant::opensearch_role_descriptor(tenant_id)
                    .ok_or(SearchError::InvalidTenant)
            }
        }
    }

    pub async fn provision_tenant(
        &self,
        tenant_id: &str,
    ) -> Result<TenantSearchCredential, SearchError> {
        self.health_check().await?;

        let valid = match self.provider {
            SearchProvider::Elasticsearch => {
                crate::elasticsearch_tenant::valid_tenant_id(tenant_id)
            }
            SearchProvider::OpenSearch => crate::opensearch_tenant::valid_tenant_id(tenant_id),
        };

        if !valid {
            return Err(SearchError::InvalidTenant);
        }

        let role_name = format!("proxima_tenant_{tenant_id}");
        let username = format!("px_{tenant_id}");
        let password = format!(
            "{}{}{}",
            uuid::Uuid::new_v4().simple(),
            uuid::Uuid::new_v4().simple(),
            uuid::Uuid::new_v4().simple()
        );

        let role_path = self.resource_path("role", &role_name)?;
        let user_path = self.resource_path("user", &username)?;
        let role_body = self.role_body(tenant_id)?;

        self.request(Method::PUT, &role_path, Some(&role_body), false)
            .await?;

        let user_body = match self.provider {
            SearchProvider::Elasticsearch => json!({
                "password": password,
                "roles": [role_name],
                "full_name": format!("Proxima tenant {tenant_id}"),
                "enabled": true
            }),
            SearchProvider::OpenSearch => json!({
                "password": password,
                "backend_roles": [role_name],
                "attributes": {
                    "managed_by": "proxima-engine",
                    "tenant_id": tenant_id
                }
            }),
        };

        self.request(Method::PUT, &user_path, Some(&user_body), false)
            .await?;

        let credential = TenantSearchCredential {
            provider: self.provider,
            tenant_id: tenant_id.to_owned(),
            username,
            role_name,
            password,
        };

        self.verify_tenant(&credential).await?;
        Ok(credential)
    }

    pub async fn rotate_credential(
        &self,
        credential: &mut TenantSearchCredential,
    ) -> Result<(), SearchError> {
        if credential.provider != self.provider {
            return Err(SearchError::InvalidConfiguration(
                "credential belongs to a different provider",
            ));
        }

        self.verify_tenant(credential).await?;

        let password = format!(
            "{}{}{}",
            uuid::Uuid::new_v4().simple(),
            uuid::Uuid::new_v4().simple(),
            uuid::Uuid::new_v4().simple()
        );

        let user_path = self.resource_path("user", &credential.username)?;
        let body = match self.provider {
            SearchProvider::Elasticsearch => json!({
                "password": password,
                "roles": [credential.role_name],
                "full_name": format!("Proxima tenant {}", credential.tenant_id),
                "enabled": true
            }),
            SearchProvider::OpenSearch => json!({
                "password": password,
                "backend_roles": [credential.role_name],
                "attributes": {
                    "managed_by": "proxima-engine",
                    "tenant_id": credential.tenant_id
                }
            }),
        };

        self.request(Method::PUT, &user_path, Some(&body), false)
            .await?;
        credential.password = password;
        self.verify_tenant(credential).await
    }

    pub async fn verify_tenant(
        &self,
        credential: &TenantSearchCredential,
    ) -> Result<(), SearchError> {
        if credential.provider != self.provider {
            return Err(SearchError::InvalidConfiguration(
                "credential belongs to a different provider",
            ));
        }

        let user_path = self.resource_path("user", &credential.username)?;
        let role_path = self.resource_path("role", &credential.role_name)?;

        let user = self.request(Method::GET, &user_path, None, false).await?;
        let role = self.request(Method::GET, &role_path, None, false).await?;

        let user_exists = match self.provider {
            SearchProvider::Elasticsearch => user.get(&credential.username).is_some(),
            SearchProvider::OpenSearch => {
                user.get("user").is_some() || user.get(&credential.username).is_some()
            }
        };

        if !user_exists || role.is_null() || role.as_object().is_some_and(|v| v.is_empty()) {
            return Err(SearchError::IdentityVerificationFailed);
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn revoke_tenant(
        &self,
        credential: &TenantSearchCredential,
    ) -> Result<(), SearchError> {
        if credential.provider != self.provider {
            return Err(SearchError::InvalidConfiguration(
                "credential belongs to a different provider",
            ));
        }

        let user_path = self.resource_path("user", &credential.username)?;
        let role_path = self.resource_path("role", &credential.role_name)?;

        self.request(Method::DELETE, &user_path, None, true).await?;
        self.request(Method::DELETE, &role_path, None, true).await?;

        Ok(())
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\security_breaker.rs ==========
use std::collections::{HashMap, VecDeque};
use std::net::IpAddr;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct BreakerConfig {
    pub enabled: bool,
    pub failure_threshold: usize,
    pub observation_window: Duration,
    pub cooldown: Duration,
}

impl BreakerConfig {
    pub fn from_env() -> Self {
        let mode = std::env::var("PROXIMA_SECURITY_BREAKER")
            .or_else(|_| std::env::var("PROXIMA_SECURITY_CIRCUIT_BREAKER"))
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| "fail-closed".to_string());
        Self {
            enabled: !matches!(
                mode.trim().to_ascii_lowercase().as_str(),
                "off" | "disabled" | "false" | "0"
            ),
            failure_threshold: std::env::var("PROXIMA_BREAKER_FAILURE_THRESHOLD")
                .ok()
                .and_then(|v| v.parse().ok())
                .filter(|v| *v > 0)
                .unwrap_or(5),
            observation_window: Duration::from_secs(
                std::env::var("PROXIMA_BREAKER_WINDOW_SECONDS")
                    .ok()
                    .and_then(|v| v.parse().ok())
                    .filter(|v| *v > 0)
                    .unwrap_or(60),
            ),
            cooldown: Duration::from_secs(
                std::env::var("PROXIMA_BREAKER_COOLDOWN_SECONDS")
                    .ok()
                    .and_then(|v| v.parse().ok())
                    .filter(|v| *v > 0)
                    .unwrap_or(300),
            ),
        }
    }
}

const MAX_TRACKED_PEERS: usize = 10_000;

#[derive(Default)]
struct PeerState {
    violations: VecDeque<Instant>,
    blocked_until: Option<Instant>,
    last_seen: Option<Instant>,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct SecurityBreaker {
    config: BreakerConfig,
    peers: Arc<Mutex<HashMap<IpAddr, PeerState>>>,
}

impl SecurityBreaker {
    pub fn new(config: BreakerConfig) -> Self {
        Self {
            config,
            peers: Arc::new(Mutex::new(HashMap::new())),
        }
    }

    pub fn is_blocked(&self, peer: IpAddr) -> bool {
        if !self.config.enabled {
            return false;
        }
        let Ok(mut peers) = self.peers.lock() else {
            return true;
        };
        let Some(state) = peers.get_mut(&peer) else {
            return false;
        };
        state.last_seen = Some(Instant::now());
        match state.blocked_until {
            Some(until) if until > Instant::now() => true,
            Some(_) => {
                state.blocked_until = None;
                state.violations.clear();
                false
            }
            None => false,
        }
    }

    pub fn record_violation(&self, peer: IpAddr) -> bool {
        if !self.config.enabled {
            return false;
        }
        let Ok(mut peers) = self.peers.lock() else {
            return true;
        };
        let now = Instant::now();
        if !peers.contains_key(&peer) && peers.len() >= MAX_TRACKED_PEERS {
            peers.retain(|_, state| {
                state.blocked_until.is_some_and(|until| until > now)
                    || state
                        .violations
                        .back()
                        .is_some_and(|at| now.duration_since(*at) <= self.config.observation_window)
            });
            if peers.len() >= MAX_TRACKED_PEERS {
                if let Some(oldest) = peers
                    .iter()
                    .min_by_key(|(_, state)| state.last_seen.unwrap_or(now))
                    .map(|(ip, _)| *ip)
                {
                    peers.remove(&oldest);
                }
            }
        }
        let state = peers.entry(peer).or_default();
        state.last_seen = Some(now);
        while state
            .violations
            .front()
            .is_some_and(|at| now.duration_since(*at) > self.config.observation_window)
        {
            state.violations.pop_front();
        }
        state.violations.push_back(now);
        if state.violations.len() >= self.config.failure_threshold {
            state.blocked_until = Some(now + self.config.cooldown);
            state.violations.clear();
            true
        } else {
            false
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::net::Ipv4Addr;
    #[test]
    fn breaker_blocks_repeated_offenders_only_for_the_cooldown() {
        let breaker = SecurityBreaker::new(BreakerConfig {
            enabled: true,
            failure_threshold: 2,
            observation_window: Duration::from_secs(30),
            cooldown: Duration::from_secs(30),
        });
        let peer = IpAddr::V4(Ipv4Addr::LOCALHOST);
        assert!(!breaker.record_violation(peer));
        assert!(breaker.record_violation(peer));
        assert!(breaker.is_blocked(peer));
        assert!(!breaker.is_blocked(IpAddr::V4(Ipv4Addr::new(127, 0, 0, 2))));
    }

    #[test]
    fn disabled_breaker_never_blocks() {
        let breaker = SecurityBreaker::new(BreakerConfig {
            enabled: false,
            failure_threshold: 1,
            observation_window: Duration::from_secs(1),
            cooldown: Duration::from_secs(1),
        });
        let peer = IpAddr::V4(Ipv4Addr::LOCALHOST);
        assert!(!breaker.record_violation(peer));
        assert!(!breaker.is_blocked(peer));
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\session.rs ==========
use crate::protocol::{
    parse_backend_frame, parse_frontend_frame, parse_startup_packet, BackendMessage, StartupPacket,
    AUTHENTICATION_TAG, AUTH_OK, ERROR_RESPONSE_TAG, READY_FOR_QUERY_TAG,
};
use crate::tenant::{TenantContext, TenantTokenVerifier};
use std::io;
use tokio::io::{AsyncRead, AsyncReadExt, AsyncWrite, AsyncWriteExt};

pub trait PgStream: AsyncRead + AsyncWrite + Unpin + Send {}
impl<T> PgStream for T where T: AsyncRead + AsyncWrite + Unpin + Send {}
pub type BoxedPgStream = Box<dyn PgStream>;
use tracing::debug;

const MAX_STARTUP_PACKET: usize = 16 * 1024 * 1024;
const TENANT_TOKEN_PARAMETER: &str = "proxima_tenant_token";
const POSTGRES_IDENTIFIER_MAX_BYTES: usize = 63;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct EstablishedSession {
    pub tenant_context: Option<TenantContext>,
}

pub async fn establish(
    mut client: BoxedPgStream,
    mut upstream: BoxedPgStream,
    verifier: Option<&TenantTokenVerifier>,
    tenant_role_prefix: &str,
) -> io::Result<(BoxedPgStream, BoxedPgStream, EstablishedSession)> {
    let startup = read_startup(&mut client).await?;

    match startup {
        StartupPacket::Startup {
            protocol_version,
            parameters,
        } => {
            let (startup, tenant_context) =
                prepare_startup(protocol_version, parameters, verifier, tenant_role_prefix)?;
            debug!(
                tenant_bound = tenant_context.is_some(),
                "PostgreSQL startup packet received"
            );
            forward_startup(&mut upstream, &startup).await?;
            broker_startup_authentication(&mut client, &mut upstream).await?;
            Ok((client, upstream, EstablishedSession { tenant_context }))
        }
        StartupPacket::SslRequest => {
            upstream
                .write_all(&encode_startup(&StartupPacket::SslRequest)?)
                .await?;
            let mut response = [0u8; 1];
            upstream.read_exact(&mut response).await?;
            client.write_all(&response).await?;

            if response[0] == b'S' {
                if verifier.is_some() {
                    return Err(io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                        "Proxima tenant enforcement cannot run through end-to-end TLS without TLS termination",
                    ));
                }
                return Ok((
                    client,
                    upstream,
                    EstablishedSession {
                        tenant_context: None,
                    },
                ));
            }

            let startup = read_startup(&mut client).await?;
            match startup {
                StartupPacket::Startup {
                    protocol_version,
                    parameters,
                } => {
                    let (startup, tenant_context) = prepare_startup(
                        protocol_version,
                        parameters,
                        verifier,
                        tenant_role_prefix,
                    )?;
                    forward_startup(&mut upstream, &startup).await?;
                    broker_startup_authentication(&mut client, &mut upstream).await?;
                    Ok((client, upstream, EstablishedSession { tenant_context }))
                }
                other => Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                    format!("expected PostgreSQL startup after SSL rejection, got {other:?}"),
                )),
            }
        }
        StartupPacket::CancelRequest {
            process_id,
            secret_key,
        } => {
            forward_startup(
                &mut upstream,
                &StartupPacket::CancelRequest {
                    process_id,
                    secret_key,
                },
            )
            .await?;
            Ok((
                client,
                upstream,
                EstablishedSession {
                    tenant_context: None,
                },
            ))
        }
        StartupPacket::Unknown { .. } => Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "unsupported PostgreSQL startup packet",
        )),
    }
}

fn prepare_startup(
    protocol_version: i32,
    parameters: Vec<(String, String)>,
    verifier: Option<&TenantTokenVerifier>,
    tenant_role_prefix: &str,
) -> io::Result<(StartupPacket, Option<TenantContext>)> {
    let mut tenant_token = None;
    let mut forwarded = Vec::with_capacity(parameters.len());

    for (key, value) in parameters {
        if key == TENANT_TOKEN_PARAMETER {
            if tenant_token.replace(value).is_some() {
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                    "duplicate Proxima tenant token",
                ));
            }
        } else if key == "options" {
            let (clean_options, option_token) = extract_tenant_token_from_options(&value)?;
            if let Some(option_token) = option_token {
                if tenant_token.replace(option_token).is_some() {
                    return Err(io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                        "duplicate Proxima tenant token",
                    ));
                }
            }
            if !clean_options.is_empty() {
                forwarded.push((key, clean_options));
            }
        } else {
            forwarded.push((key, value));
        }
    }

    let tenant_context = match verifier {
        Some(verifier) => {
            let token = tenant_token.ok_or_else(|| {
                io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                    "Proxima tenant token is required",
                )
            })?;

            Some(verifier.verify_now(&token).map_err(|error| {
                io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                    format!("invalid Proxima tenant token: {error}"),
                )
            })?)
        }
        None => None,
    };

    if let Some(context) = tenant_context.as_ref() {
        let normalized_tenant_id = context.tenant_id.replace('-', "_");
        let role = format!("{tenant_role_prefix}{normalized_tenant_id}");
        if role.len() > POSTGRES_IDENTIFIER_MAX_BYTES {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                "tenant role name exceeds PostgreSQL identifier limit",
            ));
        }
        forwarded.retain(|(key, _)| key != "user");
        forwarded.push(("user".to_owned(), role));
    }

    Ok((
        StartupPacket::Startup {
            protocol_version,
            parameters: forwarded,
        },
        tenant_context,
    ))
}

fn extract_tenant_token_from_options(value: &str) -> io::Result<(String, Option<String>)> {
    let mut tokens = value.split_whitespace().peekable();
    let mut output = Vec::new();
    let mut tenant_token = None;

    while let Some(token) = tokens.next() {
        if token == "-c" {
            let assignment = tokens.next().ok_or_else(|| {
                io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidData, "options contains incomplete -c")
            })?;
            if let Some(token_value) = assignment.strip_prefix("proxima_tenant_token=") {
                if token_value.is_empty() {
                    return Err(io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                        "Proxima tenant token in options is empty",
                    ));
                }
                if tenant_token.replace(token_value.to_owned()).is_some() {
                    return Err(io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                        "duplicate Proxima tenant token",
                    ));
                }
                continue;
            }
            output.push("-c".to_owned());
            output.push(assignment.to_owned());
        } else if let Some(token_value) = token.strip_prefix("proxima_tenant_token=") {
            if token_value.is_empty() {
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                    "Proxima tenant token in options is empty",
                ));
            }
            if tenant_token.replace(token_value.to_owned()).is_some() {
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                    "duplicate Proxima tenant token",
                ));
            }
        } else {
            output.push(token.to_owned());
        }
    }

    Ok((output.join(" "), tenant_token))
}

async fn broker_startup_authentication(
    client: &mut dyn PgStream,
    upstream: &mut dyn PgStream,
) -> io::Result<()> {
    let mut authenticated = false;

    loop {
        let frame = read_backend_frame(upstream).await?;
        client.write_all(&frame).await?;

        let message = parse_backend_frame(&frame)
            .map_err(|error| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidData, error.to_string()))?
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::UnexpectedEof, "incomplete backend frame")
            })?
            .0;

        if message.tag == ERROR_RESPONSE_TAG {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                "PostgreSQL authentication/startup failed",
            ));
        }

        if message.tag == AUTHENTICATION_TAG {
            let auth_code = parse_authentication_code(&message)?;
            match auth_code {
                AUTH_OK => authenticated = true,
                3 | 5 | 7 | 8 | 9 | 10 | 11 => {
                    let response = read_frontend_frame(client).await?;
                    upstream.write_all(&response).await?;
                }
                12 => {}
                other => {
                    return Err(io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::Unsupported,
                        format!("unsupported PostgreSQL authentication method: {other}"),
                    ));
                }
            }
        }

        if message.tag == READY_FOR_QUERY_TAG {
            if !authenticated {
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                    "PostgreSQL session became ready without AuthenticationOk",
                ));
            }
            return Ok(());
        }
    }
}

fn parse_authentication_code(message: &BackendMessage) -> io::Result<i32> {
    if message.payload.len() < 4 {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "truncated PostgreSQL authentication message",
        ));
    }
    Ok(i32::from_be_bytes(message.payload[..4].try_into().unwrap()))
}

async fn read_backend_frame(stream: &mut dyn PgStream) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut header = [0u8; 5];
    stream.read_exact(&mut header).await?;
    let length = i32::from_be_bytes(header[1..5].try_into().unwrap());
    if !(4..=MAX_STARTUP_PACKET as i32).contains(&length) {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            format!("invalid PostgreSQL backend frame length: {length}"),
        ));
    }
    let mut frame = vec![0u8; 5 + length as usize - 4];
    frame[..5].copy_from_slice(&header);
    stream.read_exact(&mut frame[5..]).await?;
    Ok(frame)
}

async fn read_frontend_frame(stream: &mut dyn PgStream) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut header = [0u8; 5];
    stream.read_exact(&mut header).await?;
    let length = i32::from_be_bytes(header[1..5].try_into().unwrap());
    if !(4..=MAX_STARTUP_PACKET as i32).contains(&length) {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            format!("invalid PostgreSQL frontend frame length: {length}"),
        ));
    }
    let mut frame = vec![0u8; 5 + length as usize - 4];
    frame[..5].copy_from_slice(&header);
    stream.read_exact(&mut frame[5..]).await?;
    parse_frontend_frame(&frame)
        .map_err(|error| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidData, error.to_string()))?
        .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::UnexpectedEof, "incomplete frontend frame"))?;
    Ok(frame)
}

async fn read_startup(stream: &mut dyn PgStream) -> io::Result<StartupPacket> {
    let mut length_bytes = [0u8; 4];
    stream.read_exact(&mut length_bytes).await?;

    let length = u32::from_be_bytes(length_bytes) as usize;
    if !(8..=MAX_STARTUP_PACKET).contains(&length) {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            format!("invalid PostgreSQL startup length: {length}"),
        ));
    }

    let mut packet = vec![0u8; length];
    packet[..4].copy_from_slice(&length_bytes);
    stream.read_exact(&mut packet[4..]).await?;

    parse_startup_packet(&packet)
        .map_err(|error| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidData, error.to_string()))?
        .map(|(startup, _)| startup)
        .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::UnexpectedEof, "incomplete startup packet"))
}

async fn forward_startup(stream: &mut dyn PgStream, startup: &StartupPacket) -> io::Result<()> {
    stream.write_all(&encode_startup(startup)?).await
}

fn encode_startup(startup: &StartupPacket) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>> {
    match startup {
        StartupPacket::SslRequest => Ok(8i32
            .to_be_bytes()
            .into_iter()
            .chain(crate::protocol::SSL_REQUEST_CODE.to_be_bytes())
            .collect()),
        StartupPacket::CancelRequest {
            process_id,
