            secret_key,
        } => {
            let mut packet = Vec::with_capacity(16);
            packet.extend_from_slice(&16i32.to_be_bytes());
            packet.extend_from_slice(&crate::protocol::CANCEL_REQUEST_CODE.to_be_bytes());
            packet.extend_from_slice(&process_id.to_be_bytes());
            packet.extend_from_slice(&secret_key.to_be_bytes());
            Ok(packet)
        }
        StartupPacket::Startup {
            protocol_version,
            parameters,
        } => {
            let mut body = Vec::new();
            body.extend_from_slice(&protocol_version.to_be_bytes());

            for (key, value) in parameters {
                body.extend_from_slice(key.as_bytes());
                body.push(0);
                body.extend_from_slice(value.as_bytes());
                body.push(0);
            }
            body.push(0);

            let length = body.len() + 4;
            let length = i32::try_from(length).map_err(|_| {
                io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, "startup packet too large")
            })?;

            let mut packet = Vec::with_capacity(length as usize);
            packet.extend_from_slice(&length.to_be_bytes());
            packet.extend_from_slice(&body);
            Ok(packet)
        }
        StartupPacket::Unknown { .. } => Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "cannot encode unknown startup packet",
        )),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::tenant::TenantTokenVerifier;

    fn verifier() -> TenantTokenVerifier {
        TenantTokenVerifier::new(b"01234567890123456789012345678901").unwrap()
    }

    #[test]
    fn extracts_and_removes_tenant_token() {
        let verifier = verifier();
        let token = verifier.sign_for_test("tenant_a", u64::MAX);
        let (startup, context) = prepare_startup(
            crate::protocol::PROTOCOL_3_0,
            vec![
                ("user".into(), "proxima".into()),
                (TENANT_TOKEN_PARAMETER.into(), token),
            ],
            Some(&verifier),
            "proxima_tenant_",
        )
        .unwrap();

        assert_eq!(context.unwrap().tenant_id, "tenant_a");
        assert_eq!(
            startup,
            StartupPacket::Startup {
                protocol_version: crate::protocol::PROTOCOL_3_0,
                parameters: vec![("user".into(), "proxima_tenant_tenant_a".into())],
            }
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn normalizes_hyphens_in_uuid_tenant_role() {
        let verifier = verifier();
        let tenant_id = "caeaad20-744b-4f3b-9f5d-feb95c051141";
        let token = verifier.sign_for_test(tenant_id, u64::MAX);
        let (startup, _) = prepare_startup(
            crate::protocol::PROTOCOL_3_0,
            vec![
                ("user".into(), "proxima".into()),
                (TENANT_TOKEN_PARAMETER.into(), token),
            ],
            Some(&verifier),
            "proxima_tenant_",
        )
        .unwrap();

        assert_eq!(
            startup,
            StartupPacket::Startup {
                protocol_version: crate::protocol::PROTOCOL_3_0,
                parameters: vec![(
                    "user".into(),
                    "proxima_tenant_caeaad20_744b_4f3b_9f5d_feb95c051141".into()
                )],
            }
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_missing_token_when_enforcement_is_enabled() {
        let verifier = verifier();
        let error = prepare_startup(
            crate::protocol::PROTOCOL_3_0,
            vec![("user".into(), "proxima".into())],
            Some(&verifier),
            "proxima_tenant_",
        )
        .unwrap_err();

        assert_eq!(error.kind(), io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied);
    }

    #[test]
    fn extracts_token_from_libpq_options() {
        let verifier = verifier();
        let token = verifier.sign_for_test("tenant_a", u64::MAX);
        let (options, extracted) = extract_tenant_token_from_options(&format!(
            "-c proxima_tenant_token={token} -c statement_timeout=1000"
        ))
        .unwrap();

        assert_eq!(extracted, Some(token));
        assert_eq!(options, "-c statement_timeout=1000");
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_duplicate_option_tokens() {
        let verifier = verifier();
        let token = verifier.sign_for_test("tenant_a", u64::MAX);
        let error = extract_tenant_token_from_options(&format!(
            "-c proxima_tenant_token={token} proxima_tenant_token={token}"
        ))
        .unwrap_err();

        assert_eq!(error.kind(), io::ErrorKind::InvalidData);
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_role_names_over_postgres_limit() {
        let verifier = verifier();
        let token = verifier.sign_for_test("tenant_a", u64::MAX);
        let error = prepare_startup(
            crate::protocol::PROTOCOL_3_0,
            vec![(TENANT_TOKEN_PARAMETER.into(), token)],
            Some(&verifier),
            &"x".repeat(63),
        )
        .unwrap_err();

        assert_eq!(error.kind(), io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput);
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_invalid_tenant_signature() {
        let verifier = verifier();
        let mut token = verifier.sign_for_test("tenant_a", u64::MAX);
        let last = token.pop().unwrap();
        token.push(if last == 'a' { 'b' } else { 'a' });
        let error = prepare_startup(
            crate::protocol::PROTOCOL_3_0,
            vec![(TENANT_TOKEN_PARAMETER.into(), token)],
            Some(&verifier),
            "proxima_tenant_",
        )
        .unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(error.kind(), io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied);
    }

    #[test]
    fn tenant_switch_between_connections_maps_to_distinct_roles() {
        let verifier = verifier();
        for (tenant, role) in [
            ("tenant_a", "proxima_tenant_tenant_a"),
            ("tenant_b", "proxima_tenant_tenant_b"),
            ("tenant_a", "proxima_tenant_tenant_a"),
        ] {
            let token = verifier.sign_for_test(tenant, u64::MAX);
            let (startup, context) = prepare_startup(
                crate::protocol::PROTOCOL_3_0,
                vec![
                    ("user".into(), "proxima".into()),
                    (TENANT_TOKEN_PARAMETER.into(), token),
                ],
                Some(&verifier),
                "proxima_tenant_",
            )
            .unwrap();
            assert_eq!(context.unwrap().tenant_id, tenant);
            assert_eq!(
                startup,
                StartupPacket::Startup {
                    protocol_version: crate::protocol::PROTOCOL_3_0,
                    parameters: vec![("user".into(), role.into())],
                }
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_duplicate_tokens() {
        let verifier = verifier();
        let token = verifier.sign_for_test("tenant_a", u64::MAX);
        let error = prepare_startup(
            crate::protocol::PROTOCOL_3_0,
            vec![
                (TENANT_TOKEN_PARAMETER.into(), token.clone()),
                (TENANT_TOKEN_PARAMETER.into(), token),
            ],
            Some(&verifier),
            "proxima_tenant_",
        )
        .unwrap_err();

        assert_eq!(error.kind(), io::ErrorKind::InvalidData);
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\sqlite_tenant.rs ==========
use rusqlite::{Connection, OpenFlags};
use std::fs::{self, OpenOptions};
use std::io;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::time::Duration;

const DATABASE_FILE: &str = "tenant.sqlite";

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum SqliteTenantError {
    InvalidTenantId,
    Io(io::Error),
    Sqlite(rusqlite::Error),
    UnsafePath,
}
impl std::fmt::Display for SqliteTenantError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::InvalidTenantId => write!(f, "invalid SQLite tenant identifier"),
            Self::Io(e) => write!(f, "SQLite tenant storage I/O error: {e}"),
            Self::Sqlite(e) => write!(f, "SQLite error: {e}"),
            Self::UnsafePath => write!(
                f,
                "SQLite tenant storage path is not a safe regular directory or file"
            ),
        }
    }
}
impl std::error::Error for SqliteTenantError {}
impl From<io::Error> for SqliteTenantError {
    fn from(e: io::Error) -> Self {
        Self::Io(e)
    }
}
impl From<rusqlite::Error> for SqliteTenantError {
    fn from(e: rusqlite::Error) -> Self {
        Self::Sqlite(e)
    }
}

/// Trusted in-process SQLite connection permanently bound to one tenant file.
/// Never accept a filesystem path from an untrusted database client.
pub struct SqliteTenantDatabase {
    tenant_id: String,
    path: PathBuf,
    connection: Connection,
}

impl SqliteTenantDatabase {
    pub fn open(root: impl AsRef<Path>, tenant_id: &str) -> Result<Self, SqliteTenantError> {
        validate_tenant_id(tenant_id)?;
        fs::create_dir_all(root.as_ref())?;
        let root = fs::canonicalize(root.as_ref())?;
        let tenant_dir = root.join(tenant_id);
        match fs::create_dir(&tenant_dir) {
            Ok(()) => set_private_dir_permissions(&tenant_dir)?,
            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists => {}
            Err(e) => return Err(e.into()),
        }
        let metadata = fs::symlink_metadata(&tenant_dir)?;
        if metadata.file_type().is_symlink() || !metadata.is_dir() {
            return Err(SqliteTenantError::UnsafePath);
        }
        let tenant_dir = fs::canonicalize(&tenant_dir)?;
        set_private_dir_permissions(&tenant_dir)?;
        if !tenant_dir.starts_with(&root) {
            return Err(SqliteTenantError::UnsafePath);
        }

        let path = tenant_dir.join(DATABASE_FILE);
        match OpenOptions::new().write(true).create_new(true).open(&path) {
            Ok(file) => {
                drop(file);
                set_private_file_permissions(&path)?;
            }
            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists => {}
            Err(e) => return Err(e.into()),
        }
        let metadata = fs::symlink_metadata(&path)?;
        if metadata.file_type().is_symlink() || !metadata.is_file() {
            return Err(SqliteTenantError::UnsafePath);
        }
        set_private_file_permissions(&path)?;
        let canonical_path = fs::canonicalize(&path)?;
        if !canonical_path.starts_with(&tenant_dir) {
            return Err(SqliteTenantError::UnsafePath);
        }

        let flags = OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_READ_WRITE
            | OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_CREATE
            | OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_FULL_MUTEX
            | OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_NOFOLLOW;
        let connection = Connection::open_with_flags(&canonical_path, flags)?;
        connection.busy_timeout(Duration::from_secs(5))?;
        connection.execute_batch(
            "PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON; PRAGMA trusted_schema = OFF; PRAGMA secure_delete = ON; PRAGMA synchronous = FULL;"
        )?;
        let _: String = connection.query_row("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL", [], |row| row.get(0))?;
        Ok(Self {
            tenant_id: tenant_id.to_owned(),
            path: canonical_path,
            connection,
        })
    }
    pub fn tenant_id(&self) -> &str {
        &self.tenant_id
    }
    pub fn path(&self) -> &Path {
        &self.path
    }
    /// Expose only to trusted application code; never share across tenant identities.
    pub fn connection(&self) -> &Connection {
        &self.connection
    }
}

pub fn validate_tenant_id(id: &str) -> Result<(), SqliteTenantError> {
    if id.is_empty()
        || id.len() > 64
        || !id
            .bytes()
            .all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'-' || b == b'_')
    {
        return Err(SqliteTenantError::InvalidTenantId);
    }
    Ok(())
}
#[cfg(unix)]
fn set_private_dir_permissions(path: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    fs::set_permissions(path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o700))
}
#[cfg(not(unix))]
fn set_private_dir_permissions(_: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
    Ok(())
}
#[cfg(unix)]
fn set_private_file_permissions(path: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    fs::set_permissions(path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o600))
}
#[cfg(not(unix))]
fn set_private_file_permissions(_: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use tempfile::tempdir;
    #[test]
    fn tenant_files_are_physically_separate() {
        let root = tempdir().unwrap();
        let a = SqliteTenantDatabase::open(root.path(), "tenant-a").unwrap();
        let b = SqliteTenantDatabase::open(root.path(), "tenant-b").unwrap();
        a.connection()
            .execute_batch(
                "CREATE TABLE records(value TEXT); INSERT INTO records VALUES ('A-only');",
            )
            .unwrap();
        b.connection()
            .execute_batch(
                "CREATE TABLE records(value TEXT); INSERT INTO records VALUES ('B-only');",
            )
            .unwrap();
        let av: String = a
            .connection()
            .query_row("SELECT value FROM records", [], |r| r.get(0))
            .unwrap();
        let bv: String = b
            .connection()
            .query_row("SELECT value FROM records", [], |r| r.get(0))
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(av, "A-only");
        assert_eq!(bv, "B-only");
        assert_ne!(a.path(), b.path());
    }
    #[test]
    fn rejects_traversal_and_invalid_ids() {
        for id in ["", "../escape", "a/b", ".", "tenant with spaces"] {
            assert!(matches!(
                validate_tenant_id(id),
                Err(SqliteTenantError::InvalidTenantId)
            ));
        }
        assert!(validate_tenant_id(&"x".repeat(65)).is_err());
    }
    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[test]
    fn rejects_symlinked_tenant_directory() {
        use std::os::unix::fs::symlink;
        let root = tempdir().unwrap();
        let outside = tempdir().unwrap();
        symlink(outside.path(), root.path().join("tenant-a")).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(
            SqliteTenantDatabase::open(root.path(), "tenant-a"),
            Err(SqliteTenantError::UnsafePath)
        ));
    }
    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[test]
    fn storage_permissions_are_owner_only() {
        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
        let root = tempdir().unwrap();
        let db = SqliteTenantDatabase::open(root.path(), "tenant-a").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            fs::metadata(db.path().parent().unwrap())
                .unwrap()
                .permissions()
                .mode()
                & 0o777,
            0o700
        );
        assert_eq!(
            fs::metadata(db.path()).unwrap().permissions().mode() & 0o777,
            0o600
        );
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\tenant.rs ==========
use hmac::{Hmac, Mac};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use std::fmt;
use std::fs::{self, OpenOptions};
use std::io::Write;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

type HmacSha256 = Hmac<Sha256>;

const VERSION: &str = "v1";
const MAX_TENANT_ID_LENGTH: usize = 128;
const SIGNATURE_HEX_LENGTH: usize = 64;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct TenantContext {
    pub tenant_id: String,
    pub expires_at: u64,
    pub emergency: bool,
    pub emergency_nonce_hash: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum TenantTokenError {
    InvalidFormat,
    UnsupportedVersion,
    InvalidTenantId,
    InvalidExpiry,
    Expired,
    InvalidSignature,
    EmergencyTokenLifetimeInvalid,
    EmergencyTokenReplay,
    EmergencyReplayStoreUnavailable,
}

impl fmt::Display for TenantTokenError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::InvalidFormat => write!(f, "invalid Proxima tenant token format"),
            Self::UnsupportedVersion => write!(f, "unsupported Proxima tenant token version"),
            Self::InvalidTenantId => write!(f, "invalid tenant identifier"),
            Self::InvalidExpiry => write!(f, "invalid tenant token expiry"),
            Self::Expired => write!(f, "tenant token has expired"),
            Self::InvalidSignature => write!(f, "invalid tenant token signature"),
            Self::EmergencyTokenLifetimeInvalid => {
                write!(f, "emergency token lifetime must be at most 15 minutes")
            }
            Self::EmergencyTokenReplay => write!(f, "emergency token has already been used"),
            Self::EmergencyReplayStoreUnavailable => {
                write!(f, "emergency replay protection is unavailable")
            }
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for TenantTokenError {}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct TenantTokenVerifier {
    secret: Vec<u8>,
    previous_secret: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    replay_dir: PathBuf,
}

impl TenantTokenVerifier {
    pub fn new(secret: impl AsRef<[u8]>) -> Result<Self, TenantTokenError> {
        let secret = secret.as_ref();
        if secret.len() < 32 {
            return Err(TenantTokenError::InvalidSignature);
        }

        let replay_dir = std::env::var_os("PROXIMA_BREAK_GLASS_REPLAY_DIR")
            .map(PathBuf::from)
            .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("data/proxima-break-glass-replays"));
        Ok(Self {
            secret: secret.to_vec(),
            previous_secret: None,
            replay_dir,
        })
    }

    pub fn new_with_previous(
        secret: impl AsRef<[u8]>,
        previous_secret: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Result<Self, TenantTokenError> {
        let secret = secret.as_ref();
        if secret.len() < 32
            || previous_secret.is_some_and(|previous| previous.as_bytes().len() < 32)
        {
            return Err(TenantTokenError::InvalidSignature);
        }
        let replay_dir = std::env::var_os("PROXIMA_BREAK_GLASS_REPLAY_DIR")
            .map(PathBuf::from)
            .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("data/proxima-break-glass-replays"));
        Ok(Self {
            secret: secret.to_vec(),
            previous_secret: previous_secret.map(|previous| previous.as_bytes().to_vec()),
            replay_dir,
        })
    }

    pub fn with_replay_dir(
        secret: impl AsRef<[u8]>,
        replay_dir: PathBuf,
    ) -> Result<Self, TenantTokenError> {
        let secret = secret.as_ref();
        if secret.len() < 32 {
            return Err(TenantTokenError::InvalidSignature);
        }
        Ok(Self {
            secret: secret.to_vec(),
            previous_secret: None,
            replay_dir,
        })
    }

    pub fn verify(
        &self,
        token: &str,
        now_unix_seconds: u64,
    ) -> Result<TenantContext, TenantTokenError> {
        if token.starts_with("bg1.") {
            return self.verify_break_glass(token, now_unix_seconds);
        }
        let mut parts = token.split('.');
        let version = parts.next().ok_or(TenantTokenError::InvalidFormat)?;
        let tenant_id = parts.next().ok_or(TenantTokenError::InvalidFormat)?;
        let expiry = parts.next().ok_or(TenantTokenError::InvalidFormat)?;
        let signature = parts.next().ok_or(TenantTokenError::InvalidFormat)?;

        if parts.next().is_some() {
            return Err(TenantTokenError::InvalidFormat);
        }

        if version != VERSION {
            return Err(TenantTokenError::UnsupportedVersion);
        }

        validate_tenant_id(tenant_id)?;

        let expires_at = expiry
            .parse::<u64>()
            .map_err(|_| TenantTokenError::InvalidExpiry)?;

        if now_unix_seconds >= expires_at {
            return Err(TenantTokenError::Expired);
        }

        let expected = sign_payload(&self.secret, version, tenant_id, expires_at);
        let valid_current = constant_time_hex_eq(&expected, signature);
        let valid_previous = self.previous_secret.as_ref().is_some_and(|secret| {
            constant_time_hex_eq(
                &sign_payload(secret, version, tenant_id, expires_at),
                signature,
            )
        });
        if !valid_current && !valid_previous {
            return Err(TenantTokenError::InvalidSignature);
        }

        Ok(TenantContext {
            tenant_id: tenant_id.to_owned(),
            expires_at,
            emergency: false,
            emergency_nonce_hash: None,
        })
    }

    pub fn verify_now(&self, token: &str) -> Result<TenantContext, TenantTokenError> {
        let now = SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
            .map_err(|_| TenantTokenError::InvalidExpiry)?
            .as_secs();

        self.verify(token, now)
    }

    fn verify_break_glass(&self, token: &str, now: u64) -> Result<TenantContext, TenantTokenError> {
        let parts: Vec<&str> = token.split('.').collect();
        if parts.len() != 5 || parts[0] != "bg1" {
            return Err(TenantTokenError::InvalidFormat);
        }
        let tenant_id = parts[1];
        validate_tenant_id(tenant_id)?;
        let expires_at = parts[2]
            .parse::<u64>()
            .map_err(|_| TenantTokenError::InvalidExpiry)?;
        if expires_at <= now {
            return Err(TenantTokenError::Expired);
        }
        if expires_at.saturating_sub(now) > 900 {
            return Err(TenantTokenError::EmergencyTokenLifetimeInvalid);
        }
        let nonce = parts[3];
        if nonce.len() != 32 || !nonce.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
            return Err(TenantTokenError::InvalidFormat);
        }
        let payload = format!("bg1.{tenant_id}.{expires_at}.{nonce}");
        let valid_current = constant_time_hex_eq(&sign_message(&self.secret, &payload), parts[4]);
        let valid_previous = self
            .previous_secret
            .as_ref()
            .is_some_and(|secret| constant_time_hex_eq(&sign_message(secret, &payload), parts[4]));
        if !valid_current && !valid_previous {
            return Err(TenantTokenError::InvalidSignature);
        }
        fs::create_dir_all(&self.replay_dir)
            .map_err(|_| TenantTokenError::EmergencyReplayStoreUnavailable)?;
        let nonce_hash = format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(nonce.as_bytes()));
        let replay_path = self.replay_dir.join(format!("{nonce_hash}.used"));
        let mut file = match OpenOptions::new()
            .write(true)
            .create_new(true)
            .open(replay_path)
        {
            Ok(file) => file,
            Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists => {
                return Err(TenantTokenError::EmergencyTokenReplay)
            }
            Err(_) => return Err(TenantTokenError::EmergencyReplayStoreUnavailable),
        };
        file.write_all(format!("expires_at={expires_at}\n").as_bytes())
            .and_then(|_| file.sync_all())
            .map_err(|_| TenantTokenError::EmergencyReplayStoreUnavailable)?;
        Ok(TenantContext {
            tenant_id: tenant_id.to_owned(),
            expires_at,
            emergency: true,
            emergency_nonce_hash: Some(nonce_hash),
        })
    }

    #[cfg(test)]
    pub(crate) fn sign_for_test(&self, tenant_id: &str, expires_at: u64) -> String {
        validate_tenant_id(tenant_id).unwrap();
        format!(
            "{VERSION}.{tenant_id}.{expires_at}.{}",
            sign_payload(&self.secret, VERSION, tenant_id, expires_at)
        )
    }
}

pub fn issue_break_glass_token(
    secret: &[u8],
    tenant_id: &str,
    ttl_seconds: u64,
) -> Result<String, String> {
    if secret.len() < 32 {
        return Err("tenant signing secret must be at least 32 bytes".into());
    }
    if !(1..=900).contains(&ttl_seconds) {
        return Err("TTL must be between 1 and 900 seconds".into());
    }
    validate_tenant_id(tenant_id).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let now = SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .map_err(|_| "system clock before UNIX epoch".to_string())?
        .as_secs();
    let expires_at = now.saturating_add(ttl_seconds);
    let nonce = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().simple().to_string();
    let payload = format!("bg1.{tenant_id}.{expires_at}.{nonce}");
    Ok(format!("{payload}.{}", sign_message(secret, &payload)))
}

fn validate_tenant_id(tenant_id: &str) -> Result<(), TenantTokenError> {
    if tenant_id.is_empty()
        || tenant_id.len() > MAX_TENANT_ID_LENGTH
        || !tenant_id
            .bytes()
            .all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || byte == b'-' || byte == b'_')
    {
        return Err(TenantTokenError::InvalidTenantId);
    }

    Ok(())
}

fn sign_payload(secret: &[u8], version: &str, tenant_id: &str, expires_at: u64) -> String {
    let payload = format!("{version}.{tenant_id}.{expires_at}");
    sign_message(secret, &payload)
}

fn sign_message(secret: &[u8], payload: &str) -> String {
    let mut mac = HmacSha256::new_from_slice(secret).expect("HMAC accepts keys of any length");
    mac.update(payload.as_bytes());

    let mut output = String::with_capacity(SIGNATURE_HEX_LENGTH);
    for byte in mac.finalize().into_bytes() {
        output.push_str(&format!("{byte:02x}"));
    }
    output
}

fn constant_time_hex_eq(expected: &str, actual: &str) -> bool {
    if actual.len() != expected.len() {
        return false;
    }

    let mut difference = 0u8;
    for (left, right) in expected.bytes().zip(actual.bytes()) {
        difference |= left ^ right;
    }

    difference == 0
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn verifier() -> TenantTokenVerifier {
        TenantTokenVerifier::new(b"01234567890123456789012345678901").unwrap()
    }

    #[test]
    fn verifies_signed_tenant_context() {
        let verifier = verifier();
        let token = verifier.sign_for_test("tenant_a", 2_000);

        assert_eq!(
            verifier.verify(&token, 1_000).unwrap(),
            TenantContext {
                tenant_id: "tenant_a".into(),
                expires_at: 2_000,
                emergency: false,
                emergency_nonce_hash: None
            }
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_tampered_tenant_id() {
        let verifier = verifier();
        let token = verifier.sign_for_test("tenant_a", 2_000);
        let tampered = token.replacen("tenant_a", "tenant_b", 1);

        assert_eq!(
            verifier.verify(&tampered, 1_000),
            Err(TenantTokenError::InvalidSignature)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_expired_token() {
        let verifier = verifier();
        let token = verifier.sign_for_test("tenant_a", 2_000);

        assert_eq!(
            verifier.verify(&token, 2_000),
            Err(TenantTokenError::Expired)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn emergency_token_is_tenant_bound_short_lived_and_one_time() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let secret = b"01234567890123456789012345678901";
        let verifier =
            TenantTokenVerifier::with_replay_dir(secret, dir.path().to_path_buf()).unwrap();
        let token = issue_break_glass_token(secret, "tenant_a", 300).unwrap();
        let now = SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
            .unwrap()
            .as_secs();
        let context = verifier.verify(&token, now).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(context.tenant_id, "tenant_a");
        assert!(context.emergency);
        assert!(context.emergency_nonce_hash.is_some());
        assert_eq!(
            verifier.verify(&token, now),
            Err(TenantTokenError::EmergencyTokenReplay)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn emergency_token_lifetime_is_bounded() {
        let secret = b"01234567890123456789012345678901";
        assert!(issue_break_glass_token(secret, "tenant_a", 901).is_err());
        assert!(issue_break_glass_token(secret, "tenant_a", 0).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_weak_signing_secret() {
        assert!(matches!(
            TenantTokenVerifier::new("too-short"),
            Err(TenantTokenError::InvalidSignature)
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_invalid_tenant_identifier() {
        let verifier = verifier();

        assert_eq!(
            verifier.verify("v1.bad!.2000.00", 0),
            Err(TenantTokenError::InvalidTenantId)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_extra_token_parts() {
        let verifier = verifier();
        assert_eq!(
            verifier.verify("v1.tenant_a.2000.00.extra", 1000),
            Err(TenantTokenError::InvalidFormat)
        );
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod previous_key_tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn accepts_existing_tokens_with_previous_key_during_rotation() {
        let previous = "legacy-global-tenant-signing-key-0001";
        let current = "organization-scoped-tenant-signing-key-01";
        let verifier = TenantTokenVerifier::new_with_previous(current, Some(previous)).unwrap();
        let token = format!(
            "v1.tenant_a.2000.{}",
            sign_payload(previous.as_bytes(), "v1", "tenant_a", 2000)
        );
        assert_eq!(verifier.verify(&token, 1000).unwrap().tenant_id, "tenant_a");
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_previous_key_when_not_configured() {
        let previous = "legacy-global-tenant-signing-key-0001";
        let current = "organization-scoped-tenant-signing-key-01";
        let verifier = TenantTokenVerifier::new(current).unwrap();
        let token = format!(
            "v1.tenant_a.2000.{}",
            sign_payload(previous.as_bytes(), "v1", "tenant_a", 2000)
        );
        assert_eq!(
            verifier.verify(&token, 1000),
            Err(TenantTokenError::InvalidSignature)
        );
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\tls.rs ==========
use crate::config::{Config, UpstreamTlsMode};
use rustls::crypto::ring;
use rustls::pki_types::{CertificateDer, PrivateKeyDer, ServerName};
use rustls::{ClientConfig, RootCertStore, ServerConfig};
use std::fs::File;
use std::io::{self, BufReader};
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::io::{AsyncReadExt, AsyncWriteExt};
use tokio::net::TcpStream;
use tokio_rustls::{
    client::TlsStream as ClientTlsStream, server::TlsStream as ServerTlsStream, TlsAcceptor,
    TlsConnector,
};

pub fn client_acceptor(config: &Config) -> io::Result<Option<TlsAcceptor>> {
    // reqwest and rustls can enable multiple crypto providers through Cargo feature unification.
    // Install an explicit provider before any ServerConfig/ClientConfig builder is created.
    let _ = ring::default_provider().install_default();
    let (Some(cert_path), Some(key_path)) = (&config.tls_cert_file, &config.tls_key_file) else {
        return Ok(None);
    };

    let certs = load_certs(cert_path)?;
    let key = load_private_key(key_path)?;
    let server = ServerConfig::builder()
        .with_no_client_auth()
        .with_single_cert(certs, key)
        .map_err(|error| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                format!("invalid TLS certificate/key: {error}"),
            )
        })?;

    Ok(Some(TlsAcceptor::from(Arc::new(server))))
}

pub fn upstream_connector(config: &Config) -> io::Result<Option<TlsConnector>> {
    let _ = ring::default_provider().install_default();
    if config.upstream_tls_mode == UpstreamTlsMode::Disable {
        return Ok(None);
    }

    let ca_path = config.upstream_tls_ca_file.as_ref().ok_or_else(|| {
        io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
            "upstream TLS CA file is required",
        )
    })?;

    let mut roots = RootCertStore::empty();
    for cert in load_certs(ca_path)? {
        roots.add(cert).map_err(|error| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
                format!("invalid upstream CA certificate: {error}"),
            )
        })?;
    }

    let client = ClientConfig::builder()
        .with_root_certificates(roots)
        .with_no_client_auth();

    Ok(Some(TlsConnector::from(Arc::new(client))))
}

pub async fn accept_client_tls(
    acceptor: &TlsAcceptor,
    stream: TcpStream,
    timeout_duration: std::time::Duration,
) -> io::Result<ServerTlsStream<TcpStream>> {
    tokio::time::timeout(timeout_duration, acceptor.accept(stream))
        .await
        .map_err(|_| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::TimedOut, "client TLS handshake timed out"))?
        .map_err(|error| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                format!("client TLS handshake failed: {error}"),
            )
        })
}

pub async fn connect_upstream_tls(
    connector: &TlsConnector,
    mut stream: TcpStream,
    server_name: &str,
    timeout_duration: std::time::Duration,
) -> io::Result<ClientTlsStream<TcpStream>> {
    stream.write_all(&8i32.to_be_bytes()).await?;
    stream
        .write_all(&crate::protocol::SSL_REQUEST_CODE.to_be_bytes())
        .await?;

    let mut response = [0u8; 1];
    stream.read_exact(&mut response).await?;
    if response[0] != b'S' {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
            "PostgreSQL upstream rejected TLS",
        ));
    }

    let name = ServerName::try_from(server_name.to_owned()).map_err(|_| {
        io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
            "invalid upstream TLS server name",
        )
    })?;

    tokio::time::timeout(timeout_duration, connector.connect(name, stream))
        .await
        .map_err(|_| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::TimedOut, "upstream TLS handshake timed out"))?
        .map_err(|error| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                format!("upstream TLS handshake failed: {error}"),
            )
        })
}

fn load_certs(path: &str) -> io::Result<Vec<CertificateDer<'static>>> {
    let file = File::open(path)?;
    let mut reader = BufReader::new(file);
    rustls_pemfile::certs(&mut reader)
        .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
        .map_err(|error| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                format!("failed to parse certificate PEM: {error}"),
            )
        })
}

fn load_private_key(path: &str) -> io::Result<PrivateKeyDer<'static>> {
    let file = File::open(path)?;
    let mut reader = BufReader::new(file);
    rustls_pemfile::private_key(&mut reader)
        .map_err(|error| {
            io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                format!("failed to parse private key PEM: {error}"),
            )
        })?
        .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidData, "no private key found in PEM"))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::io::Write;
    use tempfile::NamedTempFile;

    #[test]
    fn invalid_cert_file_is_rejected() {
        let mut file = NamedTempFile::new().unwrap();
        writeln!(file, "not a certificate").unwrap();
        let mut config = test_config();
        config.tls_cert_file = Some(file.path().to_string_lossy().into_owned());
        config.tls_key_file = Some(file.path().to_string_lossy().into_owned());
        assert!(client_acceptor(&config).is_err());
    }

    fn test_config() -> Config {
        Config {
            listen_addr: "127.0.0.1:6432".parse().unwrap(),
            upstream_addr: "127.0.0.1:5432".into(),
            tenant_signing_key: None,
            tenant_signing_key_previous: None,
            tenant_role_prefix: "proxima_tenant_".into(),

            tls_cert_file: None,
            tls_key_file: None,
            tls_handshake_timeout: std::time::Duration::from_secs(1),
            require_client_tls: false,
            upstream_tls_mode: UpstreamTlsMode::Disable,
            upstream_tls_ca_file: None,
            upstream_tls_server_name: None,
            upstream_tls_handshake_timeout: std::time::Duration::from_secs(1),
            dashboard_enabled: false,
            dashboard_listen_addr: "127.0.0.1:9080".parse().unwrap(),
        }
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\bin\proxima-attest-verify.rs ==========
use proxima_engine::attestation::{AttestationConfig, AttestationLedger};
use std::process::ExitCode;

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> ExitCode {
    let config = AttestationConfig::from_env();
    let path = config.ledger_path.clone();
    let ledger = AttestationLedger::new(config);
    match ledger.verify_chain().await {
        Ok(result) if result.valid && result.records > 0 => {
            println!(
                "VALID: {} attestation record(s) verified in {}",
                result.records,
                path.display()
            );
            ExitCode::SUCCESS
        }
        Ok(result) if result.valid => {
            eprintln!(
                "NO RECORDS: there is no signed attestation evidence to verify in {}",
                path.display()
            );
            ExitCode::from(2)
        }
        Ok(result) => {
            eprintln!(
                "INVALID: {} record(s) verified before failure in {}: {}",
                result.records,
                path.display(),
                result
                    .error
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| "unknown chain failure".into())
            );
            ExitCode::from(2)
        }
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("ERROR: could not verify {}: {error}", path.display());
            ExitCode::from(1)
        }
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\bin\proxima-break-glass-token.rs ==========
use proxima_engine::tenant::issue_break_glass_token;
use std::env;
fn main() {
    let mut args = env::args().skip(1);
    let tenant = args.next().unwrap_or_default();
    let ttl = args
        .next()
        .and_then(|v| v.parse::<u64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(300);
    if tenant.is_empty() || args.next().is_some() {
        eprintln!("Usage: cargo run -p proxima-engine --bin proxima-break-glass-token -- <tenant_id> [ttl_seconds]");
        std::process::exit(2);
    }
    let secret = match env::var("PROXIMA_TENANT_SIGNING_KEY") {
        Ok(v) if v.len() >= 32 => v,
        _ => {
            eprintln!("PROXIMA_TENANT_SIGNING_KEY must contain at least 32 bytes.");
            std::process::exit(2)
        }
    };
    match issue_break_glass_token(secret.as_bytes(), &tenant, ttl) {
        Ok(token) => println!("{token}"),
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("Could not issue emergency token: {error}");
            std::process::exit(2)
        }
    }
}

========== crates\proxima-engine\src\bin\proxima-mysql-gateway.rs ==========
use proxima_engine::mysql_gateway::run_mysql_gateway;
use proxima_engine::mysql_identity::MysqlIdentityRegistry;

use std::env;
use std::net::SocketAddr;
use std::sync::Arc;

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    tracing_subscriber::fmt()
        .with_env_filter(tracing_subscriber::EnvFilter::from_default_env())
        .init();

    let registry_path = env::var("PROXIMA_MYSQL_IDENTITY_REGISTRY")
        .map_err(|_| "PROXIMA_MYSQL_IDENTITY_REGISTRY is required")?;

    let address: SocketAddr = env::var("PROXIMA_MYSQL_LISTEN_ADDR")
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| "127.0.0.1:3307".to_string())
        .parse()?;

    let registry = Arc::new(MysqlIdentityRegistry::load(registry_path)?);

    tracing::info!(
        principals = registry.principal_count(),
        "MySQL identity registry loaded"
    );

    run_mysql_gateway(address, registry).await?;

    Ok(())
}
